# 《说春秋》之一《齐楚崛起》前十章中英对照

说明：
- 本文件按源文本中的 `第3节` 到 `第32节` 整理，对应第一章“天仙妹妹”、第二章“后宫风暴”、第三章“琼瑶的故事”、第四章“烽火戏诸侯”、第五章“春秋开始了”、第六章“亲娘靠不住”、第七章“黄泉相见”、第八章“亲爹也靠不住”、第九章“荒淫无度”和第十章“人鹤情未了”。
- 排版规则为“一句中文，一句英文”；遇到较长句子时，补充语法说明和常用短语。
- 英文以自然流畅为主，较难词汇在首次出现处标注中文义。

## 第一章 天仙妹妹
## Chapter 1: The Fairy Maiden

歌里唱的是：爱江山，更爱美人。  
A song says, "Love the realm, but love beauty even more."  
这不是真理。  
That is not the truth.  
真理是什么？  
Then what is?  
真理是：人往往更爱自己缺的东西。  
The truth is this: people usually love most what they lack.  
有江山的更爱美人，有美人的更爱江山。  
Men with kingdoms crave beauty, and men with beauty crave kingdoms.  
俗话说，每个成功男人背后，都站着一个女人。  
There is a saying that behind every successful man stands a woman.  
事实却是，每个丢了江山的男人背后，也往往站着一个女人。  
The reality, though, is that behind every man who loses a kingdom, there is often a woman too.  
女人会改变历史吗？  
Do women change history?  
不，她们只是常常让历史改了方向。  
No. They simply often make history turn in a different direction.  

### 天仙妹妹
### The Fairy Maiden

时间回到公元前780年。  
Let us go back to 780 BCE.  
那时是周朝，中国历史上的第三个王朝。  
It was the Zhou dynasty（周朝）, the third dynasty in Chinese history.  
一个古老的爱情故事，就从汉中平原开始。  
An old love story began on the Hanzhong Plain.  
男主角叫褒洪德，二十四岁。  
The male lead was named Bao Hongde, and he was twenty-four.  
他是褒城大夫褒顼的独生子。  
He was the only son of Bao Xu, the chief official of Baocheng.  
褒城在今天陕西勉县一带，位于汉中平原西端。  
Baocheng lay in what is now Mian County, Shaanxi, at the western edge of the Hanzhong Plain.  
褒城大夫，差不多就相当于县长。  
Its ruling official was roughly the equivalent of a county magistrate（县长）.  
这一天，褒洪德闲得发慌，便出城闲逛。  
That day Bao Hongde had nothing better to do, so he wandered out of the city.  
到了河边，他遇见了本书的第一个女主角。  
By the river, he met the first heroine of this book.  
她是什么模样？  
What did she look like?  
那是个美人，看着也就十五六岁。  
She was a beauty, seemingly no more than fifteen or sixteen.  
头上包着花头巾，还插着一朵野花。  
She wore a flowered kerchief and had tucked a wildflower into it.  
身上是一件麻布缝的花衣裳，稍微有点宽。  
She had on a flower-patterned dress sewn from coarse linen, a little loose on her frame.  
背后背着个大箩筐，里面装满了蘑菇。  
On her back she carried a large basket filled with mushrooms.  
她看上去极干净，极漂亮，还有点泼辣劲儿。  
She looked pure, lovely, and just a little bit spirited（泼辣的）.  
闭上眼想一想，大概就是十五岁时的宋祖英。  
Close your eyes and imagine Song Zuying at fifteen.  
《诗经》里说：“窈窕淑女，君子好逑。”  
As *The Book of Songs* puts it, "A graceful maiden is a gentleman's perfect match."  
这样的姑娘，褒洪德哪里还忍得住。  
A girl like that was enough to make Bao Hongde lose all restraint.  
谁见了，都会想追。  
Anyone who saw her would have wanted to court her.  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`表语从句`、`what 引导宾语从句`、`并列句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `The truth is this` 和 `people usually love most what they lack`。<br>
难点拆解：`The truth is this` 后面接解释内容，起到表语从句的作用；`what they lack` 是名词性从句，表示“他们所缺少的东西”；后一句用并列结构把“有江山”和“有美人”并排写出来，读起来更利落。<br>
常用短语：
- `love most what one lacks`：最爱自己缺少的东西
- `stand behind someone`：站在某人背后，支持某人
- `turn in a different direction`：转向另一个方向
- `lose all restraint`：一下子忍不住了

姑娘听见有人唱情歌，抬头一看，见是个年轻英俊的贵族公子在冲自己抛眼神。  
Hearing someone singing a love song, the girl looked up and saw a young nobleman casting flirtatious glances her way.  
她一下子红了脸，又轻轻送来一缕秋波。  
Her cheeks flushed at once, and she sent back a soft sidelong look.  
就是这一笑，把褒洪德的魂都勾走了。  
That single smile stole Bao Hongde's soul away.  
也是这一笑，让历史从此改道。  
That same smile would also send history in a new direction.  
在周朝，自由恋爱并不稀奇。  
In Zhou times, free courtship was not unusual.  
《诗经》早就替他们作了证。  
*The Book of Songs* had already testified to that.  
两个人就这样一见钟情。  
That was how the two of them fell in love at first sight.  
接着，他们开始对唱情歌。  
Then they began trading love songs.  
具体唱了什么，今天已经无从考证。  
No one can say for sure anymore what exactly they sang.  
无非也就是周朝版的“妹妹你大胆地往前走”一类。  
Most likely it was just some Zhou-dynasty version of "Girl, go boldly forward."  
也不知是唱得太好，还是唱得太糟。  
Whether the singing was wonderfully good or terribly bad, no one knows.  
忽然，一只癞蛤蟆从草丛里蹦出来，扑通一声跳进水里。  
All at once a toad sprang from the grass and plopped into the river.  
接着是第二只、第三只、第四只。  
Then came a second, a third, and a fourth.  
后来干脆数不清了。  
Before long there were too many to count.  
成千上万只癞蛤蟆往水里跳。  
Thousands upon thousands of toads were leaping into the water.  
这算什么预兆？  
What sort of omen（预兆） was that supposed to be?  
难道是癞蛤蟆也想吃天鹅肉？  
Were the toads dreaming of swan meat too?  
还是它们只是想洗个澡？  
Or did they simply want a bath?  
总之，两个人很快就聊熟了。  
In any case, the two of them grew familiar with each other in no time.  

姑娘其实只有十四岁。  
The girl was actually only fourteen.  
她只是看着稍微成熟一点。  
She only looked a little more mature than her age.  
她家住在附近山里。  
Her family lived in the nearby mountains.  
父亲卖桑木弓，所以叫桑叔。  
Her father sold mulberry-wood bows, so people called him Sang Shu.  
母亲编簸箕，不过当然不姓簸箕。  
Her mother wove baskets, though of course her surname was not Basket.  
她说：“我爹娘叫我天仙妹妹，因为他们是在河边捡到我的。”  
She said, "My parents call me the Fairy Maiden because they found me by the river."  
“他们说我是老天爷送来的礼物，是个天仙。”  
"They say I was a gift sent by Heaven, a fairy girl."  
原来她就是传说里的天仙妹妹。  
So she really was the legendary Fairy Maiden.  
怪不得这样迷人。  
No wonder she was so enchanting（迷人的）.  
褒洪德也赶紧自报家门。  
Bao Hongde hurried to introduce himself as well.  
他说：“我叫褒洪德，家里人都叫我褒子。”  
He said, "My name is Bao Hongde, but everyone at home calls me Baozi."  
姑娘笑着说：“那我就叫你褒子哥哥。”  
With a laugh, the girl said, "Then I'll call you Brother Baozi."  
两个人越说越近。  
The more they talked, the closer they grew.  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`原因状语从句`、`宾语从句`、`直接引语`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `My parents call me the Fairy Maiden`，后面接 `because ...` 说明原因。<br>
难点拆解：`because they found me by the river` 是原因状语从句，解释名字的来历；`They say I was ...` 里的 `I was a gift sent by Heaven` 是宾语从句；英文对话里保留直接引语，人物口气会更自然。<br>
常用短语：
- `call someone ...`：称某人为……
- `find someone by the river`：在河边捡到某人
- `a gift sent by Heaven`：老天送来的礼物
- `introduce oneself`：自我介绍

等到太阳快落山时，两个人已经私下定了终身。  
By the time the sun was about to set, the two had already pledged themselves to each other in private.  
一出中国古代版的灰姑娘，就这么上演了。  
A Chinese antiquity version of Cinderella was already unfolding.  
两人抱在一起时，大地忽然颤动，河水也像沸腾了一样。  
As they embraced, the earth suddenly trembled, and the river seemed to boil.  
难道爱情刚开始，就已经惊天动地了吗？  
Could love really shake heaven and earth the moment it began?  
未免也来得太猛了一点。  
That would have been a little too dramatic.  
“不好了，地震了！”  
"This is bad. It's an earthquake!"  
两个人顿时慌了神，各自往家里狂奔。  
The two of them panicked at once and bolted for home in different directions.  
地上掉了一路蘑菇。  
Mushrooms were left scattered all over the ground.  
现在我们知道，那些癞蛤蟆既不是来吃天鹅肉，也不是来洗澡。  
Now we know those toads had come neither for swan meat nor for a bath.  
它们是来预告地震的。  
They had come to warn of the quake.  
自古以来，癞蛤蟆似乎就有预测地震的本事。  
Since ancient times, toads have apparently had a talent for predicting earthquakes.  

### 岐山地震
### The Qishan Earthquake

这场地震有里氏七点九级。  
The earthquake measured 7.9 on the Richter scale.  
震中在岐山，所以史称“岐山地震”。  
Its epicenter（震中） lay in Qishan, so history remembers it as the Qishan Earthquake.  
岐山在哪里？  
Where is Qishan?  
就在今天陕西宝鸡岐山县一带。  
It is in what is now Qishan County, Baoji, Shaanxi.  
这场地震造成了什么后果？  
What did the quake bring about?  
《史记》记载：“幽王二年，西州三川皆震。是岁也，三川竭，岐山崩。”  
The *Records of the Grand Historian* says, "In the second year of King You, the three rivers of the western region were shaken. That year the rivers ran dry, and Mount Qishan collapsed."  
所谓三川，就是泾水、黄河和洛水。  
The "three rivers" meant the Jing, the Yellow River, and the Luo.  
当时究竟惨到什么地步？  
How terrible was it?  
不用细描，想想汶川地震就差不多了。  
There is no need for lengthy description; think of the Wenchuan earthquake and you will be close enough.  
岐山和汶川，本就在同一条地质断裂带上。  
Qishan and Wenchuan lie on the same geological fault zone（断裂带）.  
至于三川为什么断流，说白了就是堰塞湖。  
As for why the rivers stopped flowing, the plain answer is landslide dams.  
整个周朝都被吓坏了。  
The whole Zhou realm was terrified.  
因为岐山是周朝发家的福地。  
That was because Qishan was the blessed cradle of the Zhou house.  
用后来的说法，它就是周朝的龙脉。  
In later language, it was the Zhou dynasty's dragon vein（龙脉）.  
龙脉都崩了，谁能不怕？  
If the dragon vein collapsed, who would not be afraid?  
太史伯阳父第一时间就开始唱衰周朝。  
The Grand Historian Boyangfu began predicting doom for the Zhou almost at once.  
太史掌管史籍，地位极高。  
The Grand Historian oversaw the records of the state and held immense status.  
大致相当于今天国家级学术机构的掌门人。  
He was roughly the equivalent of the head of a national scholarly academy.  
他说：“国家要完了，百姓靠土地和水活着，没了水，还怎么活？”  
He said, "The state is finished. The people live by land and water. If the water is gone, how are they to live?"  
他说话一点也不顾社会影响。  
He spoke with very little concern for public morale.  
他还搬出旧例来证明。  
He even brought up earlier precedents（先例） as proof.  
他说，伊水洛水断流，夏朝就完了。  
He said that when the Yi and Luo ran dry, the Xia collapsed.  
黄河断流，商朝也完了。  
When the Yellow River failed, the Shang collapsed as well.  
如今轮到周朝，恐怕也一样。  
Now that it was Zhou's turn, he feared the outcome would be no different.  
他又断言：“国家必须依靠山川，山崩川竭，就是亡国的征兆。”  
He further declared, "A state must depend on its mountains and rivers. When the mountains collapse and the rivers fail, the omen is ruin."  
“如果要亡国，也不会超过十年。”  
"If the state is to fall, it will happen within ten years."  
他连时间都替周朝算好了。  
He even calculated the timetable for Zhou's destruction.  
后来果然应验了吗？  
Was his prophecy（预言） fulfilled in the end?  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`条件状语从句`、`宾语从句`、`引用结构`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `He declared`，后面接引号内容说明他的判断。<br>
难点拆解：`If the state is to fall` 是条件状语从句，表示“如果国家真要灭亡”；引号中的完整判断是 `declared` 的内容，相当于宾语从句；英文在处理史书引语时，常先用 `He declared` 或 `He said` 引出，再接原话。<br>
常用短语：
- `bring about`：带来，造成
- `lie on the same fault zone`：位于同一条断裂带上
- `public morale`：社会情绪，民心
- `be fulfilled in the end`：最后应验

周朝都城镐京，就是今天的西安。  
The Zhou capital, Haojing, stood where Xi'an is today.  
它和岐山的距离，大致相当于成都到汶川。  
Its distance from Qishan was roughly the same as Chengdu's distance from Wenchuan.  
镐京震感很强，但破坏还不算大。  
Haojing felt the quake strongly, though the actual damage there was limited.  
周太师周公和太保召公立刻组织军民赶往岐山救灾。  
Duke Zhou, the Grand Preceptor, and Duke Shao, the Grand Protector, immediately organized troops and civilians to go to Qishan for relief work.  
半个月后，救灾结束了。  
Half a month later, the rescue effort came to an end.  
周公和召公回到都城，怒气冲冲去见周幽王。  
Duke Zhou and Duke Shao returned to the capital and went to see King You of Zhou in a fury.  
周幽王是谁？  
Who was King You of Zhou?  
他就是周朝天子。  
He was the Son of Heaven of the Zhou.  
那时还没有“皇帝”这个叫法，只叫“王”。  
In those days there was no title of "emperor"; rulers were simply called kings.  
“幽王”这个名字听着就不妙。  
The posthumous title "King You" already sounds ominous（不祥的）.  
因为他的事迹太糟，后人才这么叫他。  
Posterity called him that because his deeds were so disgraceful（恶劣的）.  
要记住，古代帝王的称号都是死后才定的。  
Remember this: ancient rulers received such titles only after death.  
所以幽王活着的时候，并不知道自己以后会被叫作幽王。  
So while he was alive, he had no idea that history would later call him King You.  
影视剧里要是有人当面叫他“周幽王”，那多半是编剧没搞清楚。  
If someone in a drama calls him "King You of Zhou" to his face, that usually means the screenwriter has not done the history homework.  
周公和召公为什么这样生气？  
Why were Duke Zhou and Duke Shao so angry?  
因为他们是来骂他的。  
Because they had come to scold him.  
周公开口就说：“大王，你不务正业，只知道劳民伤财，大兴土木，惹怒了上天。”  
Duke Zhou opened with, "Your Majesty, you neglect proper duties, exhaust the people, keep launching huge building projects, and have angered Heaven."  
“所以老天才用地震来惩罚我们。”  
"That is why Heaven has punished us with an earthquake."  
周公一点也没客气。  
Duke Zhou was not the least bit polite.  
幽王却不吃这一套。  
King You was not buying any of it.  
他说：“修宫室，正说明国家有钱。”  
He said, "Rebuilding palaces only shows that the state is prosperous."  
“再说了，要不是重修了旧宫，这一震说不定早塌了。”  
"Besides, if we had not renovated the old halls, they might well have collapsed this time."  
召公也劝他说，先把选美女的事放一放，多关心百姓。  
Duke Shao also urged him to set aside his beauty search for a while and pay more attention to the suffering of the people.  
幽王更不服气了。  
The king was even less convinced.  
他说：“地震是天灾，关我什么事？”  
He said, "An earthquake is a natural disaster. What does that have to do with me?"  
他登基以后，就开始在全国范围选美女。  
From the day he took the throne, he had been holding a nationwide search for beauties.  
简直像古代版的选秀节目。  
It was practically an ancient talent show.  
可选了一年多，还是没找到真正中意的。  
Yet after more than a year, he still had not found anyone he truly liked.  
所以他本来就烦得很。  
So he was already annoyed to begin with.  
从这个角度看，他倒真像是选秀的祖师爷。  
From that angle, he really did look like the founding father of beauty contests.  
他哪里想得到，几千年后满中国还在搞这一套。  
He could never have guessed that thousands of years later the whole country would still be doing the same thing.  
那他为什么还肯忍着周公召公？  
Then why did he still tolerate Duke Zhou and Duke Shao?  
因为这两人威望高、势力大，又都是他的叔叔辈。  
Because the two men were prestigious（有威望的）, powerful, and both his elders by family rank.  
所以，他再不爱听，也只能听着。  
So however much he disliked their words, he had to sit there and take them.  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`原因状语从句`、`让步状语从句`、`并列句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `he had to sit there and take them`，前面用 `Because ...` 和 `however much ...` 补充原因与让步。<br>
难点拆解：`Because the two men were ...` 交代原因；`however much he disliked their words` 是让步状语从句，表示“再不爱听也得听”；最后用并列动词 `sit there and take them` 写出无奈接受的状态。<br>
常用短语：
- `do one's history homework`：把历史功课做足
- `not buy any of it`：一点也不信，不吃这一套
- `to begin with`：本来，一开始
- `sit there and take it`：只能忍着受着

周公召公一走，幽王满肚子闷气正没处撒。  
The moment the two dukes left, King You was left with a belly full of frustration and nowhere to vent it.  
这时，太宰虢石父来了。  
At that moment, Guo Shifu, the Grand Steward, arrived.  
太宰差不多就是总理。  
The title was roughly equivalent to prime minister.  
幽王瞪着他说：“老虢，你也是来烦我的？”  
The king glared at him and said, "Old Guo, have you come to pester（烦） me too?"  
虢石父笑着说：“大王，我是来给您道喜的。”  
Guo Shifu smiled and said, "Your Majesty, I have come to congratulate you."  
幽王问：“喜从何来？”  
The king asked, "And what is there to celebrate?"  
虢石父立刻回答：“当然是大王领导抗震救灾，大获全胜。”  
Guo Shifu instantly replied, "Why, Your Majesty led the earthquake relief effort to a complete victory."  
幽王一听，脸色果然好看了些。  
At that, the king's expression immediately softened.  
虢石父见他心情转好，越发来劲。  
Seeing the king's mood improve, Guo Shifu became even more energetic.  
他说这次救灾又快又全，出了大力，救下无数灾民。  
He said the relief effort had been swift and thorough, that every effort had been made, and that countless victims had been saved.  
如今百姓个个感恩戴德，连歌都编出来了。  
Now, he claimed, the people were overflowing with gratitude and had even composed songs of praise.  
幽王一听，立刻来了兴趣。  
That instantly caught the king's interest.  
虢石父清了清嗓子，张口就唱。  
Guo Shifu cleared his throat and began to sing.  
他唱的大意是：天灾来了，死了也没处诉苦。  
The rough meaning of his song was this: when disaster comes, even death leaves no room for complaint.  
周公召公奔走呼号，天子心疼，诸侯落泪。  
Duke Zhou and Duke Shao rush about crying orders, the Son of Heaven aches with concern, and the feudal lords weep.  
大家就算做了鬼，也觉得幸福。  
Even as ghosts, everyone would count themselves blessed.  
烈马战车驰援灾区，锹头锄头左右开工。  
Warhorses and chariots race to the disaster zone, while shovels and hoes work from left and right.  
百姓们齐声感谢大王。  
The people all raise their voices to thank the king.  
就算再死一次，也心满意足。  
They would be satisfied even if they had to die all over again.  
幽王听完，果然乐了。  
The king grinned from ear to ear when he heard it.  
他问：“做我的臣民，冤死了也幸福？”  
He asked, "So if you are my subjects, even dying unjustly still makes you happy?"  
虢石父忙说：“那当然，百姓就是这么想的。”  
Guo Shifu hurried to say, "Of course. That is exactly how the people feel."  
幽王高兴地说：“好，传唱下去。”  
The delighted king said, "Good. Spread it everywhere."  
虢石父正得意，幽王却忽然哈哈大笑。  
Guo Shifu was basking in his own success when the king suddenly burst into loud laughter.  
幽王边笑边说，要是虢石父出门被车撞死，自己也一定亲自去叫魂，让他死得幸福。  
Laughing as he spoke, the king said that if Guo Shifu were run over and killed on the way home, he would personally come cry over him so that he too could die happily.  
幽王当然不傻。  
The king was not stupid, of course.  
他知道这首歌就是虢石父现编出来拍马屁的。  
He knew perfectly well that Guo Shifu had improvised（现编） the whole song simply to flatter him.  
不过，他偏偏喜欢这首歌，也喜欢虢石父这个人。  
Even so, he happened to like both the song and the man who had sung it.  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`现在分词作状语`、`宾语从句`、`目的状语从句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `the king said`，前面用 `Laughing as he spoke` 补充状态，后面接说话内容。<br>
难点拆解：`Seeing the king's mood improve` 和 `Laughing as he spoke` 都是现在分词短语，起状语作用；`He knew ... that ...` 里的 `that ...` 是宾语从句；`so that he too could die happily` 表示目的或结果，说明他那句挖苦话的落点。<br>
常用短语：
- `vent one's frustration`：发泄闷气
- `catch someone's interest`：引起某人的兴趣
- `burst into loud laughter`：突然大笑起来
- `improvise the whole thing`：把整件事现编出来

### 褒城大夫
### The Lord of Baocheng

褒城离震中更近，所以城里房屋损坏得很厉害。  
Baocheng was closer to the epicenter, so its buildings were damaged much more severely.  
十几天里，褒洪德一直跟着父亲忙着救灾。  
For more than ten days, Bao Hongde followed his father around in relief work.  
他根本顾不上去见天仙妹妹。  
He simply had no chance to go see the Fairy Maiden.  
等忙得差不多了，褒顼决定去一趟镐京。  
Once the worst of the work was over, Bao Xu decided to make a trip to Haojing.  
一来汇报褒城灾情。  
One reason was to report Baocheng's condition after the disaster.  
二来给周幽王问安。  
Another was to pay respects to King You.  
三来还带了几首歌功颂德的民歌。  
And he also took along a few folk songs praising the ruler.  
半路上，他听说周公召公刚刚骂过幽王。  
On the way, he heard that Duke Zhou and Duke Shao had just scolded the king.  
百姓还都在夸他们。  
And the common people were all praising them for it.  
褒顼心里一动。  
That set Bao Xu thinking.  
他觉得周公召公进谏都没事，自己说几句，既安全又能得名声。  
He figured that if even Duke Zhou and Duke Shao could remonstrate（进谏） without trouble, then a few words from him should be both safe and good for his reputation.  
于是到了镐京，他洗把脸，正了正帽子，就匆匆忙忙去见王了。  
So after arriving in Haojing, he washed his face, straightened his hat, and hurried off to see the king.  
可惜他忘了先照照自己。  
Unfortunately, he forgot to take a good look at himself first.  
古往今来，不管是拍马屁还是提意见，都得先看准对方脸色。  
In every age, whether you flatter someone or advise him, you first have to read his expression correctly.  
褒顼上来就说：“大王，地震是人祸招来的，您还是别选美女了。”  
Bao Xu opened with, "Your Majesty, the earthquake was brought on by human misrule. You really should stop selecting beauties."  
幽王一听就火了。  
King You flared up the moment he heard it.  
他心想，周公召公仗着资格老，说几句也就算了。  
He thought to himself that Duke Zhou and Duke Shao could get away with a lecture because of their seniority.  
你褒顼算什么东西？  
But who exactly did Bao Xu think he was?  
幽王问他：“你的意思，是我把地震弄出来的？”  
The king asked, "Are you saying I caused the earthquake?"  
褒顼一下就慌了。  
Bao Xu panicked at once.  
他支支吾吾，连句整话都说不出了。  
He stammered so badly that he could hardly form a complete sentence.  
幽王拍桌子下令，把他抓起来关进大牢。  
The king slapped the table and ordered him seized and thrown into prison.  
幽王无意中倒做对了一件事。  
By accident, the king had done one thing right.  
为什么选了一年多美女，还是选不到满意的？  
Why had the beauty search dragged on for more than a year without success?  
因为大家都没动力，甚至心里都在抵触。  
Because nobody had any incentive, and deep down most people were resisting the task.  
事情自然就办不好。  
Naturally, the work was done badly.  
想让别人卖力，光靠觉悟不行。  
If you want people to work hard, moral awareness alone is not enough.  
办法只有两个。  
There are only two methods.  
要么悬出重赏。  
Either you offer a heavy reward.  
重赏之下，必有勇夫。  
Under great rewards, brave men will always appear.  
要么把他爹关进牢里，看他还敢不卖力。  
Or you lock up somebody's father and see whether he still dares to hold back.  
这种办法，说白了就是绑架。  
To put it bluntly, that method is kidnapping.  
从管理学角度看，后者往往更有效。  
From a management perspective, the latter often works better.  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`条件状语从句`、`either ... or ...` 结构、`宾语从句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `There are only two methods`，后面展开 `Either ... or ...` 两种办法。<br>
难点拆解：`If you want people to work hard` 是条件状语从句；`Either you offer ... or you lock up ...` 是标准二选一结构；`He figured that ...` 里的 `that ...` 是宾语从句，用来表达人物心里的判断。<br>
常用短语：
- `read someone's expression correctly`：先看准对方脸色
- `flare up`：一下子发火
- `have any incentive`：有动力
- `to put it bluntly`：说白了

褒顼下狱后，家里立刻乱了套。  
Once Bao Xu was jailed, the household was thrown into chaos.  
褒洪德第一时间揣着银子和土产赶到首都。  
Bao Hongde immediately rushed to the capital carrying silver and local gifts.  
走后门这件事，古今一向差不多。  
As for back-door favors, things were much the same then as now.  
可首都不是褒城。  
But the capital was not Baocheng.  
那里到处都是王孙公子。  
It was packed with princes and noble sons.  
在褒城还算人物的褒顼，到了镐京根本什么也不是。  
Bao Xu might count for something in Baocheng, but in Haojing he counted for nothing at all.  
褒洪德揣着金银到了首都，才真正明白什么叫提着猪头找不到庙门。  
Only after arriving in the capital with gold and silver did Bao Hongde truly understand what it meant to carry a pig's head around and still not find the temple gate.  
他连银子该送给谁都不知道。  
He did not even know whom he was supposed to bribe.  
眼看父亲在牢里都快关满一年了，事情还是毫无进展。  
His father was nearing a full year in prison, and still nothing had moved.  
母子两个都傻了眼。  
Mother and son were beside themselves.  
如果老爷死在牢里，这一家子的封地也就保不住了。  
If the old man died in jail, the family would not be able to keep its fief.  
关键时刻，褒母忽然灵光一闪。  
At the crucial moment, Bao Hongde's mother suddenly had a flash of inspiration.  
她说：“天子不就想要美女吗？”  
She said, "Isn't it simply a beauty that the Son of Heaven wants?"  
“只要我们献上一个绝色美人，还怕你爹出不来？”  
"If we present him with a stunning beauty, why would your father not be released?"  
话是这么说，做起来却不容易。  
That sounded simple, but doing it was another matter.  
幽王全国海选了一年多都没挑中。  
King You had held a nationwide search for more than a year and still found no one good enough.  
他们又上哪儿找去？  
Where were they supposed to find such a woman?  
褒母咬咬牙，说不如把女儿送进去。  
Biting the bullet, Bao's mother suggested sending her own daughter in.  
褒洪德立刻反对。  
Bao Hongde objected at once.  
他说，妹妹那双罗圈腿，顶多也就是个宫女命。  
He said his sister's bowed legs might at best earn her a place as a palace maid.  
母子俩商量半天，还是没结果。  
Mother and son talked in circles for ages and still got nowhere.  
偏偏就在这时，褒洪德脑子里猛地一亮。  
And then, at exactly that moment, a sudden light flashed through Bao Hongde's mind.  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`条件状语从句`、`反问句`、`宾语从句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `why would your father not be released`，前面接 `If we present him with ...` 设定条件。<br>
难点拆解：`If we present him with a stunning beauty` 是条件状语从句；后半句虽然是问句形式，其实是反问，意思是“那还怕什么”；`He said ...` 后面的内容则是宾语从句，用来转述褒洪德的判断。<br>
常用短语：
- `back-door favors`：走后门
- `count for nothing`：根本不算什么
- `have a flash of inspiration`：突然灵光一闪
- `bite the bullet`：咬牙硬扛，硬着头皮

他想到了天仙妹妹。  
He thought of the Fairy Maiden.  
如果把她献给幽王，幽王一定会喜欢。  
If he offered her to King You, the king would surely like her.  
于是褒洪德带着聘礼，去了天仙妹妹家。  
So Bao Hongde took betrothal gifts and went to the Fairy Maiden's home.  
桑叔高兴得很。  
Sang Shu was delighted.  
地震把家里震裂了，正愁没钱修。  
The earthquake had cracked the house, and money for repairs was badly needed.  
现在既能得银子，女儿又像是有了好归宿，怎么会不高兴？  
Now he could get silver, and his daughter seemed to have found a good future. Why would he not be pleased?  
就这样，天仙妹妹进了褒家。  
That was how the Fairy Maiden entered the Bao household.  
她自己也挺高兴。  
She herself was rather pleased as well.  
从今以后，她可以吃好的、喝好的，还能挤进上流社会。  
From now on she could eat well, drink well, and edge her way into upper society.  
她也知道，按门第来说，自己进不了正门。  
She knew too that, by the rules of rank, she could never enter as the principal wife.  
最多也就是个妾。  
At most she could only become a concubine（妾）.  
可妾又怎么了？  
But what was so bad about that?  
运气好了，未必不能翻身。  
With enough luck, she might still rise one day.  
可她的美梦，很快就被打碎了。  
But that pleasant dream was shattered almost immediately.  
褒洪德把眼下的大局给她讲了一遍。  
Bao Hongde laid out the whole situation for her.  
又给她讲了许多舍己救人、舍生取义的故事。  
Then he told her many stories about sacrificing oneself for others and giving up life for a greater cause.  
最后，他把拿她换父亲出狱的计划，原原本本说了出来。  
At last he told her, plainly and in full, of the plan to trade her for his father's release.  
天仙妹妹哭了。  
The Fairy Maiden wept.  
褒子哥哥也哭了。  
Brother Baozi wept too.  
褒母也跟着哭，还跪下来求她救全家。  
Bao's mother joined in, crying as well, and even knelt to beg her to save the whole family.  
天仙妹妹心想，我既然爱褒子哥哥，就该为他作出牺牲。  
The Fairy Maiden thought that if she truly loved Brother Baozi, then she ought to make a sacrifice for him.  
最后，她答应了。  
In the end, she agreed.  
褒洪德感动得鼻涕眼泪一起流。  
Bao Hongde was so moved that tears and snot ran together.  
可让他在亲爹和心上人之间选一个，他终究还是选了亲爹。  
But when forced to choose between his own father and the woman he loved, he still chose his father.  
到了这个份上，还能唱什么“更爱美人”呢？  
At that point, who could still go on singing about loving beauty more?  

### 褒姒的第一次
### Bao Si's First Time

事情就这样定下来了。  
That was how the matter was settled.  
不过，正式送进宫前，还得先做准备。  
But before she could be sent to the palace, some preparations still had to be made.  
第一件事，就是给天仙妹妹换个身份。  
The first task was to give the Fairy Maiden a new identity.  
以她原来的出身，根本不可能进宫。  
With her original background, there was no way she could enter the royal palace.  
好在全城户籍都掌握在褒家手里。  
Luckily, the Bao family controlled the household registers of the whole city.  
所以他们也不用找什么造假作坊。  
So there was no need to look for a forgery shop.  
假户口，自己就能做。  
They could forge the papers themselves.  
从这一刻起，她不再姓桑，也不再叫天仙妹妹。  
From that moment on, she no longer bore the surname Sang, and she was no longer called the Fairy Maiden.  
她改名叫褒姒。  
She was renamed Bao Si.  
官方身份，则成了褒洪德的堂妹，一个贵族女子。  
Her official identity became that of Bao Hongde's cousin, a noblewoman.  
身份造好之后，接下来就是培训。  
Once the identity was forged, the next step was training.  
训练内容也不复杂。  
The contents of that training were not especially complicated.  
无非是穿衣、礼仪、说话口气、走路姿态。  
They were mainly dress, etiquette（礼仪）, tone of speech, and the way she walked.  
目的只有一个。  
There was only one goal.  
那就是让人一看就觉得，她天生出自贵族。  
It was to make people feel at a glance that she had been born into nobility.  
而不是个刚换上好衣裳的乡下姑娘。  
Not a country girl who had merely changed into finer clothes.  
培训很成功。  
The training was a great success.  
这也说明，天仙妹妹天生就有贵族气。  
That also proved that the Fairy Maiden had a natural aristocratic air.  
从此以后，天仙妹妹消失了。  
From then on, the Fairy Maiden vanished.  
褒姒诞生了。  
Bao Si was born.  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`目的状语`、`宾语从句`、`被动含义`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `The goal was to make people feel ...`。<br>
难点拆解：`to make people feel ...` 是不定式作表语补充目的；`that she had been born into nobility` 是宾语从句，说明别人“觉得什么”；这里的 `had been born` 带有被动含义，表示“出身于”，不是简单过去动作。<br>
常用短语：
- `give someone a new identity`：给某人换一个身份
- `control the household registers`：掌握户籍
- `at a glance`：一眼就
- `be born into nobility`：生来出自贵族之家

一切准备停当后，褒母亲自让人备好了车。  
Once everything was ready, Bao's mother personally ordered a carriage prepared.  
第二天一早，就让儿子送褒姒进京。  
Early the next morning, she would have her son escort Bao Si to the capital.  
可到了晚上，褒子哥哥还是忍不住去见她最后一面。  
But that night, Brother Baozi still could not resist going to see her one last time.  
他还文绉绉地叹了一句：“乐莫乐兮新相知，悲莫悲兮生别离。”  
He even sighed in lofty style, "Nothing is happier than new love, and nothing sadder than parting while still alive."  
昏暗灯光下，两个人都伤心得很。  
In the dim light, both of them were heartbroken.  
情到深处，自然难以自持。  
When feeling runs deep, people naturally struggle to keep control.  
他们抱在了一起。  
They fell into each other's arms.  
褒姒说：“哥哥，你要了我吧。”  
Bao Si said, "Brother, take me now."  
这种时候，只要是个正常男人，就没法拒绝。  
At such a moment, any normal man would have been unable to refuse.  
实际上，褒子哥哥深夜跑来，本来就有这个意思。  
In truth, that was exactly what Brother Baozi had come there for in the middle of the night.  
于是灯影摇动，屋里响起了低低的呻吟。  
So the lamplight trembled, and soft moans rose through the room.  
第二天一早，两人都带着满脸倦意上路。  
The next morning, both of them set out with deep weariness on their faces.  
褒母只当他们是哭过，哪里知道他们不止哭过。  
Bao's mother assumed they had been crying, never knowing they had done more than cry.  
褒城离镐京不算太远。  
Baocheng was not terribly far from Haojing.  
可要翻过秦岭，却一点也不轻松。  
But crossing the Qinling Mountains was no easy task.  
他们足足走了三天，才到了都城。  
It took them a full three days to reach the capital.  
一路上，自然少不了云雨之欢。  
And along the road, they naturally did not refrain from lovers' pleasures.  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`时间状语从句`、`条件状语从句`、`宾语从句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `Bao's mother assumed ...` 和 `any normal man would have been unable to refuse`。<br>
难点拆解：`Once everything was ready` 是时间状语从句；`At such a moment` 之后隐含条件，“在这种情况下”；`Bao's mother assumed they had been crying` 中 `they had been crying` 是宾语从句，说明她以为发生了什么。<br>
常用短语：
- `one last time`：最后一次
- `keep control`：控制住自己
- `be unable to refuse`：无法拒绝
- `set out with ... on one's face`：带着某种神情出发

幽王选美女，大致有一套流程。  
King You's beauty search followed a basic procedure.  
先由大臣们初选。  
First, ministers carried out the preliminary screening.  
再由幽王在朝廷亲自面试。  
Then King You personally interviewed the finalists in court.  
朝廷就是他处理政务的地方。  
Court was the place where he handled state affairs.  
差不多相当于今天人们脑补中的白宫场景。  
It was something like the White House as modern people imagine it.  
登基三年来，幽王在这里面试过不知多少美女。  
In the three years since his accession（登基）, King You had interviewed an untold number of beauties there.  
可没有一个，能让他真正心动。  
Yet not one of them had truly stirred his heart.  
他常常感慨，天下无美女，做天子也没意思。  
He often lamented that there were no beauties under Heaven and that being the Son of Heaven was therefore no fun at all.  
等到面试这天，他懒洋洋坐在王座上，本来也没抱太大希望。  
So when interview day came, he lounged on the throne with very little hope to begin with.  
褒洪德上殿后，先得做选手介绍。  
Once Bao Hongde stepped into court, he first had to introduce the contestant.  
毕竟，这个美人是他选送来的。  
After all, he was the one who had recommended this beauty.  
他对幽王说：“大王，我爹褒顼老糊涂了，冒犯了您。”  
He said to the king, "Your Majesty, my father Bao Xu grew old and muddle-headed and offended you."  
“为了替他赎罪，我特地寻来一个绝世美女，进献给大王。”  
"In order to atone（赎罪） for him, I have specially searched out an incomparable beauty and present her to Your Majesty."  
幽王淡淡地说：“带上来看看。”  
The king said coolly, "Bring her in and let me see."  
音乐声中，褒姒走上殿来。  
To the sound of music, Bao Si entered the hall.  
褒洪德回头看她，只见她缓缓走来，真像仙女下凡。  
Bao Hongde glanced back and saw her coming forward slowly, like a fairy descending to earth.  
整个朝堂的人都屏住了呼吸。  
Everyone in court held their breath.  
所有目光，都盯住了同一个方向。  
Every eye fixed on the same direction.  
褒洪德再回头看幽王时，心里已经明白了。  
When Bao Hongde turned back toward the king, he already understood.  
父亲这回一定能出狱了。  
His father would surely be released this time.  
幽王已经看呆了。  
King You had already been struck dumb.  
口水几乎都要从嘴边流下来。  
His saliva almost seemed ready to spill from the corner of his mouth.  
在那一刻，他知道自己以后的人生都要归这个女人了。  
At that moment, he knew that the rest of his life would belong to this woman.  
只是他还不知道，连自己的江山也会被她拖进去。  
What he did not yet know was that his kingdom would be dragged in with it as well.  
很快，幽王下了旨。  
Very soon, the king gave his decree.  
美人留下。  
The beauty was to remain.  
褒顼放人。  
Bao Xu was to be released.  
不仅官复原职，还要重重赏赐，算作国家补偿。  
Not only was he to be restored to office, he was also to receive a rich reward as state compensation（补偿）.  
父亲得救了。  
His father was saved.  
可天仙妹妹，也从此没了。  
But the Fairy Maiden was lost to him from that day on.  
褒洪德眼里含着泪。  
Bao Hongde's eyes filled with tears.  
他也说不清，自己究竟是高兴，还是伤心。  
He himself could not tell whether he was happy or heartbroken.  
也许，两样都有。  
Perhaps he was both.  
褒姒随着宫人往后宫走去。  
Bao Si walked toward the inner palace with the court attendants.  
临走时，她还是忍不住回头看了褒洪德一眼。  
As she left, she still could not help looking back at Bao Hongde once.  
她那时还不知道。  
At that moment, she did not yet know.  
从这一刻起，她会成为中国历史上的名女人。  
From that moment on, she would become a famous woman in Chinese history.  
她也会改写这段历史。  
And she would help rewrite that history.  
从天仙妹妹到“芙蓉姐姐”式的名人，成名的方法有很多。  
From a Fairy Maiden to a Sister Furong（芙蓉姐姐）-style celebrity, there are many ways to become famous.  
这只是其中一种。  
This was only one of them.  
褒姒究竟会怎样改写中国历史？  
How exactly would Bao Si rewrite Chinese history?  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`时间状语从句`、`宾语从句`、`表语从句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `he knew` 和 `What he did not yet know was ...`。<br>
难点拆解：`When Bao Hongde turned back ...` 是时间状语从句；`he knew that ...` 里的 `that ...` 是宾语从句；`What he did not yet know was ...` 则是典型的主语从句加表语结构，用来突出“他不知道的真正后果是什么”。<br>
常用短语：
- `stir one's heart`：让某人心动
- `be struck dumb`：看呆了
- `from that moment on`：从那一刻起
- `help rewrite history`：帮助改写历史

## 第二章 后宫风暴
## Chapter 2: Turmoil in the Inner Palace

选秀的结果，算得上皆大欢喜。  
The beauty selection ended, at least on the surface, in general satisfaction.  
幽王时代的“超女冠军”，就这样诞生了。  
And so King You's reign produced its own "Super Girl champion."  
幽王当即宣布退朝。  
The king immediately dismissed court.  
一回到后宫，他就抱着褒姒左看右看，上看下看。  
The moment he returned to the harem（后宫）, he held Bao Si and looked her over from every angle.  
穿着衣服看，脱了衣服也看。  
He admired her clothed, and admired her unclothed as well.  
怎么看，都觉得她美得不可方物。  
However he looked, she seemed breathtakingly（美得惊人的） beautiful.  
比起当年的妲己，恐怕还要艳上三分。  
He thought she might even outshine the legendary Daji by several degrees.  
看够了，摸够了，幽王决定给她“颁奖”。  
Once he had looked and touched to his heart's content, King You decided it was time to "present the prize."  
天子能赏给女臣民的最高奖赏是什么？  
What was the highest reward a Son of Heaven could give a female subject?  
那就是临幸。  
It was royal favor in bed.  
说白了，就是把她抱上床。  
To put it bluntly, it meant taking her to bed himself.  
有人也许会问，幽王难道没发现这个“冠军”已经不是处女了吗？  
Some people might ask whether the king failed to notice that this "champion" was no longer a virgin.  
答案很简单。  
The answer is simple.  
那个年代的中国人，还没有这种处女情结。  
In those days, the Chinese had not yet developed that obsession（执念） with virginity.  

### 后宫的编制问题
### The Staffing Problem in the Harem

这里得先说说周朝后宫的编制。  
Here we need to say something first about the staffing structure of the Zhou harem.  
也就是，天子法定到底能有多少女人。  
In other words, how many women the Son of Heaven was legally allowed to have.  
《礼记》说，后宫归王后统领。  
The *Book of Rites* says that the harem was under the queen's authority.  
王后之下，是三个夫人、九个嫔、二十七个世妇、八十一个御妻。  
Below the queen came three principal consorts（夫人）, nine secondary consorts（嫔）, twenty-seven court ladies, and eighty-one royal women.  
全部加起来，一共一百二十一个女人。  
Altogether, that made one hundred and twenty-one women.  
差不多就是一个加强连的规模。  
That was roughly the size of a reinforced company.  
这个数字正好是十一的平方。  
The number happened to be eleven squared.  
也就是说，每排十一个人，能整整排出十一排。  
That is to say, they could be lined up in eleven neat rows of eleven.  
一年三百六十五天，平均下来，每个人大概能轮上三天。  
With 365 days in a year, each woman averaged about three days.  
万一轮到你的那天正赶上来月经，那也只能算你倒霉。  
If your turn happened to fall on your period, then that was simply your bad luck.  
天子正好也能顺便歇一天。  
The Son of Heaven, meanwhile, got a day off.  
所以说，当天子也不容易。  
So being king was not especially easy either.  
这一百二十一个女人里，王后和三个夫人还有硬性规定。  
Among these 121 women, the queen and the three principal consorts had additional requirements.  
她们都必须是诸侯或公卿的女儿。  
They all had to be daughters of feudal lords or high ministers.  
幽王登基第一年，就把这套编制全用满了。  
In the very first year of his reign, King You had already filled the entire roster.  
如今褒姒来了，却没有空缺。  
Now Bao Si had arrived, but there was no vacancy.  
那怎么办？  
So what was to be done?  
幽王的想法很简单。  
King You's thinking was very simple.  
先上手再说，编制问题以后再补。  
Enjoy her first, and sort out the staffing issue later.  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`条件状语从句`、`并列句`、`数字表达`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `that made one hundred and twenty-one women`，后面再展开解释。<br>
难点拆解：`If your turn happened to fall ...` 是条件状语从句，表示“如果正好轮到……”；前后多句用并列方式一层层补充人数、排布和轮值逻辑；英文里数字说明通常先给总数，再给分解，读起来更清楚。<br>
常用短语：
- `under the queen's authority`：归王后统领
- `a reinforced company`：一个加强连
- `happen to fall on`：恰好赶上
- `sort out ... later`：以后再处理……

宫里又来了新人。  
A newcomer had entered the palace.  
褒姒进宫的消息，很快传遍了整个后宫。  
News of Bao Si's arrival spread through the whole harem in no time.  
大家起初都没把她当回事。  
At first, nobody took her very seriously.  
不就是又来了一个“超女”吗？  
Wasn't she just another "Super Girl" contestant?  
按惯例，幽王对这种新人的新鲜感通常只维持一到三天。  
By custom, King You's freshness of interest in such newcomers lasted only one to three days.  
顶多三天，他就会腻。  
Three days at most, and he would grow bored.  
一旦失去兴趣，新人就会被打发去做宫女。  
Once he lost interest, the newcomer would be sent off to ordinary palace service.  
可这一次，所有人都看错了。  
This time, however, everyone was wrong.  
三天过去，褒姒还在幽王身边。  
Three days passed, and Bao Si was still at the king's side.  
十三天过去，幽王还是陪着她。  
Thirteen days passed, and he was still with her.  
三十天一到，整个后宫都炸锅了。  
By day thirty, the whole harem was in an uproar.  
大家都意识到，事情不妙。  
Everyone realized that something was very wrong.  
幽王这回算是被彻底套牢了。  
This time, King You had been thoroughly trapped.  
歌里不是唱“雨露滋润禾苗壮”吗？  
As the song goes, "Rain and dew make the crops grow strong."  
这一百多个有正式编制的姐妹，本来一年也分不到几回“雨露”。  
The hundred-odd women on the official roster barely got a touch of that "dew" a few times a year.  
现在倒好，褒姒一来，整天就她那片云在下雨。  
But once Bao Si arrived, every cloud in the palace seemed to rain only over her patch.  
她那里被浇得透透的，别人的责任田却快成黄土高坡了。  
Her field was being watered endlessly, while everyone else's plots were turning into dry loess hills.  
为什么会这样？  
Why had it come to this?  
为什么幽王在别人身上会审美疲劳，到了褒姒这里却一点也不疲劳？  
Why did King You tire of every other beauty, yet never tire of Bao Si?  
这个十四岁的采蘑菇小姑娘，究竟有什么魔力？  
What special spell did this fourteen-year-old mushroom girl possess?  

褒姒十四岁，按今天看，不过是初二的年纪。  
Bao Si was fourteen, which by modern standards is barely middle-school age.  
放在今天，幽王这事都能算侵犯未成年人了。  
By today's standards, what the king was doing would count as violating a minor.  
可褒姒偏偏已经被迫去过成人的生活。  
And yet Bao Si had already been pushed into adult life.  
虽说她发育得比常人早些，可她自己还是懵懵懂懂。  
She may have matured earlier than most, but inwardly she was still bewildered（茫然的） and unsure.  
宫里没有桑木弓，也没有簸箕。  
There were no mulberry bows in the palace, and no baskets either.  
更没有蘑菇可采。  
There were no mushrooms to gather.  
她整天该干什么，她自己也不知道。  
She did not even know what she was supposed to do all day.  
她想念褒子哥哥。  
She missed Brother Baozi.  
她并不喜欢眼前这个老男人。  
She did not actually like the old man before her.  
所以她想说什么就说什么，想干什么就干什么。  
So she said whatever she pleased and did whatever she pleased.  
奇怪的是，这老男人对她越来越好。  
Strangely, the old man treated her better and better.  
看上去，他甚至像是在讨好她。  
At times, he even seemed to be currying favor（讨好） with her.  
为什么会这样？  
Why was that?  
褒姒自己也不知道。  
Bao Si herself did not know.  
原因有两个。  
There were two reasons.  
先说第一个。  
Let us start with the first.  

俗话说，家花不如野花香。  
As the saying goes, the flower at home never smells as sweet as the wild one outside.  
越带野性，越招人喜欢。  
The more untamed a woman seems, the more captivating（迷人的） she can be.  
幽王后宫里那些正式编制的女人，并不是不优秀。  
The women on King You's official roster were not lacking in excellence.  
问题是，她们太优秀了。  
The problem was that they were too accomplished.  
不是大家闺秀，就是小家碧玉。  
They were either grand ladies from noble houses or refined little beauties.  
她们懂礼仪，知进退。  
They understood etiquette and knew exactly when to advance and when to retreat.  
琴棋书画，各有各的本事。  
Each had her own gifts in music, chess, calligraphy, and painting.  
说白了，那全是高素质的知性美女。  
To speak plainly, they were polished, intelligent beauties of the highest order.  
可幽王偏偏不喜欢。  
Yet King You did not like them.  
因为从小到大，他见到的女人全是这一类。  
From childhood on, every woman he had known had been of that type.  
从祖母到母亲，再到一百多个姨妈，再到自己那一百多个女人。  
From grandmother to mother, to a hundred-odd aunts, and then to his own hundred-odd women.  
个个都温柔贤惠，谈吐得体。  
Every one of them was gentle, virtuous, and impeccably（无可挑剔地） mannered.  
可在他眼里，这样的女人就像温吞水。  
But to him, women like that were like lukewarm water.  
没有性格，千人一面。  
They had no edge, and they all looked the same.  
说到底，就是看腻了。  
In the end, he had simply grown sick of them.  
审美疲劳早就重到了极点。  
His aesthetic fatigue had reached the limit.  
可褒姒完全不一样。  
Bao Si was entirely different.  
她天真烂漫，心直口快。  
She was innocent, lively, and blunt-spoken.  
一点小事就大惊小怪。  
She made a fuss over the smallest things.  
有时还会冒脏话。  
Sometimes she even swore.  
急起来，连幽王也敢骂。  
When she got angry, she even scolded the king himself.  
她喜欢光着脚到处跑。  
She liked running around barefoot.  
喜欢在花园里追蝴蝶。  
She liked chasing butterflies in the garden.  
还喜欢随地吐痰。  
She even liked spitting wherever she pleased.  
说得更直白些，她活脱脱就是《还珠格格》里的小燕子。  
To put it more bluntly, she was practically Little Swallow from *My Fair Princess*.  
物以稀为贵。  
Rare things are precious.  
男人不坏，女人不爱。  
People say women do not love men who are too good.  
女人不坏，男人其实也不爱。  
The same, in truth, goes for men and women.  
所以，尽管褒姒只有十四岁。  
So although Bao Si was only fourteen.  
尽管她平时营养一般，身体发育也还没有到最成熟的状态。  
Although her nutrition had been poor and her body had not yet reached full maturity.  
按理说，四十多岁的幽王，不该只从性这一点上被她征服。  
In theory, a man in his forties should not have been conquered by her on the basis of sex alone.  
可事实就是，幽王的确被她征服了。  
And yet the fact remained: King You was conquered.  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`让步状语从句`、`宾语从句`、`并列句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `King You was conquered`，前面用 `although ...` 和一连串并列句来铺垫原因。<br>
难点拆解：`Although ...` 是让步状语从句，表示“尽管如此”；中间大量并列短句用来堆出人物对比；`She did not know`、`he had simply grown sick of them` 这类表达都把心理变化写得很直接。<br>
常用短语：
- `grow sick of`：看腻了，厌倦了
- `currying favor with`：讨好……
- `be entirely different`：完全不同
- `on the basis of`：仅仅基于……

### 后宫风暴
### The Palace Storm

一般来说，超女一红，正牌歌手就会郁闷。  
Generally speaking, once a reality-show winner becomes a sensation（走红的人）, the established singers become miserable.  
褒姒一红，真正不高兴的却是后宫的领导层。  
Once Bao Si caught fire, the truly unhappy party was the leadership of the inner palace.  
按理论说，后宫的领导不是天子，而是王后。  
In theory, the harem was led not by the king but by the queen.  
《礼记》就是这么写的。  
That is exactly what the *Book of Rites* says.  
王后是申侯的女儿，姓申。  
The queen was the daughter of the Marquis of Shen and was surnamed Shen.  
当然，也可以说她姓姜。  
Of course, one could also say her surname was Jiang.  
因为申侯本来就是姜姓。  
That was because the Shen house originally belonged to the Jiang clan.  
因此史书上叫她申姜、申后、姜后，都不算错。  
For that reason, the histories call her Shen Jiang, Queen Shen, or Queen Jiang, and none of those is wrong.  
这里我们统一叫她申后。  
Here we will simply call her Queen Shen.  
申后不是“超女”。  
Queen Shen was not a "Super Girl."  
因为她根本不是选秀选出来的。  
She had not come out of any pageant at all.  
按照周朝的规矩，给太子娶妻有两个标准。  
Under Zhou rules, there were two standards for choosing a crown prince's wife.  
第一，必须是诸侯之女。  
First, she had to be the daughter of a feudal lord.  
第二，必须异姓，不能和王室同姓姬。  
Second, she had to be of a different clan and could not share the royal Ji surname.  
申国是侯国，申侯又属姜姓。  
Shen was a marquisate, and its ruling house belonged to the Jiang clan.  
所以当年周宣王就把申侯的女儿娶给了太子。  
So King Xuan of Zhou had married the Marquis of Shen's daughter to the crown prince.  
后来她顺理成章成了王后。  
Later, she naturally became queen.  
王后当然不是普通选秀女。  
A queen, of course, was no ordinary pageant girl.  
她是国母。  
She was the mother of the state.  
申后气得牙痒痒。  
Queen Shen was furious.  
她心里想，这个小妖精太猖狂了，非得给她点颜色看看。  
She thought to herself that this little vixen had become far too arrogant and had to be taught a lesson.  
她是国母没错。  
She was the mother of the state, yes.  
可即便如此，她也已经三十多天没见到幽王了。  
Yet even she had not seen King You for more than thirty days.  
理论往往很脆弱。  
Theory is often fragile.  
现实才最说明问题。  
Reality is what proves things in the end.  
这个时候，理论上的领导根本不敢硬碰幽王。  
At this point, the theoretical head of the harem dared not confront King You head-on.  
因为幽王随时都能把那套理论废掉。  
He had the power to nullify（废掉） that whole theory whenever he liked.  
所以申后只能在屋里干生气。  
So Queen Shen could do nothing but sit in her room and fume.  
偏偏就在这时，她儿子太子宜臼来了。  
Just then, her son, Crown Prince Yijiu, arrived.  
宜臼刚从外祖家回来。  
Yijiu had just returned from his maternal grandparents' home.  
一进门，看见母亲满脸愁容。  
The moment he entered, he saw the gloom on his mother's face.  
他问：“娘，谁欺负你了？是爹吗？”  
He asked, "Mother, who has bullied you? Was it Father?"  
放在平时，这简直是句废话。  
Under normal circumstances, that would have been a ridiculous question.  
除了幽王，谁还敢欺负申后？  
Who besides King You would dare bully Queen Shen?  
可今天，这句话正好戳中了她的痛处。  
But that day, the question touched the sorest point in her heart.  
申后把褒姒的事说了一遍。  
Queen Shen told him all about Bao Si.  
说完后，她忧心忡忡地叹道。  
When she was finished, she sighed in deep anxiety.  
她说，褒姒妖媚得很。  
She said Bao Si was dangerously seductive（狐媚的）.  
把幽王迷得茶饭不思，白日宣淫。  
She had charmed the king into neglecting food and sleep and indulging himself in broad daylight.  
照这样下去，迟早会给他生出一个弟弟来。  
If things went on like this, she would sooner or later bear him a younger brother.  
自己老了，王后的位置保不住也就算了。  
She herself was getting older; if she lost the queenship, so be it.  
最怕的是，连儿子的太子之位也保不住。  
What truly frightened her was that even her son's place as crown prince might not last.  
太子一听就急了。  
The prince grew agitated at once.  
他说，干脆把那女人的脸抓烂，看父王还宠不宠她。  
He said they should simply claw the woman's face to pieces and see if his father still adored her.  
他想到的办法就是毁容。  
The only method that occurred to him was disfigurement（毁容）.  
说实话，这办法又糙又狠。  
To be honest, it was crude and brutal.  
申后赶紧拦他。  
Queen Shen hurried to stop him.  
她说，真这么干，幽王非杀了他不可。  
She told him that if he really did it, the king would surely have him killed.  
可太子已经下了决心。  
But the crown prince had already made up his mind.  
他准备动手。  
He was ready to act.  
这场行动，有个名字。  
The operation even had a name.  
就叫“后宫风暴”。  
It was called "the Palace Storm."  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`宾语从句`、`条件状语从句`、`直接引语`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `What truly frightened her was ...` 和后面的 `He said ...`。<br>
难点拆解：`What truly frightened her was ...` 是主语从句加表语结构；`If things went on like this` 是条件状语从句；后面大量人物对白用直接引语，能保留母子对话的紧张感。<br>
常用短语：
- `touch the sorest point`：戳到最痛的地方
- `sooner or later`：迟早
- `make up one's mind`：下定决心
- `be ready to act`：准备动手

机会总是留给有准备的人。  
Opportunity always favors those who are prepared.  
太子正是那个有准备的人。  
The crown prince was precisely such a man.  
那天不刮风，不下雨，天上太阳很大。  
That day there was no wind, no rain, and the sun shone brightly.  
说白了，就是个大晴天。  
To put it simply, it was a splendidly sunny day.  
幽王去上朝了。  
King You had gone to court.  
他最近偶尔还会露个面，好让大家知道他还活着。  
Lately he had been showing up occasionally just so people would know he was still alive.  
“后宫风暴”行动开始了。  
Operation Palace Storm began.  

褒姒在屋里无聊得很。  
Bao Si was bored out of her mind in her room.  
一个老宫女正在给她讲鬼故事。  
An old palace maid was telling her ghost stories.  
忽然，外面闹了起来。  
Suddenly, a commotion broke out outside.  
更奇怪的是，吵闹声里居然还夹着男人的声音。  
Stranger still, there was a man's voice mixed into the noise.  
很快，宫女跑来禀报。  
A maid soon ran in to report.  
说是太子带着申后那边的一群宫女，跑来这里拔花。  
The crown prince had brought a group of Queen Shen's maids over to pull up flowers.  
没有得到允许，这就可以算采花贼了。  
Since they had no permission, they practically counted as flower thieves.  
双方就为这事在外头吵了起来。  
The two sides had begun quarreling outside over that very matter.  
褒姒一听，决定出去看看。  
Hearing this, Bao Si decided to go out and take a look.  
不就是拔几朵花吗？  
Wasn't it only a matter of pulling a few flowers?  
顶多算内部矛盾。  
At worst, it was merely an internal dispute.  
她走出去一看。  
When she stepped outside, she saw it at once.  
只见一个十七八岁的俊秀少年站在那里，正指挥众人胡乱拔花。  
A handsome youth of seventeen or eighteen stood there, directing the others as they uprooted flowers at random.  
不用问，这就是太子。  
Needless to say, this was the crown prince.  
褒妃一紧张，开口就叫了句：“哥哥，采花就采花，别连根拔啊。”  
Flustered, Lady Bao blurted out, "Brother, if you're going to pick flowers, at least don't yank them out by the roots."  
她这一声“哥哥”，把辈分都叫乱了。  
With that single word "brother," she completely muddled the hierarchy.  
按照原计划，太子一见到褒姒，就该扑上去抓她的脸。  
According to the original plan, the prince was supposed to lunge at Bao Si and claw her face the moment he saw her.  
直接毁容，省事利落。  
A quick disfigurement was meant to settle everything neatly.  
之所以用这么原始的办法，只因为那时还没有硫酸。  
The only reason for using such a primitive method was that sulfuric acid had not yet been invented.  
可当他真正看见褒姒时，他整个人都愣住了。  
But the instant he truly saw Bao Si, he froze.  
脚也迈不动了。  
He could not take another step.  
原先那阵“风暴”，一下就被忘到了脑后。  
The whole planned "storm" vanished from his mind at once.  
他甚至本能地想上前搭讪一句：“吃了吗？”  
He even had the instinct to walk up and ask, "Have you eaten?"  
幸好还没等他开口，旁边宫女已经先行礼了。  
Luckily, before he could say it, the palace maids were already bowing.  
她们齐声道：“娘娘万安。”  
Together they said, "Peace and health to Your Lady."  
太子这才猛地反应过来。  
Only then did the crown prince snap back to himself.  
原来她就是褒姒。  
So this was Bao Si.  
难怪父王会被迷成那样。  
No wonder his father had been bewitched by her.  
若是换成自己，恐怕也一样逃不过。  
Had it been him, he suspected he would not have escaped either.  
这么娇嫩的美人，谁还忍心打她？  
How could anyone bear to strike such a delicate beauty?  
又怎么忍心毁她的脸？  
And how could anyone bear to ruin her face?  
于是，“后宫风暴”临时降级成了“后宫微风”。  
So the Palace Storm was temporarily downgraded to a Palace Breeze.  
太子只在褒姒香肩上轻轻揉了两下，就算交差。  
The prince merely rubbed Bao Si's fragrant shoulder twice and counted the mission complete.  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`时间状语从句`、`原因状语从句`、`并列谓语`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `the whole planned "storm" vanished from his mind`。<br>
难点拆解：`the moment he saw her` 相当于时间状语从句；`The only reason ... was that ...` 说明原因；后面一串动作如 `froze`、`could not take another step`、`vanished` 形成并列推进，把太子的心理变化写得很快。<br>
常用短语：
- `at the very moment ...`：就在……的时候
- `vanish from one's mind`：一下从脑子里消失
- `snap back to oneself`：猛地回过神来
- `count the mission complete`：算作交差

### 幽王很生气
### The King Was Furious

幽王飞奔回宫。  
King You raced back to the palace.  
这辈子，他还没跑过这么快。  
He had never run so fast in his life.  
可他还是回来晚了。  
And yet he was still too late.  
等他气喘吁吁赶到后宫时，只见褒姒坐在地上哭。  
When he burst into the inner palace gasping for breath, he found Bao Si sitting on the ground in tears.  
他结结巴巴地问：“褒、褒、褒姒，怎么回事？”  
He stammered, "Bao-Bao-Bao Si, what happened?"  
幽王一到，宫女们立刻散开。  
The moment the king arrived, the palace maids scattered aside.  
褒姒看到他，哭得更凶。  
The sight of him only made Bao Si cry harder.  
幽王朝四周怒吼：“到底怎么回事？”  
King You roared at the women around him, "What in the world happened?"  
宫女们吓坏了，一个个跪在地上发抖。  
The maids were terrified and dropped to their knees, trembling.  
历来都是神仙打架，凡人遭殃。  
It is always the little people who suffer when the mighty go to war.  
最后，还是那个讲鬼故事的老宫女，哆哆嗦嗦把经过说了一遍。  
In the end it was the old maid who had been telling ghost stories who shakily recounted the whole affair.  
幽王听完勃然大怒。  
When he finished listening, King You flew into a rage.  
他说：“这个小兔崽子，真是反了他了。来人，把太子给我抓回来！”  
He shouted, "That little bastard has gone too far. Someone seize the crown prince and drag him back!"  
他素来一言九鼎。  
He had always expected his word to be absolute.  
如今竟然有人敢在他头上动土。  
And now someone had dared dig the soil over his own head.  
别管是谁，先砍了再说。  
Whoever it was, he felt like chopping first and asking questions later.  
内侍领命而去。  
The chamberlains took the order and went.  
幽王转身安慰褒姒。  
The king turned to comfort Bao Si.  
他说：“亲爱的，别怕，寡人为你做主。”  
He said, "My dear, don't be afraid. I will uphold justice for you."  
褒姒边哭边说：“大王，算了吧。你放我回家吧。”  
Through her tears Bao Si said, "Your Majesty, forget it. Please let me go home."  
“我家虽然穷，我爹娘虽然卑贱，可他们一直把我当掌上明珠，从没打过我骂过我。”  
"My home may be poor, and my parents may be lowly, but they have always treasured me like the pearl in their palm. They never beat me, never scolded me."  
“我宁可回去过苦日子，你就让我走吧。”  
"I would rather go back and live a hard life. Please let me go."  
她是真想回去。  
She genuinely wanted to leave.  
回去了，她就能和褒子哥哥团聚。  
If she went back, she could reunite with Brother Baozi.  
幽王立刻说不行。  
The king answered at once that it was impossible.  
褒姒又求了一遍。  
Bao Si pleaded again.  
她说自己什么都不要，只穿走自己的衣服就够了。  
She said she wanted nothing at all and would leave wearing only her own clothes.  
可她越这么说，幽王就越恼火。  
But the more she said it, the angrier King You became.  
他说：“不行。谁都可以走，就你不能走。”  
He said, "No. Anyone else may leave, but not you."  
“只要我还是大周天子一天，你就一天也不能离开我。”  
"As long as I remain the Son of Heaven of Zhou, you will not leave me for even a single day."  
这话说得一点回旋余地也没有。  
There was no room for argument in his tone.  
褒姒不再说话，只是继续哭。  
Bao Si said nothing more and simply kept crying.  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`让步状语从句`、`条件状语从句`、`宾语从句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `Anyone else may leave, but not you` 和 `As long as I remain ...`。<br>
难点拆解：`Although/though` 的意思在中文里由“虽然……可是……”体现；`As long as ...` 是条件状语从句，表示“只要……”；褒姒的话里 `she wanted nothing at all` 等内容都属于求情时的宾语表达。<br>
常用短语：
- `through one's tears`：一边哭一边
- `treasure someone like the pearl in one's palm`：视若掌上明珠
- `leave wearing only ...`：只带着……离开
- `there is no room for argument`：没有争辩余地

不久，内侍回来禀报，说太子畏罪潜逃了。  
Not long after, the chamberlains returned and reported that the crown prince had fled in fear of punishment.  
其实太子根本不是逃走，而是他们故意放跑的。  
In truth, the prince had not fled at all; they had let him go on purpose.  
为什么要放他？  
Why let him go?  
这里头大有学问。  
There was a great deal of practical wisdom in that.  
如果真把太子押回来，幽王一怒之下把他砍了。  
If they had actually brought the prince back and the king had killed him in rage.  
等过几天幽王后悔了，捉人的内侍多半就得跟着陪葬。  
Then once the king regretted it a few days later, the men who seized him would likely have paid with their lives.  
如果幽王最后只是骂一顿，那太子还是太子。  
If the king merely scolded him in the end, then the prince would still remain crown prince.  
而太子又最会记仇。  
And princes are very good at remembering grudges.  
等他日后登基，抓他的人也不会有好果子吃。  
When he eventually took the throne, the men who had captured him would not have a good end either.  
所以与其真把人抓来，不如偷偷放走，还能落个人情。  
So rather than truly arresting him, it was wiser to let him slip away quietly and earn his goodwill besides.  
幽王转念一想，也觉得跑了就跑了吧。  
On second thought, King You decided that if the prince had run, then let him run.  
真抓回来，反倒难办。  
Bringing him back would only have created a harder problem.  
不过，若是以后再闹这么一出怎么办？  
But what if something like this happened again?  
想来想去，他想出了一个办法。  
After turning it over in his mind, he came up with a solution.  
第二天，幽王就下了旨。  
The very next day, he issued a decree（诏令）.  
太子宜臼举止失当，命他去申国“插队”，接受外祖父的再教育。  
Crown Prince Yijiu had behaved improperly, so he was ordered to go to Shen State, be "sent down," and undergo reeducation（再教育） under his maternal grandfather.  
没有诏令，不得擅自回京。  
Without permission, he was not to return to the capital on his own.  
此外，褒姒在试用期内表现优异。  
In addition, Bao Si had performed excellently during her probationary（试用的） period.  
特许她由临时身份转为正式编制，职位是夫人。  
She was therefore promoted from temporary status to a formal place on the establishment, with the rank of consort.  
从此以后，改称褒妃。  
From then on, she was to be known as Consort Bao.  
至于后宫编制不是本来就满了吗？  
As for the fact that the harem roster had supposedly been full—  
满了又怎么样？  
what of it?  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`条件状语从句`、`被动表达`、`并列句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `he issued a decree`，后面分条说明对太子和褒姒的处理。<br>
难点拆解：前面连续两个 `If ...` 都是条件状语从句；`was ordered to go ...`、`was to be known as ...` 都是被动表达；英文常把政令内容拆成几句并列陈述，这样比一口气直译更清楚。<br>
常用短语：
- `pay with one's life`：赔上性命
- `remember grudges`：记仇
- `on second thought`：转念一想
- `be known as ...`：改称为……

### 继续斗争
### The Fight Goes On

第一次交手，申后显然没占到便宜。  
The first round had clearly gone badly for Queen Shen.  
不仅儿子被打发去了外祖家。  
Not only had her son been packed off to his grandparents' place.  
还让幽王顺手把褒姒封成了褒妃。  
King You had also used the chance to raise Bao Si to the rank of Consort Bao.  
她一下从“超女冠军”升成了“国家一级演员”。  
In one leap, Bao Si had risen from a "pageant champion" to something like a "national first-rank performer."  
申后当然气得不轻。  
Queen Shen was furious, of course.  
按理说，这样的大事本该征求她的意见。  
In theory, a matter this important should have required her opinion.  
可幽王竟然直接拍板了。  
Yet King You had decided it outright.  
显然，在幽王看来，太子的那场“后宫风暴”就是申后在背后指使的。  
Clearly, King You believed the prince's Palace Storm had been instigated by Queen Shen behind the scenes.  
申后越想越发愁。  
The more she thought about it, the more troubled she became.  
她从来没有什么后宫斗争经验。  
She had never had much experience in harem warfare.  
这些年，后宫一直是按资历排位置。  
For years the inner palace had run by seniority.  
谁也没见过褒姒这样一夜爆红的人物。  
No one had ever seen a figure like Bao Si, who had burst to fame overnight.  
怎么办？  
What was to be done?  
申后已经感觉到了威胁。  
Queen Shen could already feel the threat.  
她知道，莽撞下手只会把事情弄得更糟。  
She knew that rash action would only make matters worse.  
这时候，该想一个更讲技巧的法子了。  
What she needed now was a method with a little more finesse（技巧）.  
可她左思右想，就是想不出来。  
But however hard she thought, nothing came to mind.  
一转眼，褒妃进宫已经三个月了。  
In the blink of an eye, Consort Bao had been in the palace for three months.  
幽王还是天天和她腻在一起。  
King You was still attached to her day after day.  
而且据说，褒妃已经怀孕了。  
And rumor had it that Consort Bao was now pregnant.  
后宫里的人一听到怀孕这两个字，谁都知道这意味着什么。  
Anyone in the inner palace understood what the word pregnant could mean.  
申后越发郁闷，整天愁眉不展。  
Queen Shen grew more miserable, wearing a troubled face all day long.  
终于，有人来给她出主意了。  
At last, someone came to offer her advice.  
历史要继续往前走，总要有人跳出来推一把。  
If history is to keep moving, someone always has to step forward and give it a push.  
一个叫琼瑶的老宫女开口问她，娘娘为什么整日愁眉苦脸，别把身子愁坏了。  
An old palace maid named Qiongyao asked her why she wore such a gloomy face every day and warned her not to worry herself ill.  
琼瑶已经五十多岁了。  
Qiongyao was already past fifty.  
从前她是姜太后的宫女。  
She had once served Queen Dowager Jiang.  
太后去世以后，她就跟了申后。  
After the dowager died, she entered Queen Shen's service.  
她为人机灵体贴，又会讲故事。  
She was shrewd（机灵的）, attentive（体贴的）, and good at telling stories.  
太后当年很喜欢她。  
The dowager had been very fond of her.  
申后对她也一向另眼相待。  
Queen Shen had always treated her with special regard.  
平日说话，也比对别人随意些。  
She spoke to her more informally than to most others.  
申后见是琼瑶，便长叹一声，把自己的烦恼说了出来。  
Seeing it was Qiongyao, the queen let out a long sigh and told her all her worries.  
琼瑶说，主子有事，做奴婢的就该效劳。  
Qiongyao said that when the mistress had troubles, it was a servant's duty to shoulder the burden.  
她坦白说，这些天看着娘娘发愁，自己也跟着着急。  
She confessed that watching the queen worry these past days had made her anxious too.  
所以她已经暗中托人去查了褒妃的来历。  
So she had secretly arranged for people to investigate Consort Bao's background.  
如今，她倒真有一个办法。  
And now she truly had an idea.  
只是，不知娘娘愿不愿意听。  
She only did not know whether the queen wished to hear it.  
说这话时，她还特地朝四周看了看。  
As she said this, she glanced around the room.  
申后立刻会意，把身边侍女全支开了。  
Queen Shen understood at once and dismissed all the other maids.  
屋里只剩下她和琼瑶两个人。  
That left only the two of them in the room.  
接着，琼瑶讲起了一个故事。  
Then Qiongyao began to tell a story.  
而且，是一个让申后越听越兴奋的故事。  
And it was a story that made Queen Shen grow more excited with every word.  
那到底是什么故事？  
But what story was it?  

### 来说是非者
### The Tale-Bearer

自从幽王把宜臼赶走以后，宫里倒是平静了下来。  
Since King You had driven Yijiu away, the palace had quieted down somewhat.  
褒妃也正式进了编制，成了后宫里的正式成员。  
Consort Bao had also been formally entered onto the roster and had become an official member of the inner palace.  
最近又传出她有孕的消息，幽王自然高兴得很。  
And with news now spreading that she was pregnant, King You was naturally delighted.  
那一天，褒妃正和幽王一边吃新下来的水果，一边看西方歌舞。  
That day, Consort Bao and King You were eating the season's fresh fruit while watching western songs and dances.  
所谓西方歌舞，其实就是西边胡人的歌舞。  
These western performances were, in fact, songs and dances of the western Hu peoples.  
这时，申后急匆匆走了进来，身后还跟着老宫女琼瑶。  
At that moment Queen Shen came hurrying in, with the old maid Qiongyao following behind her.  
她一开口就说：“大王，出大事了，你还在这里歌舞升平。”  
The moment she opened her mouth, she said, "Your Majesty, something terrible has happened, and yet you sit here amid song and dance."  
众人一听都吃了一惊。  
Everyone was startled.  
幽王也愣了一下，忙问：“犬戎打过来了？”  
The king himself was taken aback and hurriedly asked, "Have the Quanrong attacked?"  
申后说：“比犬戎打来还可怕。”  
Queen Shen answered, "Something even worse than that."  
幽王顿时急了：“那到底是什么？快说。”  
King You grew anxious at once. "Then what is it? Speak quickly."  
申后却故作神秘，要先让旁人退下。  
But Queen Shen put on an air of mystery and demanded that the others withdraw first.  
幽王挥了挥手。  
The king waved his hand.  
除了褒妃，其余人都退了出去。  
Everyone withdrew except Consort Bao.  
申后还有些迟疑，又说褒妃最好也回避一下。  
Queen Shen hesitated and added that perhaps Consort Bao should avoid the scene as well.  
褒妃刚要起身，就被幽王一把拉住。  
Bao Fei had just begun to rise when King You caught her by the arm.  
他说：“不用，你不用走。”  
He said, "No need. You stay."  
申后似乎有些为难。  
Queen Shen looked somewhat uneasy.  
幽王却已经不耐烦了。  
But the king was already growing impatient.  
他嫌申后总爱装神弄鬼，吞吞吐吐。  
He was annoyed by her constant love of mystery and her hesitant manner.  
见幽王发了话，申后也没办法，只好对琼瑶说：“开始吧。”  
Seeing that the king had spoken, Queen Shen had no choice but to turn to Qiongyao and say, "Begin."  
幽王心里也有些纳闷。  
The king was puzzled as well.  
什么大事，竟然还得让一个老宫女来讲？  
What sort of grave matter required an old palace maid to tell it?  
不过看在琼瑶资历极老的份上，他还是点了点头。  
Yet out of respect for Qiongyao's great seniority, he nodded for her to continue.  
正所谓，来说是非者，便是是非人。  
As the saying goes, the one who brings gossip is often trouble himself.  
琼瑶终于开口讲故事了。  
At last Qiongyao began her story.  
她的开场白是：在很久很久以前……  
And her opening line was: "A very, very long time ago..."  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`原因状语从句`、`宾语从句`、`直接引语`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `Queen Shen had no choice` 和 `Qiongyao began her story`。<br>
难点拆解：`Seeing that the king had spoken` 带有原因状语意味，相当于“既然幽王已经发话”；`He was annoyed that ...` 这类结构里，后面是宾语从句；这一段大量用直接引语，能保持人物试探、逼问和卖关子的口气。<br>
常用短语：
- `put on an air of mystery`：故作神秘
- `have no choice but to do`：只好……
- `out of respect for ...`：出于对……的尊重
- `opening line`：开场白

## 第三章 琼瑶的故事
## Chapter 3: Qiongyao's Story

接下来这个故事，听着虽然荒诞。  
What follows may sound absurd（荒诞的）.  
但它绝不是作者凭空编出来的。  
But it is by no means something the author simply made up.  
太史公的《史记》里，对这段事写得很详细。  
Sima Qian's *Records of the Grand Historian* gives a fairly detailed account of it.  
所以，还是接着听琼瑶讲吧。  
So let us go back and listen to Qiongyao's story.  
那是十四年前。  
It happened fourteen years earlier.  
那时周王还是宣王，也就是幽王的父亲。  
At that time the Zhou king was still King Xuan, the father of King You.  
忽然有一天，宫里出了怪事。  
Then one day, something strange happened in the palace.  
到底什么怪事？  
What sort of strange thing?  
一个五十多岁的老宫女木桃，突然生下了一个女婴。  
An old palace maid named Mutao, already past fifty, suddenly gave birth to a baby girl.  
大家都看傻了。  
Everyone was dumbfounded（目瞪口呆的）.  
别人五十多岁早就绝经了，她居然还能生孩子。  
Most women her age had long since gone through menopause, yet she had somehow borne a child.  
更别说，一个五十多岁的老宫女居然还能被天子临幸。  
More than that, a palace maid in her fifties was somehow supposed to have received the king's favor in bed.  
这事听上去，连天子的口味都显得可疑。  
The story made even the king's tastes sound questionable.  
消息很快报到了姜后那里。  
The news was quickly reported to Queen Jiang.  
姜后先是一惊，随后大怒。  
Queen Jiang was shocked at first, and then she flew into a rage.  
她把木桃抓来审问。  
She had Mutao dragged in for questioning.  
她心里想，这个老东西比我还老，天子怎么可能看上她？  
She thought to herself that this old woman was even older than she was, so how could the king possibly have wanted her?  
她一拍桌子，喝道：“说，把你的野汉子招出来。”  
Slapping the table, she barked, "Speak. Name the man you've been fooling around with."  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`让步状语从句`、`间接引语`、`直接引语`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `Queen Jiang was shocked ... and then she flew into a rage`。<br>
难点拆解：`Most women her age ... yet she had somehow borne a child` 里 `yet` 表示强转折；`She thought to herself that ...` 后面是宾语从句；最后用直接引语保留姜后的质问语气。<br>
常用短语：
- `by no means`：绝不
- `fly into a rage`：勃然大怒
- `drag someone in for questioning`：把某人拖来审问
- `fool around with`：和……乱来

出乎众人意料，木桃一点也不害怕。  
To everyone's surprise, Mutao was not afraid in the least.  
她反倒喊起冤来。  
Instead, she cried out that she had been wronged.  
她说，要是真偷了情，这辈子也值了，死也认。  
She said that if she really had had an affair, her life would at least have been worth it, and she would gladly accept death.  
偷了就是偷了，没偷就是没偷。  
If she had done it, then she had done it; if she had not, then she had not.  
她没理由明明没偷，还硬说自己偷了。  
She had no reason to confess to something she had never done.  
姜后听得又气又想笑。  
Queen Jiang felt both angry and amused.  
她反问道：“没有？难道你还能自己生不成？”  
She snapped back, "No affair? Then what, did you manage to give birth all by yourself?"  
木桃忙说，这事说来话长。  
Mutao hurriedly said that it was a long story.  
随后，她开始讲起自己的往事。  
Then she began to tell the tale of her past.  

### 木桃的故事
### Mutao's Story

木桃的故事，要从四十年前说起。  
Mutao's story had to be traced back forty years.  
那时候宣王还只是太子。  
At that time King Xuan was still only crown prince.  
当时在位的周王，则是幽王的祖父厉王。  
The reigning Zhou king was King Li, the grandfather of King You.  
有一天，厉王闲着没事，在库房里转来转去。  
One day King Li had nothing to do and wandered around the royal storerooms.  
忽然，他看见一个罐子上积满了灰，连蜘蛛都在上面安家了。  
Suddenly he noticed a jar thick with dust, so neglected that spiders had made a home on it.  
他就问管库的官员，这东西是干什么用的。  
So he asked the storehouse official what the thing was for.  
库官赶忙回答，这看着像个普通罐子，其实一点也不普通。  
The official hurriedly answered that although it looked like an ordinary jar, it was anything but ordinary.  
这可是文物古迹。  
It was an ancient relic（古迹）.  
照他的说法，这罐子在那儿的时候，大王您都还没出生。  
According to him, the jar had already been there before the king himself was even born.  
厉王一听有点不高兴。  
King Li was not pleased to hear that.  
他问，难不成这还是商朝的东西？  
He asked whether it was supposed to date all the way back to the Shang.  
库官连忙说，何止商朝，这还是夏朝的。  
The official hurried to say that it went back not merely to the Shang, but to the Xia.  
他说，当年夏桀在位时，王宫里忽然来了两条龙。  
He said that in the days of King Jie of Xia, two dragons had suddenly appeared in the royal palace.  
夏桀吓坏了，不知该怎么办。  
King Jie was terrified and did not know what to do.  
太史便让他准备一个罐子，把龙口水接下来。  
So the court historian told him to prepare a jar and collect the dragons' saliva.  
于是，夏桀照办了。  
And King Jie did exactly that.  
两条龙流了些口水后就走了。  
The two dragons dripped some saliva and then left.  
这个罐子里装的，正是龙口水。  
What the jar contained was precisely dragon spit.  
从夏到商，再到周，一直没人敢打开。  
From Xia to Shang and then to Zhou, no one had ever dared open it.  
因为传说说得很吓人。  
That was because the legend attached to it was terrifying.  
谁打开，谁就亡国。  
Whoever opened it would lose the state.  
厉王当然不信。  
King Li, of course, did not believe a word of it.  
他摆摆手，说懒得去翻什么史书记载。  
He waved his hand and said he could not be bothered to look through old records.  
你不是说没人敢开吗？  
Wasn't that what they all said, that no one dared open it?  
那他偏偏就要开。  
Then he would be the one to open it.  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`宾语从句`、`条件状语从句`、`强调结构`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `What the jar contained was ...` 和 `Whoever opened it would lose the state`。<br>
难点拆解：`What the jar contained was ...` 是主语从句加表语；`Whoever opened it ...` 是条件意味很强的名词性从句；`Then he would be the one to open it` 用强调方式写出厉王偏要逆着来的态度。<br>
常用短语：
- `date back to`：追溯到
- `not believe a word of it`：一个字也不信
- `be bothered to do`：懒得去做
- `be the one to do`：偏偏就是那个要去做的人

库官只好赶紧把罐子擦干净。  
The official had no choice but to clean the jar at once.  
他把罐子放在盘子里，递给厉王。  
He set it on a tray and handed it to King Li.  
厉王假装去接，故意一松手。  
King Li pretended to take it and then deliberately let go.  
盘子摔在地上，罐子也跟着砸得粉碎。  
The tray crashed to the floor, and the jar shattered with it.  
里面立刻流出一大滩水。  
At once, a large pool of liquid spilled out.  
不用说，这就是龙口水。  
Needless to say, this was the dragons' saliva.  
而且量还真不少。  
And there was quite a lot of it.  
这也说明，罐子的密封居然真不错。  
Which also showed that the seal on the jar had been remarkably good.  
龙口水发黄发浑，臭得惊人。  
The dragon spit was yellowish, cloudy, and unbelievably foul-smelling.  
想想也是，放了七百多年，哪有不臭的。  
Then again, after sitting there for more than seven hundred years, how could it possibly not stink?  
厉王掩着鼻子，摆手让人把它擦掉。  
Covering his nose, King Li waved for people to wipe it away.  
可怪事又来了。  
But then something even stranger happened.  
那龙口水黏黏糊糊，怎么擦也擦不掉。  
The dragon spit was thick and sticky, and no one could wipe it off.  
厉王又把太史找来问办法。  
So King Li summoned the historian again and demanded a solution.  
太史说，龙属至阳，只能用至阴去克。  
The historian said that dragons belonged to the utmost yang and could only be countered by the utmost yin.  
他让大王赶紧找几个宫女来，光着屁股大喊大叫。  
He told the king to quickly bring in a group of palace maids and have them strip their bottoms bare and shout at the top of their lungs.  
这样一来，龙口水自然就没了。  
If they did that, the dragon spit would disappear on its own.  
厉王心想，别的不好说，宫女倒是有的是。  
King Li thought that if nothing else, there was certainly no shortage of palace maids.  
于是，他一口气找来了三十六个宫女。  
So he summoned thirty-six of them in one go.  
这些宫女全都脱得精光，把龙口水围了三圈。  
They stripped completely naked and surrounded the spill in three circles.  
接着便像啦啦队一样大呼小叫。  
Then they shrieked and shouted around it like a troupe of cheerleaders.  
说来也怪，那龙口水果然怕了她们。  
Strange as it sounds, the dragon spit really did seem to fear them.  
它突然化成一只乌龟，缩着脑袋就跑。  
It suddenly turned into a tortoise and fled with its head pulled in.  
三十六个光屁股宫女在后面追个不停。  
The thirty-six bare-bottomed maids chased after it without stopping.  
眼看就要追上时，前面撞见了一个七八岁的小宫女。  
Just as they were about to catch it, they came upon a little maid of seven or eight standing ahead.  
那乌龟一下蹿到小宫女身后，随即就不见了。  
The tortoise darted behind the child and vanished on the spot.  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`结果表达`、`方式状语`、`时间状语从句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `The tortoise darted ... and vanished`。<br>
难点拆解：前面用多个并列短句把动作一步步推进；`Just as they were about to catch it` 是时间状语从句，表示“眼看就要……”；`like a troupe of cheerleaders` 是方式状语，让画面更生动。<br>
常用短语：
- `have no choice but to do`：只好……
- `cover one's nose`：捂住鼻子
- `in one go`：一口气，一下子
- `vanish on the spot`：当场消失

### 妖女去了哪里
### Where Did the Demon Girl Go

木桃讲到这里，姜后已经有点听不下去了。  
By this point, Queen Jiang was already losing patience.  
她觉得这些乱七八糟的事，跟木桃怀孕生女儿有什么关系？  
She felt that all this bizarre nonsense had nothing to do with Mutao getting pregnant and giving birth.  
于是她不耐烦地问，讲完没有，龙口水跟生孩子到底有什么关系。  
So she asked irritably whether the story was over and what dragon spit had to do with childbirth in the first place.  
木桃连忙说，那个小宫女其实就是自己。  
Mutao hurriedly replied that the little palace maid had in fact been herself.  
她说，自从碰上那只乌龟，第二天肚子就鼓起来了。  
She said that from the day she encountered the tortoise, her belly had swollen the very next day.  
而且这一鼓，竟然就鼓了四十多年。  
And once it swelled, it went on swelling for more than forty years.  
直到今天，才终于生下来。  
Only today, she said, had it finally been delivered.  
照她这个说法，她生下来的不是龙女，至少也是龟女。  
By her account, what she had given birth to was not merely a dragon girl, but at the very least a tortoise girl.  
姜后听得脸都沉了。  
Queen Jiang's face darkened as she listened.  
她冷笑着说，这么说来，你生的还是个龙女了？  
With a sneer, she said that by this logic, Mutao had given birth to a dragon girl, had she?  
是不是连自己的王后位子都该让出来给她？  
Was she supposed to hand over her place as queen as well?  
木桃一看不妙，立刻改口。  
Seeing things turn dangerous, Mutao changed her wording at once.  
她说，不是不是，不是龙女，是妖女。  
No, no, she said, not a dragon girl, but a demon girl.  
姜后懒得再听她胡扯。  
Queen Jiang could not be bothered to listen to any more of it.  
她立刻下令，把那妖女抱出去扔进河里。  
She immediately ordered that the demon girl be carried out and thrown into the river.  
木桃哪里还敢多嘴。  
Mutao no longer dared say another word.  
她只能眼睁睁看着宫里人把女儿抢走。  
She could only watch helplessly as palace servants snatched her child away.  
孩子被草席一裹，就抱出宫外，扔进河里去了。  
Wrapped in a reed mat, the baby was carried outside the palace and thrown into the river.  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`宾语从句`、`结果表达`、`省略句式`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `She immediately ordered ...`。<br>
难点拆解：`She felt that ...` 和 `Mutao hurriedly replied that ...` 后面都是宾语从句；`Wrapped in a reed mat` 是省略式补充，等于“when she was wrapped ...”；整段用短句快速推进，突出姜后的决绝。<br>
常用短语：
- `lose patience`：失去耐心
- `in the first place`：说到底，归根结底
- `change one's wording`：改口
- `watch helplessly`：眼睁睁看着

### 妖女是谁
### Who Was the Demon Girl

故事讲到这里，褒妃自己反倒听入了神。  
By this point in the story, Consort Bao herself had become completely absorbed.  
她忍不住问，后来呢？  
She could not help asking what happened next.  
琼瑶小心翼翼地说，据说那个妖女并没有死，而是被人救了。  
Qiongyao answered cautiously that, according to rumor, the demon girl had not died but had instead been rescued.  
太史公还说过，这个女孩日后会断送大周的江山。  
The Grand Historian had also supposedly said that the girl would one day bring down the Zhou state.  
褒妃听到孩子没死，先松了一口气。  
At the news that the child had survived, Consort Bao first let out a quiet breath of relief.  
她又问，那她现在在哪儿？  
Then she asked where the girl was now.  
琼瑶不敢正眼看她，只接着往下说。  
Qiongyao did not dare look at her directly and simply went on speaking.  
她说，据说那妖女是被一家卖桑木弓的人救走的。  
She said that the demon girl had supposedly been saved by a family that sold mulberry-wood bows.  
后来，那孩子还去了褒城。  
Later, the child had ended up in Baocheng.  
若按年纪算起来，如今正差不多十五岁。  
If one calculated by age, the girl would now be around fifteen.  
说这话时，琼瑶还偷偷瞟了褒姒一眼。  
As she said this, Qiongyao cast Bao Si a furtive glance.  
褒妃听了，心里居然还有点替那女孩高兴。  
Hearing this, Consort Bao was oddly glad for the girl.  
那孩子总算没有死。  
At least the child had not died.  
更巧的是，她居然也在褒城。  
More curious still, she too had apparently lived in Baocheng.  
褒妃甚至想，自己会不会还见过她。  
Consort Bao even wondered whether she might once have met her.  
到了这一步，幽王已经完全明白琼瑶这故事到底想说什么。  
By this stage, King You fully understood what Qiongyao was driving at.  
他看着褒妃那副单纯善良的样子，只觉得这套说法简直荒唐。  
Looking at Consort Bao's innocent and gentle face, he felt the whole story was absurd.  
这样的人，怎么会断送大周江山？  
How could a woman like this possibly destroy the Zhou realm?  
于是，他故意问琼瑶，知不知道那个妖女叫什么、住在哪里。  
So he deliberately asked Qiongyao whether she knew the demon girl's name and where she lived.  
琼瑶先说，有句话她不敢讲。  
Qiongyao first said that there was one thing she hardly dared to say.  
幽王只回了她一个字：说。  
The king answered her with a single word: Speak.  
琼瑶只得硬着头皮往下讲。  
Qiongyao had no choice but to press on.  
她说，据她所知，褒妃正是褒城人。  
She said that, as far as she knew, Consort Bao was indeed from Baocheng.  
她的养父母并非亲生父母，而且正是卖桑木弓的人。  
Her foster parents were not her real parents, and they were precisely people who sold mulberry-wood bows.  
她的年龄，也和那妖女完全对得上。  
Her age also matched the demon girl exactly.  
琼瑶壮着胆子说，只怕褒妃就是那个妖女。  
Summoning her courage, Qiongyao said that Consort Bao was, most likely, that very demon girl.  
褒妃这才猛然明白过来。  
Only then did Consort Bao suddenly understand.  
原来申后和琼瑶根本不是来讲故事的。  
Queen Shen and Qiongyao had never come merely to tell a story.  
她们是来要她命的。  
They had come to take her life.  
至于琼瑶讲的那套，她自己当然不信。  
As for Qiongyao's tale, Consort Bao herself naturally did not believe it.  
可她信不信并不重要。  
But whether she believed it was beside the point.  
真正要紧的是，幽王信不信。  
What mattered was whether King You believed it.  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`宾语从句`、`强调句式`、`倒装`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `What mattered was whether King You believed it`。<br>
难点拆解：`What mattered was ...` 是主语从句加表语；`Only then did ... understand` 用了倒装结构，强调“直到这时才明白”；整段不断切换人物视角，用宾语从句承接“她说”“他觉得”“她明白了”。<br>
常用短语：
- `be absorbed in`：听得入神
- `be driving at`：真正想说的是
- `summon one's courage`：鼓起勇气
- `be beside the point`：不是重点

幽王显然不信。  
King You quite obviously did not believe her.  
他只问了一句，这故事是你编的吧？  
He merely asked whether she had made the whole thing up.  
琼瑶立刻发誓，说如果是编的，就叫天打五雷轰。  
Qiongyao swore at once that if she had invented it, she deserved to be struck by thunder from Heaven.  
幽王便说，那就把木桃找来对质。  
The king then said that Mutao should be brought in to confront her.  
可偏偏木桃已经死了。  
But Mutao happened to be dead already.  
不光木桃死了，所有知道这事的人都死了。  
Not only Mutao, but everyone else who knew of the matter had died as well.  
除了琼瑶，一个都不剩。  
No one was left except Qiongyao.  
这就叫死无对证。  
That, as the saying goes, is what it means for the witnesses to be dead.  
幽王的脸色于是越来越难看。  
King You's expression grew darker and darker.  
他知道，琼瑶就是冲着害褒妃来的。  
He knew that Qiongyao had come for the sake of harming Consort Bao.  
于是他大怒，下令把琼瑶拖出去喂狗。  
In a fury, he ordered that Qiongyao be dragged outside and fed to the dogs.  
申后一下就慌了，忙着替琼瑶求情。  
Queen Shen panicked at once and hastened to plead for Qiongyao.  
可在这种时候，她求情只会更糟。  
But at a moment like that, her plea only made things worse.  
因为在幽王眼里，她本来就是同案犯。  
In the king's eyes, she was already an accomplice（同案犯）.  
就连褒妃也开口替琼瑶求情。  
Even Consort Bao opened her mouth to plead for Qiongyao.  
她毕竟心软。  
She was softhearted, after all.  
可幽王反而更生气。  
But that only made King You angrier.  
他越觉得褒妃善良，就越觉得陷害她的人该死。  
The more he felt Bao Si was kind, the more he felt that those plotting against her deserved to die.  
到了这时候，谁都救不了琼瑶。  
By then, no one could save Qiongyao but herself.  
关键时刻，她想到了一个保命办法。  
At the crucial moment, she thought of one way to save her own life.  
那就是打赌。  
It was to make a wager.  
她对幽王说，褒妃已经怀了孕。  
She said to the king that Consort Bao was already pregnant.  
十月怀胎，一朝分娩。  
After ten months of pregnancy, there would one day be a birth.  
若生下来的是个正常孩子，她甘愿去喂狗。  
If the child was born normal, she would willingly go and be fed to the dogs.  
可要是真生出个妖怪，那就请大王还她一个公道。  
But if an actual monster were born, then she asked the king to clear her name.  
这提议，幽王还真不好拒绝。  
It was not an easy proposal for the king to refuse.  
若他拒绝，倒像是自己心虚。  
If he refused, it would make him look guilty or afraid.  
于是，琼瑶没有被杀。  
So Qiongyao was not killed.  
她只是被关了起来。  
She was merely locked up.  
申后也只好悻悻地回去了。  
And Queen Shen had no choice but to leave in frustration.  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`条件状语从句`、`被动语态`、`不定式表目的`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `If the child was born normal, she would ...` 和 `if an actual monster were born ...`。<br>
难点拆解：两个 `If ...` 都是条件状语从句；`be dragged outside and fed to the dogs` 是典型被动语态；`one way to save her own life` 里的不定式表示目的，“用来保命的办法”。<br>
常用短语：
- `confront someone`：当面对质
- `clear one's name`：洗清名声
- `make a wager`：打赌
- `leave in frustration`：悻悻离开

### 褒妃生了
### Consort Bao Gave Birth

褒妃的肚子一天比一天大。  
Consort Bao's belly grew larger day by day.  
终于有一天，太医告诉幽王，他们不能再做那种事了。  
At last, one day, the royal physician told King You that the two of them could no longer keep sleeping together.  
幽王说，他本来也早就没再做了。  
The king said he had already stopped anyway.  
但他还是坚持守在褒妃身边。  
But he still insisted on staying by Consort Bao's side.  
也许是他爱褒妃爱到能克制欲望的地步。  
Perhaps he loved Bao Si enough to restrain his desire.  
也许是他怕自己一不在，褒妃就会被人害死。  
Or perhaps he feared that the moment he was absent, someone would harm her.  
总之，即便褒妃已经无法再满足他，他还是不肯去找别的女人。  
In any case, even after Bao Si could no longer satisfy him physically, he still refused to go to other women.  
于是，整个后宫都弥漫着绝望。  
As a result, the whole harem was steeped in despair.  
直到有一天，宫里突然传出一声婴儿大哭。  
Then one day, a baby's wail suddenly rang through the palace.  
褒妃的孩子生下来了。  
Consort Bao's child had been born.  
所有人都盯着这个孩子。  
All eyes were fixed on the child.  
是男是女？  
Was it a boy or a girl?  
是人是妖？  
Was it human or monstrous?  
而褒妃自己多了一层关心。  
Consort Bao herself had one more concern than everyone else.  
她更想知道，这孩子是像褒子哥哥，还是像幽王。  
More than anything, she wanted to know whether the child looked like Brother Baozi or King You.  
结果出来了。  
The result soon became clear.  
是个男孩。  
It was a boy.  
而且是个人，不是妖。  
And he was human, not a demon.  
长相有点像幽王，也有点像褒妃。  
He looked a little like King You and a little like Consort Bao.  
换句话说，就是谁也不太像。  
In other words, he did not look very much like either of them.  
真要细说，其实更像褒子哥哥。  
If one were being precise, he actually looked more like Brother Baozi.  
幽王高兴坏了。  
King You was overjoyed.  
他又多了一个儿子。  
He had gained another son.  
申后却沮丧极了。  
Queen Shen, by contrast, was deeply dejected.  
她本以为，这下琼瑶总该被拖去喂狗了。  
She had assumed that Qiongyao would now finally be dragged off to feed the dogs.  
可这回，她又猜错了。  
But once again, she guessed wrong.  
褒妃对幽王说，把琼瑶放了吧。  
Consort Bao said to King You, "Let Qiongyao go."  
幽王问她，为什么。  
The king asked her why.  
褒妃说，生了孩子本是喜事，何必再杀人。  
Bao Si answered that the birth of a child was a happy event, so why spill blood on top of it.  
况且，琼瑶也是身不由己。  
Besides, Qiongyao too had not been entirely free to choose.  
在宫里混，谁都不容易。  
No one had an easy life in the palace.  
幽王最终放了琼瑶。  
In the end, King You released Qiongyao.  
整个后宫的人，也从此开始称赞褒妃。  
And from that point on, the people of the harem began to praise Consort Bao.  
连原本嫉妒她的人，也慢慢改了口。  
Even those who had once envied her started speaking of her differently.  
以德报怨，以柔克刚。  
She answered resentment with grace and hardness with softness.  
也许褒妃并不是故意这样做。  
Perhaps Consort Bao did not do this deliberately.  
但这偏偏成了她在后宫争斗中最厉害的武器。  
But it nonetheless became her strongest weapon in the struggles of the harem.  
而现在，也该说说幽王宠她的第二个原因了。  
And now it was time to explain the second reason why King You adored her so much.  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`条件状语从句`、`宾语从句`、`让步表达`。<br>
