句子主干：主句核心是 `even after ... he still refused ...` 和 `it nonetheless became her strongest weapon`。<br>
难点拆解：`even after Bao Si could no longer satisfy him physically` 带有让步意味；`she wanted to know whether ...` 里的 `whether ...` 是宾语从句；最后用 `nonetheless` 强调“偏偏、反而”。<br>
常用短语：
- `stay by someone's side`：守在某人身边
- `be steeped in`：笼罩在……之中
- `spill blood`：杀人流血
- `answer resentment with grace`：以德报怨

### 酷，一酷到底
### Cool, All the Way Through

什么样的女人，才算真正有持久魅力？  
What kind of woman possesses lasting charm?  
这个问题，几千年来一直困扰着无数女人。  
For thousands of years, that question has troubled countless women.  
尤其困扰美女。  
Beautiful women in particular have been haunted by it.  
大多数美女对男人当然有吸引力。  
Most beauties are, of course, attractive to men.  
可她们也常常发现，这种吸引力维持不了多久。  
But they often discover that such attraction does not last very long.  
过不了多久，男人就会腻。  
Before long, men grow bored.  
那为什么褒妃能成例外？  
So why was Consort Bao an exception?  
其实答案很简单。  
The answer, in fact, is simple.  
说到底，就是一个字：酷。  
Boiled down to its essence（本质）, it comes to a single word: cool.  
今天那些爱扮酷的人，要论祖师奶奶，褒妃算得上头一号。  
If one were naming the grand foremother of all those who like to act cool today, Consort Bao would rank first.  
褒妃为什么会“酷”？  
And why was she \"cool\"?  
原因并不神秘。  
The reason was not mysterious.  
她本来就不情愿进宫。  
She had never wanted to enter the palace in the first place.  
她心里装着的一直是褒子哥哥。  
The one she carried in her heart was always Brother Baozi.  
刚进宫时，她对陌生环境非常紧张。  
When she first entered the palace, the unfamiliar surroundings made her very tense.  
尤其是一想到要和幽王上床，她就更紧张。  
She was especially tense at the thought of sleeping with King You.  
后来，紧张慢慢淡了。  
Later, that tension gradually faded.  
可紧接着，她又发现了更要命的一件事。  
But then she discovered something even more deadly.  
她怀孕了。  
She was pregnant.  
而按日子一算，肚子里的孩子很可能不是幽王的。  
And when she counted the days, it seemed very likely that the child in her belly was not King You's.  
更可能，是褒子哥哥的。  
Much more likely, it was Brother Baozi's.  
若真要算概率，大概有八成是他的。  
If one insisted on speaking in probabilities, there was perhaps an eighty percent chance that it was his.  
再想想，幽王的生育能力好像也不算太强。  
And on top of that, King You did not seem especially fertile.  
近来被他临幸过的美女并不少。  
Quite a few beauties had recently shared his bed.  
可偏偏没有一个开花结果。  
Yet not one of them had borne fruit.  
所以，褒妃开始担心。  
So Consort Bao began to worry.  
她担心肚子里的孩子。  
She worried about the child inside her.  
也担心她自己。  
And she worried about herself.  
这还不算完。  
And that was still not all.  
她还得时时提防申后的暗算。  
She also had to stay constantly on guard against Queen Shen's plots.  
当这三件事同时压在身上时，她的心情可想而知。  
With all three pressures weighing on her at once, one can imagine her state of mind.  
她不是不想笑。  
It was not that she did not want to smile.  
从前采蘑菇的时候，她也是常笑的。  
Back when she gathered mushrooms, she used to smile often enough.  
只是现在，她实在笑不出来。  
It was simply that now she could not make herself smile.  
她并不是刻意装酷。  
She was not deliberately performing coolness.  
她只是被逼得不得不冷下来。  
She had simply been driven into a state of coldness.  
于是，她不笑。  
So she did not smile.  
她从来不笑。  
She never smiled.  
偏偏男人就是这样。  
And men, unfortunately, are often built that way.  
女人越讨好他们，他们越看不上。  
The more a woman tries to please them, the less they value her.  
反过来，女人越不搭理他们，他们越觉得稀罕。  
And the more indifferent a woman seems, the more irresistible they find her.  
天子无疑是天下最被讨好的男人。  
Without question, the Son of Heaven is the most fawned-over man under the sky.  
可一旦有个女人在他面前这么冷着，他反而傻眼了。  
But once a woman turned cold before him, he was the one left at a loss.  
他做惯了征服者。  
He had spent his life being the conqueror.  
所以他反倒会迷上那种“被征服”的感觉。  
That is why he ended up enchanted by the sensation of being conquered himself.  
于是，轮到他去讨好这个女人了。  
And so it became his turn to court the woman.  
褒妃正是这样的人。  
Consort Bao was exactly such a woman.  
而且她不是装出来的，她是真的冷。  
And what mattered was that she was not acting cool; she genuinely was cool.  
幽王从前见过的女人，全都冲他堆满笑。  
All the women King You had known before had smiled at him eagerly.  
像褒妃这样的人，他从来没见过。  
Someone like Consort Bao, he had never encountered.  
所以他彻底被她吸住了。  
So he was utterly captivated（迷住的） by her.  
再加上她的善良和直率，他就更脱不开身。  
Once her kindness and frankness were added to the mix, he had no hope of pulling free.  
若想抓住男人的心，对他冷一点。  
If you want to catch a man's heart, be a little cold toward him.  
女人们常说，别总给男人好脸色。  
Women often say you should not always show men your warmest face.  
别把这话当废话。  
Do not dismiss that as nonsense.  
这确实是真理。  
It is, in fact, a truth.  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`主语从句`、`条件状语从句`、`并列句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `What kind of woman possesses lasting charm?` 和 `If you want to catch a man's heart, be ...`。<br>
难点拆解：开头是典型的 `What kind of ...` 主语/疑问结构；`If you want to catch ...` 是条件状语从句；中间很多句子用并列和对照，把“越讨好越不值钱、越冷越被追”写得很鲜明。<br>
常用短语：
- `in the first place`：本来，一开始
- `weigh on someone`：压在某人心上
- `at a loss`：不知所措
- `pull free`：挣脱，脱身

幽王给这个小儿子起名叫伯服。  
King You gave this little son the name Bofu.  
他非常喜欢这个孩子。  
He was extremely fond of the boy.  
甚至愿意亲手给他端屎端尿。  
He would even clean up after him with his own hands.  
到这时，他才真正体会到做父亲的快乐。  
Only then did he truly experience the joy of fatherhood.  
可即便如此，褒妃还是那么冷。  
But even so, Consort Bao remained just as cool as ever.  
她还是不肯笑。  
She still would not smile.  
在幽王的记忆里，她似乎从来就没笑过。  
In the king's memory, it seemed she had never smiled at all.  
于是他问她，为什么从来不笑。  
So he asked her why she never smiled.  
难道是宫里的日子不如意？  
Was life in the palace making her unhappy?  
难道她心里有什么事？  
Was something troubling her?  
褒妃回答说，怎么会不如意呢。  
Consort Bao answered that of course her life should have seemed satisfactory.  
她说，大王宠爱她。  
She said that the king favored her deeply.  
她吃的是山珍海味，喝的是琼浆玉液。  
She ate delicacies and drank the finest wines.  
她听的是靡靡之音，看的是曼妙歌舞。  
She listened to soft music and watched elegant dances.  
她还有一个可爱的儿子。  
She even had a lovely son.  
照理说，她本该高兴才对。  
By all logic, she should have been happy.  
可她还是高兴不起来。  
Yet she still could not feel joy.  
幽王追问，为什么还是不高兴。  
The king pressed her and asked why she still could not be happy.  
褒妃这才把心里的话说出来。  
Only then did Consort Bao say what had truly been in her heart.  
她说，大王宠她，宫里那么多后妃都嫉妒她，恨不得她死，恨不得伯服死。  
She said that because the king loved her, so many women in the harem envied her and wished her dead, and wished Bofu dead as well.  
如今大王还在，她们母子还能活。  
As long as the king was alive, she and her son could continue to live.  
可若有一天大王不在了，她们母子又能依靠谁？  
But if one day the king were gone, on whom could mother and son rely?  
她说，到那时，自己或许一狠心就追随大王去了。  
She said that when that day came, she might harden her heart and follow the king into death.  
可伯服怎么办？  
But what then would happen to Bofu?  
宜臼会放过他吗？  
Would Yijiu spare him?  
申后会放过他吗？  
Would Queen Shen spare him?  
每每想到这些，她又怎么笑得出来。  
Whenever she thought of such things, how could she possibly smile?  
说到这里，褒妃哭了。  
By the time she reached this point, Consort Bao was crying.  
幽王搂着她和伯服，半天说不出话。  
The king held both her and Bofu and could not speak for a long while.  
这些事，他从前从来没想过。  
He had never thought of such things before.  
可既然褒妃说破了，他便猛然意识到，这确实是个问题。  
But once Bao Si had spoken it aloud, he suddenly realized that it truly was a problem.  
他知道，只要自己头天一死，第二天申后母子就能把褒妃母子剁成肉酱。  
He knew that if he died one day, Queen Shen and her son could have Bao Si and Bofu hacked to pieces the next.  
天子，原来也有自己的烦心事。  
Even the Son of Heaven, it turned out, had troubles of his own.  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`条件状语从句`、`宾语从句`、`反问句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `if one day the king were gone, on whom could mother and son rely?`。<br>
难点拆解：`if one day the king were gone` 是条件状语从句，语气带假设；`She said that ...` 后面一串内容都是宾语从句；后面的两个 `Would ... spare him?` 是反问推进，突出她的恐惧。<br>
常用短语：
- `be fond of`：喜爱
- `by all logic`：按理说
- `harden one's heart`：狠下心来
- `speak something aloud`：把话挑明说出来

### 褒妃成为褒后
### Consort Bao Becomes Queen Bao

幽王很快就下定了决心。  
King You made up his mind very quickly.  
他知道什么叫长痛不如短痛，也知道什么叫一劳永逸。  
He knew what it meant to choose a short sharp pain over a long lingering one, and he knew what it meant to settle something once and for all.  
于是，他召开了一次公卿会议。  
So he convened a meeting of high ministers and nobles.  
三公六卿加起来，共有九个人。  
The Three Dukes and Six Ministers together made up nine men in all.  
差不多就像一个最高决策核心。  
They were roughly equivalent to a top decision-making core.  
周朝中央政府里，最高的荣誉职位是三公。  
In the Zhou central government, the highest honorary posts were the Three Dukes.  
他们之下是六卿。  
Below them stood the Six Ministers.  
真正管事的，其实主要也是六卿。  
In practical terms, it was the Six Ministers who handled most affairs.  
会议一开，幽王开门见山。  
Once the meeting began, King You went straight to the point.  
他说，自己准备废掉申后，让大家说说意见。  
He said he intended to depose Queen Shen and wanted everyone to speak their minds.  
按周朝的气氛，这已经算很“民主”了。  
By Zhou standards, that already counted as rather \"democratic.\"  
所谓民主，不过就是天子先给结论。  
What \"democracy\" meant, however, was simply that the Son of Heaven stated the conclusion first.  
然后大家再想办法论证他是对的。  
Then everyone else found ways to prove he was right.  
众人心里都明白，今天这事的结果早就定了。  
Everyone understood inwardly that the outcome had already been decided before the meeting even began.  
有人装模作样地沉思。  
Some merely pretended to ponder the matter.  
因为他们其实反对，却又不敢说。  
They actually opposed it, but did not dare say so.  
照理说，申后并没有犯下什么原则性大错。  
Strictly speaking, Queen Shen had committed no fundamental offense.  
废她，显然并不合理。  
Deposing her was clearly not reasonable.  
可这种场合，总得有人先站出来。  
But in such a gathering, someone always has to step out first.  
于是虢石父站出来了。  
So Guo Shifu stepped forward.  
他说，大王英明，真是再英明不过。  
He declared that the king was wise, wise beyond measure.  
申后要德没德，要能没能，废掉她正是天下官民的共同心声。  
Queen Shen, he said, had neither virtue nor ability, and removing her was the common wish of officials and commoners alike.  
虢石父一开头，其他人也纷纷表态。  
Once Guo Shifu opened the way, the others quickly voiced their agreement as well.  
就这样，废后提案几乎算是全票通过。  
Just like that, the proposal to depose the queen was passed all but unanimously.  
幽王接着问，那谁适合做新王后。  
King You then asked who should take the queen's place.  
这一回，虢石父没有抢着发言。  
This time Guo Shifu did not rush to speak first.  
太祝尹球站出来说，天下人都知道褒妃有才有德，早就该当王后了。  
Instead, Grand Invocator Yin Qiu stood up and said that the whole world knew Consort Bao possessed both talent and virtue and ought long ago to have been made queen.  
他甚至还激动地表示，大王若不立褒妃为后，自己第一个不干。  
He even declared with passion that if the king refused to make Consort Bao queen, he would be the first to resign.  
尹球说得慷慨激昂。  
Yin Qiu spoke with stirring fervor.  
仿佛不是幽王逼着大家表态，而是大家逼着幽王点头。  
It sounded almost as if the ministers were forcing the king's hand, not the other way around.  
幽王又问其他人怎么看。  
The king asked the others for their views as well.  
众人齐声附和，说尹球说得对。  
They all chimed in and said Yin Qiu was right.  
幽王听完，只是笑。  
King You merely smiled when he heard them.  
他知道，这帮人倒是识相。  
He knew that this crowd at least understood how to read the room.  
按照《周礼》，太子必须是嫡长子。  
According to the *Rites of Zhou*, the crown prince had to be the eldest legitimate son.  
换句话说，必须是王后的第一个儿子。  
In other words, he had to be the first son born to the queen.  
这后来也被概括成一句话，叫“子以母贵”。  
Later generations summarized that principle in a simple phrase: the son is exalted through the status of his mother.  
既然王后的事已经办妥了，接下来也就顺理成章。  
Once the question of the queen had been settled, the next step followed naturally.  
太子宜臼，被废了。  
Crown Prince Yijiu was deposed.  
这就是褒姒改变历史的开始吗？  
Was this the beginning of Bao Si changing history?  
别急。  
Not so fast.  
这还只是开始。  
This was only the beginning.  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`宾语从句`、`同位语解释`、`被动表达`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `He said he intended to depose Queen Shen` 和 `Crown Prince Yijiu was deposed`。<br>
难点拆解：`he intended to depose ...` 属于宾语从句转述；`What "democracy" meant ...` 是同位语式解释；最后 `was deposed` 用被动表达政治结果，语气更客观有力。<br>
常用短语：
- `go straight to the point`：开门见山
- `read the room`：看懂场面，看懂风向
- `all but unanimously`：几乎全票
- `follow naturally`：顺理成章地发生

## 第四章 烽火戏诸侯
## Chapter 4: Fooling the Lords with Beacon Fires

### 美人还是不笑
### The Beauty Still Would Not Smile

伯服五岁那年，幽王送了他一份大礼。  
When Bofu turned five, King You gave him a grand gift.  
这份礼，就是太子之位。  
That gift was the title of crown prince（太子）.  
褒妃也同时得了一份大礼。  
At the same time, Consort Bao received a grand gift of her own.  
她被立为王后。  
She was made queen.  
从采蘑菇的小姑娘，到坐上后位。  
From a little girl who once picked mushrooms to the queen on the throne.  
褒妃这一路，走得已经很远。  
Bao Si had already come a very long way.  
“亲爱的，这下总该放心了吧？”  
"My dear, you can finally rest easy now, can you not?"  
幽王觉得，自己总算了了一桩心事。  
King You felt he had finally settled one heavy worry.  
“放心了。”  
"I am at ease."  
褒后这样回答。  
That was Queen Bao's reply.  
可她还是没笑。  
But she still did not smile.  
褒妃不笑，褒后也还是不笑。  
As a consort she did not smile, and as a queen she still would not smile.  
幽王忍不住问，她为什么还是笑不出来。  
King You could not help asking why she still could not smile.  
褒后只说，大概这一切都在意料之中。  
Queen Bao only said that perhaps all of this had already been within her expectation（意料）.  
幽王听完，难免失望。  
King You could not help feeling disappointed（失望的）.  
连这种事都不能让她笑。  
If even this could not make her smile,  
那还有什么能呢？  
then what in the world could?

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`条件状语从句`、`宾语从句`、`反问句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `If even this could not make her smile, then what in the world could?`。<br>
难点拆解：这里先用 `If even this could not ...` 作条件状语从句，后面主句省略重复成分，只保留 `what ... could`，形成很有力度的反问；`why she still could not smile` 则是宾语从句。<br>
常用短语：
- `rest easy`：放下心来
- `could not help doing`：忍不住做……
- `in the world`：到底，究竟
- `within one's expectation`：在某人的意料之中

其实她不是不想笑。  
It was not that she refused to smile.  
她是真的笑不出来。  
She genuinely（真正地） could not.  
伯服越长大，越不像她，也不像幽王。  
The older Bofu grew, the less he resembled（像） either her or King You.  
他反而越来越像褒子哥哥。  
Instead, he looked more and more like Brother Baozi.  
她一边怕秘密败露。  
On one hand, she feared the secret might come to light（败露）.  
一边又嫌幽王老了。  
On the other hand, she could not help feeling that King You had grown old.  
五十多岁的人，做什么都力不从心。  
A man in his fifties no longer had the strength for everything.  
所以她越发想念褒子哥哥。  
So she missed Brother Baozi all the more.  

褒后越冷，幽王反倒越着迷。  
The colder Queen Bao remained, the more infatuated（痴迷的） King You became.  
他干脆把人生目标，定成了“博她一笑”。  
He practically made winning one smile from her the goal of his life.  
别人也许会笑他无聊。  
Other people might call that ridiculous（可笑的）.  
可幽王其实很苦闷。  
But King You was, in truth, deeply miserable（苦闷的）.  
他从来不知道，让别人快乐，本身也是一种快乐。  
He had never known that giving joy to another person could itself be a kind of joy.  
褒后的冷淡，偏偏给了他这样一个机会。  
And Queen Bao's coldness happened to offer him exactly such a chance.  
要是他爱百姓，也能像爱褒后一样。  
If he had loved his people the way he loved Queen Bao,  
他大概真能做个伟大的天子。  
he might really have become a great Son of Heaven.

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`条件状语从句`、`宾语从句`、`比较结构`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `he might really have become a great Son of Heaven`。<br>
难点拆解：`If he had loved ...` 是与过去事实相反的条件状语从句；`that giving joy ... could itself be ...` 是宾语从句，其中 `giving joy to another person` 又是动名词短语作从句主语；`the way he loved Queen Bao` 表示比较方式。<br>
常用短语：
- `make ... the goal of one's life`：把……当成人生目标
- `in truth`：实际上
- `happen to do`：碰巧……
- `the way ...`：像……那样

一天夜里，褒后在梦里喊“包子，包子”。  
One night, Queen Bao cried out in her sleep, "Baozi, Baozi."  
幽王一下惊醒。  
King You woke with a start.  
他以为她是想吃包子了。  
He assumed that she was craving steamed buns（包子）.  
于是天还没亮，他就悄悄起身。  
So before dawn, he quietly got out of bed.  
他想给她一个惊喜。  
He wanted to surprise her.  
等褒后醒来，还没睁眼就闻到香味。  
When Queen Bao woke, she smelled something fragrant（香的） before she had even opened her eyes.  
她睁眼一看，整个人都愣住了。  
The moment she looked, she froze in astonishment（震惊）.  
床边居然摆着十几笼包子。  
More than a dozen steaming baskets of buns were sitting right by the bed.  
猪肉的，茴香的，蘑菇的，样样都有。  
Pork, fennel（茴香）, mushroom, every kind was there.  
幽王还一脸得意地看着她。  
And King You stood there looking pleased with himself.  
褒后问，把包子放这儿干什么。  
Queen Bao asked what all those buns were doing there.  
幽王说，你做梦一直喊包子，我就知道你想吃。  
King You said, "You kept calling for baozi in your dream, so I knew you wanted to eat them."  
这回褒后倒是笑了。  
This time Queen Bao did smile.  
只是那笑，是苦笑。  
But it was a bitter smile.  
她梦里的“包子”，不是吃的包子。  
The "Baozi" in her dream was not something to eat.  
她想的，是褒子哥哥。  
The person on her mind was Brother Baozi.  
眼前这些热气腾腾的包子，只会让她更伤心。  
Those steaming buns in front of her only deepened her sorrow（悲伤）.

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`时间状语从句`、`宾语从句`、`强调结构`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `Those steaming buns in front of her only deepened her sorrow`。<br>
难点拆解：`When Queen Bao woke ...` 是时间状语从句；`He assumed that ...` 和 `I knew you wanted ...` 都是宾语从句；`did smile` 里的 `did` 是强调，用来突出“这次她确实笑了”。<br>
常用短语：
- `wake with a start`：猛地惊醒
- `before dawn`：天亮前
- `the moment ...`：一……就……
- `on one's mind`：在某人心上，一直惦记

### 千金买笑
### A Thousand Pieces of Gold for a Smile

幽王并不泄气。  
King You did not lose heart.  
他认定，世上无难事，只怕有心人。  
He believed that nothing was impossible if a person truly set his mind to it.  
没过多久，有宫女来报。  
Not long afterward, a palace maid came to report something.  
两年前，她曾不小心扯裂过一件绸衣。  
Two years earlier, she said, she had accidentally torn a silk robe（绸衣）.  
那一声很特别。  
The ripping sound had been oddly distinctive（特别的）.  
当时褒后听见，居然笑过。  
And when Queen Bao heard it back then, she had actually laughed.  
幽王大喜。  
King You was delighted（大喜的）.  
他立刻命人搬来成捆绸缎。  
He immediately ordered bundles of silk to be brought in.  
又让几个力气大的宫女，当着褒后的面狠狠干撕。  
Then he had several strong palace maids tear them apart in front of Queen Bao.  
一捆一捆撕得粉碎。  
Bundle after bundle was ripped to shreds（碎片）.  
褒后还是不笑。  
Queen Bao still did not smile.  
后来她不但不笑，听见那声音还直皱眉。  
Later she not only refused to laugh, but even frowned whenever she heard that sound.  
《史记》里说，幽王为了让褒姒笑，想尽了办法。  
The *Records of the Grand Historian* says that King You exhausted every device（办法） to make Bao Si smile.  
可她就是不笑。  
And yet she simply would not.  
有钱，有权，还肯费心哄妻子。  
He had money, power, and the willingness to spend effort pleasing his wife.  
照今天看，这都算难得了。  
By today's standards, that alone would already seem rare.  
可幽王到了这一步，还是没招了。  
But even after all that, King You had run out of ideas.  
于是他只好祭出最后一招。  
So he had no choice but to use his final move.

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`时间状语从句`、`宾语从句`、`并列句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `The Records of the Grand Historian says that King You exhausted every device to make Bao Si smile`。<br>
难点拆解：`when Queen Bao heard it ...` 是时间状语从句；`says that ...` 后面是宾语从句；`He had money, power, and ...` 用并列成分把人物条件一口气铺开，读起来更有节奏。<br>
常用短语：
- `lose heart`：泄气
- `set one's mind to`：下定决心做……
- `tear ... apart`：把……撕开
- `run out of ideas`：没招了

幽王决定走群众路线。  
King You decided to appeal to the wisdom of the masses.  
说白了，就是悬赏。  
Put plainly, he was offering a reward.  
他下令宫里宫外都知道。  
He made sure the order was known both inside and outside the palace.  
谁能让褒后笑，就赏千金。  
Whoever could make Queen Bao smile would be rewarded with a thousand pieces of gold.  
群众路线一向好用。  
The "mass line" had always been effective（有效的）.  
果然，有人献上一条妙计。  
Sure enough, someone offered a clever scheme（计策）.  
这个人，就是虢石父。  
That man was Guo Shifu.  
他说自己有办法。  
He said he had a plan.  
但这办法要兴师动众。  
But the plan would require a major mobilization（兴师动众）.  
值不值得，还得看大王舍不舍得。  
Whether it was worth it depended on how far the king was willing to go.  
幽王回答得很干脆。  
King You answered without hesitation（犹豫）.  
只要能让褒后一笑。  
If it could win even one smile from Queen Bao,  
别说兴师动众。  
then let alone mobilizing a crowd,  
就算送出半壁江山，他也愿意。  
he said he would even give away half his realm（江山）.  
虢石父听完，也觉得他真是情种。  
After hearing that, even Guo Shifu had to admit that the king was hopelessly lovestruck（为情所困的）.

虢石父说，当年为了防西戎。  
Guo Shifu explained that in earlier days, in order to guard against western tribes,  
先王在骊山一线修了烽火台。  
the former kings had built beacon towers（烽火台） along the line of Mount Li.  
每隔二十里一座，一路连到诸侯国。  
There was one every twenty li, and they stretched all the way to the feudal states.  
一旦有敌人来犯，就立刻点火。  
The moment invaders attacked, the fires would be lit at once.  
诸侯见了烽火，就会连夜起兵来救。  
When the lords saw the beacons, they would march through the night to rescue the king.  
如今太平无事，烽火早就闲着了。  
Now all was peaceful, and the beacons had long gone unused.  
不如带褒后去骊山游玩。  
Why not take Queen Bao to Mount Li for an outing?  
夜里把烽火点起来。  
At night, light the beacons.  
诸侯一定会急匆匆赶来。  
The lords would surely rush over in alarm（惊慌）.  
等他们到了，却发现根本没敌人。  
When they arrived, however, they would find that there was no enemy at all.  
一个个全都会傻眼。  
Each of them would be left staring in bewilderment（发愣）.  
褒后见了，说不定就笑了。  
At the sight of that, Queen Bao might finally laugh.  
虢石父自己说着，都忍不住先笑了。  
Guo Shifu could hardly stop himself from laughing first as he described it.  
幽王一听，连声说好。  
The moment King You heard it, he said it was excellent again and again.  
他当场先赏五百金。  
He awarded Guo Shifu five hundred pieces of gold on the spot.  
还约好事成之后，再补另外五百。  
He also promised the other five hundred after the plan succeeded.

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`条件状语从句`、`时间状语从句`、`宾语从句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `he said he would even give away half his realm` 和 `When they arrived, however, they would find that there was no enemy at all`。<br>
难点拆解：`If it could win ...` 是条件状语从句；`When they arrived ...` 是时间状语从句；`he said ...`、`they would find that ...` 都带宾语从句。前一句体现幽王的夸张表态，后一句则把骗局的画面完整展开。<br>
常用短语：
- `on the spot`：当场
- `in order to`：为了
- `at the sight of`：一看到……
- `could hardly stop oneself from doing`：几乎忍不住做……

### 烽火戏诸侯
### Beacon Fires to Toy with the Lords

三天后，月朗星稀，天气正好。  
Three days later, the moon shone bright, the stars were sparse（稀疏的）, and the weather was perfect.  
虢石父特地挑了这么一个夜晚。  
Guo Shifu had chosen just such a night on purpose.  
他就是要确保烽火能传得出去。  
He wanted to make sure the signal would carry clearly.  
一切准备停当后，幽王带着褒后出了镐京。  
Once everything was ready, King You left Haojing with Queen Bao.  
两人一路去了骊山。  
Together they made their way to Mount Li.  
山上有骊宫。  
There was a palace retreat（行宫） on the mountain.  
虢石父早已备好宴席。  
Guo Shifu had already prepared a banquet（宴席）.  
还有胡姬歌舞助兴。  
There were also foreign dancers to liven things up.  
等天一黑，山上这边开宴。  
As soon as night fell, the banquet began on the mountain.  
山下那边点起烽火。  
And below, the beacon fires were lit.  
火光冲天。  
The flames shot high into the sky.  
别说二十里。  
Forget twenty li.  
就是五十里外，也看得清清楚楚。  
Even from fifty li away, they could be seen with complete clarity（清楚）.  
于是各处烽火台相继举火。  
So the beacon towers along the route lit up one after another.  
消息像接力一样传了出去。  
The signal raced outward like a relay（接力）.  
很快就传到了四面诸侯那里。  
Before long, it had reached the feudal lords in every direction.  
诸侯一见，都以为京城出了大事。  
The lords all assumed that some grave crisis（严重危机） had struck the capital.  
谁还顾得上吃饭睡觉。  
Who could still bother with eating or sleeping?  
有人扔下饭碗就走。  
Some threw down their bowls and left at once.  
有人提着裤子就上马。  
Some barely had time to pull up their trousers before jumping on horseback.  
有的国君亲自带队。  
Some rulers led the troops in person.  
有的派出本国最精锐的战车。  
Others dispatched（派出） the finest chariots in their states.  
所有人都朝一个方向狂奔。  
Everyone raced toward the same destination（目的地）.  
那就是镐京。  
That destination was Haojing.  
近三千年前，没有柏油路。  
Nearly three thousand years ago, there were no paved roads.  
也没有轮胎和舒服的车。  
There were no rubber tires or comfortable vehicles either.  
从各地一路赶来，绝不轻松。  
To rush in from all directions was no easy undertaking（辛苦差事）.  
可他们还是连夜赶路。  
And yet they still pressed on through the night.  
因为他们心里都认准了一件事。  
For each of them held fast to one conviction（信念）.  
那就是救天子，保京城。  
They had to save the Son of Heaven and defend the capital.  
一时间烟尘四起，车马喧腾。  
Dust rose everywhere, and chariots and horses thundered noisily.  
旌旗乱动，人马嘶鸣。  
Banners whipped in the air while men shouted and horses screamed.  
军队一动，后勤也跟着动。  
Once the armies moved, logistics（后勤） moved with them.  
多少人一夜难眠，这里就不细说了。  
How many people spent that night awake with worry need not be described in detail.  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`时间状语从句`、`宾语从句`、`并列句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `The lords all assumed that some grave crisis had struck the capital`。<br>
难点拆解：`As soon as night fell ...` 和 `Once the armies moved ...` 都是时间状语从句；`assumed that ...` 是宾语从句；后面多组并列短句连续推进，营造出诸侯星夜来援的紧迫感。<br>
常用短语：
- `as soon as`：一……就……
- `one after another`：一个接一个
- `hold fast to`：坚守，认定
- `need not be described in detail`：不必细说

天快亮时，秦军最先赶到。  
By dawn, the Qin army was the first to arrive.  
他们先到了镐京，却没见敌人。  
They reached Haojing first, yet found no enemy there.  
听说幽王在骊山，又立刻转向骊山。  
When they heard that King You was at Mount Li, they turned there at once.  
骊山脚下，天色正亮。  
At the foot of Mount Li, the sky was just brightening.  
山上烽火还在烧。  
The beacon fires were still burning above.  
可敌人在哪儿呢？  
But where was the enemy?  
秦军正疑惑，郑军也到了。  
As the Qin troops stood puzzled（疑惑的）, the Zheng army arrived as well.  
双方你看我，我看你。  
They looked at one another in confusion（困惑）.  
谁也说不清出了什么事。  
No one could explain what had happened.  
没多久，晋军也赶到了。  
Not long afterward, the Jin troops arrived too.  
三路人马聚在山下，乱成一团。  
Three armies gathered below the mountain, and the whole place became chaotic（混乱的）.  
就在这时，山上传来锣鼓声。  
At that moment, drums and gongs rang out from above.  
火光里走出一个人。  
A figure stepped out in the firelight.  
不是别人，正是虢石父。  
It was none other than Guo Shifu.  
他冲着山下大喊。  
He shouted down toward the men below.  
天子没事，大家回去吧。  
"The Son of Heaven is perfectly fine. You may all go home."  
今晚点烽火，只是闹着玩。  
"The beacons were lit tonight only for amusement（取乐）."  
山下诸侯一听，全都傻了。  
At those words, the lords below were left speechless（说不出话的）.  
他们辛苦跑了一夜。  
They had raced through the whole night in exhaustion（疲惫）.  
结果却被人当猴耍。  
And in the end, they had been made into the butt of a cruel joke.  
众人又气又羞，骂骂咧咧地撤了。  
Angry and humiliated（羞辱的）, they withdrew while cursing under their breath.  
褒后看到这一幕，终于笑了。  
At the sight of it, Queen Bao finally laughed.  
而且不止笑了一回。  
And she laughed more than once.  
因为后面还有十几路诸侯陆续赶来。  
For after that, more than a dozen other lords kept arriving one after another.  
幽王高兴坏了。  
King You was overjoyed.  
他立刻把剩下那五百金也赏了。  
He immediately paid out the remaining five hundred pieces of gold.  
“千金买笑”这个成语，也就这么来了。  
That is how the saying "a thousand pieces of gold for a smile" came into being.  
只是从那以后，幽王越来越常点烽火。  
But from that day on, King You lit the beacons more and more often.  
赶来救援的诸侯却越来越少。  
Yet fewer and fewer lords came to the rescue.  
慢慢地，谁也不肯再信了。  
Little by little, no one was willing to believe him any longer.

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`时间状语从句`、`被动表达`、`并列谓语`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `At those words, the lords below were left speechless` 和 `Little by little, no one was willing to believe him any longer`。<br>
难点拆解：`When they heard ...`、`At the sight of it ...` 体现时间和触发关系；`were left speechless`、`had been made into ...` 都是被动表达，突出诸侯的受骗处境；后面连续动作用并列谓语推进故事结果。<br>
常用短语：
- `none other than`：不是别人，正是……
- `under one's breath`：低声地，嘴里嘟囔着
- `come into being`：形成，产生
- `from that day on`：从那天起

### 申侯妙计
### Marquis Shen's Clever Plan

俗话说，斩草不除根，春风吹又生。  
As the saying goes, if you do not pull weeds out by the root, they will grow again with the spring wind.  
转眼到了幽王十一年。  
In the blink of an eye, it was the eleventh year of King You's reign.  
也就是公元前771年。  
That was 771 BCE.  
太子宜臼被废以后。  
After Crown Prince Yijiu had been deposed,  
他就一直住在外祖父申侯那里。  
he had been living with his maternal grandfather, the Marquis of Shen.  
一晃就是八年。  
And in a flash, eight years had passed.  
宜臼郁闷。  
Yijiu was miserable（郁闷的）.  
申侯更郁闷。  
Marquis Shen was even more miserable.  
自己的女儿失了后位。  
His daughter had lost her place as queen.  
外孙也成了废太子。  
His grandson had become a deposed crown prince.  
原本两只潜力股。  
They had once looked like two promising prospects（有前途的人）.  
如今全都砸在手里。  
Now both had gone to ruin in his hands.  
可再郁闷，也不能硬拼。  
But no matter how bitter he felt, he could not fight head-on.  
胳膊毕竟拧不过大腿。  
After all, an arm cannot twist harder than a thigh.  
所以他一直忍着。  
So he kept enduring it.  
终于，机会来了。  
At last, an opportunity（机会） appeared.  
为什么说机会来了？  
Why call it an opportunity?  
一来，幽王老拿烽火戏弄诸侯。  
First, King You had long been toying with the lords by means of false beacon fires.  
大家都已经烦透了他。  
Everyone was already thoroughly fed up with him.  
二来，犬戎那边派人来了。  
Second, envoys（使者） had come from the Quanrong.  
说想和周朝做点交易。  
They said they wished to do some trade with Zhou.  
犬戎住在周朝西边。  
The Quanrong lived to the west of Zhou.  
他们是游牧部族。  
They were a nomadic（游牧的） people.  
自己东西不多。  
They had few goods of their own.  
却很眼馋周朝的物资。  
But they greatly coveted（觊觎） Zhou's supplies.  
平时想换，周朝又不肯多理他们。  
Ordinarily they wanted to barter（以物易物）, yet Zhou was unwilling to deal much with them.  
换不到怎么办？  
And what if barter failed?  
那就抢。  
Then they robbed.  
他们骑术好，来去也快。  
They rode well and struck fast.  
只是兵力和战法都不算强。  
Only their numbers and tactics（战法） were not especially strong.  
所以平时也只敢边境骚扰。  
So they usually limited themselves to border raids（袭扰）.  
申国虽然不和犬戎接壤。  
Although Shen did not directly border the Quanrong,  
却多少和他们有些来往。  
it still had some dealings with them.  
申侯便想出一个自以为万无一失的计划。  
So Marquis Shen came up with what he thought was a foolproof（万无一失的） plan.  
他要联手犬戎，进攻镐京。  
He would join hands with the Quanrong and attack Haojing.  
为什么非得拉犬戎入伙？  
Why did he have to pull the Quanrong into it?  
因为周朝诸侯没人会去打都城。  
Because no Zhou lord would willingly attack the capital.  
那双方各得什么好处？  
Then what would each side gain?  
犬戎可以尽情抢劫。  
The Quanrong could plunder（抢劫） as much as they pleased.  
申侯则能借他们的手。  
Marquis Shen, meanwhile, could use their hands  
除掉褒后母子和虢石父这些人。  
to eliminate（除掉） Queen Bao, her son, and men like Guo Shifu.  
这样一来，宜臼就能再做太子。  
Then Yijiu could become crown prince again.  
申后也能重回后位。  
And Queen Shen could return to her old place.  
风险呢？  
What about the risks?  
申侯几乎没想。  
Marquis Shen scarcely thought about them.  
可他偏偏漏算了一件事。  
But he overlooked one crucial（关键的） thing.  
犬戎的信用，很差。  
The Quanrong had very little reliability（可信度）.  
他们常常不按约定办事。  
They often failed to act according to agreement.  
所以无论做什么。  
That is why, in any undertaking（事情）,  
挑合作伙伴都极重要。  
choosing your partner matters enormously.

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`让步状语从句`、`宾语从句`、`主语从句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `So Marquis Shen came up with what he thought was a foolproof plan`。<br>
难点拆解：`Although Shen did not ...` 是让步状语从句；`what he thought was a foolproof plan` 是主语从句式的名词性结构作 `came up with` 的宾语；后面多处问答短句把申侯的算盘拆得很清楚。<br>
常用短语：
- `in the blink of an eye`：一转眼
- `be fed up with`：烦透了……
- `join hands with`：与……联手
- `act according to agreement`：按约定办事

申侯派使者去见犬戎国主。  
Marquis Shen sent envoys to the Quanrong ruler.  
把自己的计划捎了过去。  
They conveyed his plan.  
当然，礼物也没少带。  
Naturally, they took gifts as well.  
犬戎国主一听就乐了。  
The Quanrong ruler was delighted the moment he heard it.  
这就像富人家的儿子，请强盗一起去抢自家。  
It was like the son of a rich household inviting bandits（强盗） to help rob his own home.  
哪有不答应的道理。  
How could they possibly refuse?  
双方约定了时间。  
The two sides agreed on a time.  
犬戎国主亲自带一万五千精兵出动。  
The Quanrong ruler personally led fifteen thousand seasoned（精锐的） fighters.  
申侯也带着一百乘战车前去会合。  
Marquis Shen likewise set out with one hundred chariots to meet them.  
中国历史上第一次外族大举入侵。  
In the author's telling, this was the first large-scale foreign invasion（入侵） in Chinese history.  
而申侯，也成了第一个“周奸”。  
And Marquis Shen, by the same logic, became the first traitor（叛徒） to Zhou.

### 引狼入室
### Letting the Wolf Inside

申戎联军很快逼近镐京。  
The Shen-Quanrong allied force soon closed in on Haojing.  
哨探急报幽王。  
Scouts rushed the news to King You.  
幽王大惊。  
King You was aghast（惊骇的）.  
他立刻召集紧急会议。  
He immediately called an emergency council（紧急会议）.  
可到了这时候，周朝已经很多年没打过像样的仗了。  
But by this point, Zhou had not fought a serious war for many years.  
鬼子进村，内奸带路。  
Now foreign raiders were at the gate, with an insider leading them in.  
还能怎么办？  
What else was there to do?  
虢石父只有一个主意。  
Guo Shifu had only one idea.  
点烽火。  
Light the beacons.  
快马奔上骊山，烽火立刻点起。  
Fast riders raced to Mount Li, and the beacon fires were lit at once.  
那天风和日丽。  
The weather that day was bright and mild.  
西风又不大不小。  
A light west wind blew at just the right strength.  
狼烟一起，百里之外都能看见。  
Once the smoke rose, it could be seen a hundred li away.  
申戎联军却没有马上攻城。  
Yet the allied army did not attack immediately.  
他们也在等。  
They were waiting too.  
他们怕一旦打进城。  
They feared that if they stormed the city at once,  
诸侯援军一到，自己就会被反包围。  
they would be surrounded in turn when the lords' reinforcements arrived.  
所以他们只是围城不打。  
So they only encircled the city without attacking.  
万一援军到了，他们还能拔腿就跑。  
If reinforcements came, they could still turn and run.  
三天过去了。  
Three days passed.  
诸侯一兵未发。  
Not a single lord sent a single soldier.  
申侯见状，笑了。  
Seeing this, Marquis Shen laughed.  
幽王以前玩得太过。  
King You had gone too far with his old tricks.  
这回终于没人信他了。  
Now at last, no one believed him.

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`宾语从句`、`条件状语从句`、`时间状语从句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `They feared that ... they would be surrounded in turn`。<br>
难点拆解：`that if they stormed ...` 是宾语从句里套条件状语从句；`when the lords' reinforcements arrived` 再嵌入时间关系；这种层层嵌套正好说明联军为什么围而不打。<br>
常用短语：
- `at the gate`：兵临城下
- `at once`：立刻
- `in turn`：反过来，转而
- `go too far`：做得太过

城里一下乱了套。  
Inside the city, everything fell into panic（恐慌）.  
救兵不来，谁去迎战？  
With no relief force arriving, who was to meet the enemy?  
大家都把目光投向虢石父。  
Everyone turned their eyes to Guo Shifu.  
当初拿烽火当玩笑，是你出的主意。  
It had been your idea to turn the beacon fires into a joke.  
如今诸侯不来了。  
Now the lords were not coming.  
这残局，也得你收。  
So you had to clean up this mess.  
虢石父没办法。  
Guo Shifu had no choice.  
只好硬着头皮，带一百乘战车出城。  
He could only steel himself（硬着头皮） and lead a hundred chariots out of the city.  
可他根本不会打仗。  
But he had no idea how to wage war.  
上了战场，一个回合都没撑住。  
Once he reached the battlefield, he did not last even a single round.  
脑袋都不知道飞到哪里去了。  
His head was cut off before anyone even knew what had happened.  
那一百多乘战车，也全军覆没。  
Those hundred-odd chariots were annihilated（全灭的） as well.  
城头上的幽王还在喊“老虢，老虢”。  
From the city wall, King You kept calling, "Old Guo! Old Guo!"  
虢石父勉强睁开眼。  
Guo Shifu barely opened his eyes.  
他朝城头望了一眼。  
He cast one glance toward the wall.  
笑了笑。  
He smiled faintly.  
然后就死了。  
Then he died.

### 幽王死了
### The Death of King You

城外的号角吹响。  
The war horns sounded outside the city.  
犬戎军正式攻城。  
The Quanrong army launched its assault（进攻）.  
申侯这时还在上厕所。  
At that very moment, Marquis Shen was still relieving himself.  
等他站起来时，城头已经插上了犬戎的旗。  
By the time he stood up, Quanrong banners were already flying over the city wall.  
狼进了羊圈，会是什么样子？  
What happens when wolves get into a sheepfold（羊圈）?  
最懂这个答案的，正是放羊为生的犬戎。  
No one knew the answer better than the sheep-herding Quanrong.  
他们进了镐京，就像狼进羊群。  
Once they entered Haojing, they were wolves among sheep.  
大周国都的繁华。  
The splendor（繁华） of the Zhou capital  
对他们来说，样样都新鲜。  
seemed astonishingly new to them in every way.  
看什么都像稀世珍宝。  
Everything looked like a rare treasure（珍宝）.  
于是他们见东西就抢，见人就杀。  
So they grabbed whatever they saw and killed whoever stood in their way.  
申侯的军队随后进城。  
Marquis Shen's troops entered the city afterward.  
这时他才发现麻烦大了。  
Only then did he realize how serious the trouble was.  
这帮人比他想的野蛮得多。  
They were far more savage（野蛮的） than he had imagined.  
他想拦，也根本拦不住。  
He wanted to stop them, yet he had no power to do so.  

幽王其实本来未必会死。  
King You might not necessarily have had to die.  
犬戎要的是财物。  
What the Quanrong wanted was wealth.  
申侯想杀的，也是褒后母子。  
And the people Marquis Shen wanted dead were Queen Bao and her son.  
可幽王自己不肯独活。  
But King You would not save himself alone.  
他身边只剩下郑侯姬友。  
At his side remained only Ji You, the Lord of Zheng.  
那是他的亲叔叔。  
He was the king's own uncle.  
大难临头，还是亲叔叔靠得住。  
When disaster（灾祸） struck, it was still close kin who could be relied on.  
姬友劝他，把王的行头交出来。  
Ji You urged him to hand over the royal robe and insignia（仪仗）.  
自己穿上，替他引开敌人。  
He himself would put them on and draw the enemy away.  
幽王混在百姓里逃走。  
King You could then escape among the common people.  
幽王不肯。  
King You refused.  
他说自己堂堂天子，丢不起这个人。  
He said that as the Son of Heaven, he could not bear such humiliation（羞辱）.  
姬友又劝他骑快马突围。  
Ji You then urged him to break out on swift horses.  
可幽王还是不肯。  
But King You still refused.  
他说，要走就带褒后和伯服一起走。  
He said that if he left, he would leave with Queen Bao and Bofu.  
要死，也要一家人死在一起。  
And if he died, the whole family would die together.  
到了这地步，他仍舍不下儿女情长。  
Even at such a moment, he still could not let go of private attachment（私情）.  
姬友听完，竟也有些动容。  
After hearing this, even Ji You was moved（动容的）.  
于是他让人备车。  
So he ordered a carriage prepared.  
幽王一家三口上了车。  
King You, Queen Bao, and Bofu got in.  
姬友自己登上战车。  
Ji You himself mounted a war chariot.  
又带着二三十名卫士。  
He also took twenty or thirty guards with him.  
亲自挥戟护送。  
He personally led the escort, halberd（戟） in hand.  
他们朝东门杀去。  
They fought their way toward the eastern gate.  
姬友确实勇猛。  
Ji You truly was a formidable（勇猛的） fighter.  
戎兵又一时没反应过来。  
And the Quanrong had been caught off guard（措手不及） for a moment.  
竟真让他护着幽王冲出了东门。  
So he actually managed to protect the king out through the eastern gate.  
犬戎国主本来都不想追了。  
The Quanrong ruler had not even meant to pursue them.  
偏偏申侯一再催着，非要杀褒姒不可。  
But Marquis Shen kept pressing him, insisting that Bao Si must be killed.  
于是追兵一路追到骊山脚下。  
So the pursuers chased them all the way to the foot of Mount Li.  
若幽王骑的是马，也许还能跑掉。  
Had King You been on horseback, he might have escaped.  
可他坐的是车。  
But he was in a carriage.  
车怎么跑得过骑兵？  
How could a carriage outrun cavalry（骑兵）?  
姬友拼死护驾，到最后力尽战死。  
Ji You defended the king to the death and finally fell when his strength was spent.  
幽王这时倒像条汉子。  
At that moment, King You at least behaved like a man.  
他说，不要欺负妇女儿童。  
He shouted that they were not to harm women and children.  
要杀就先杀他。  
If someone had to be killed, then kill him first.  
戎兵本来未必想杀他。  
The Quanrong soldiers had not necessarily intended to kill him.  
可既然他自己求死，他们也就一拥而上。  
But since he himself demanded death, they rushed him all at once.  
幽王被乱刀砍死。  
King You was hacked to death.  
伯服也被一刀杀了。  
Bofu too was cut down with a single stroke.  
只有吓傻的褒后，被活捉回去。  
Only the terrified（吓呆的） Queen Bao was taken away alive.  
这一年，镐京陷落。  
That year, Haojing fell.  
幽王死于乱军之中。  
And King You died amid the chaos of war.

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`虚拟条件句`、`宾语从句`、`被动表达`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `Had King You been on horseback, he might have escaped`。<br>
难点拆解：这里用了省略 `if` 的虚拟条件倒装；`He said that ...`、`insisting that ...` 都带宾语从句；`was taken away alive` 和 `was hacked to death` 属于被动表达，更突出人物遭遇。<br>
常用短语：
- `be caught off guard`：措手不及
- `all at once`：一下子，一拥而上
- `to the death`：拼死地
- `amid the chaos of`：在……的混乱中

### 申侯傻了
### Marquis Shen Is Stunned

鬼子真的进村以后。  
Once the invaders truly flooded into the city,  
镐京到处烧杀抢掠。  
Haojing was filled with arson（纵火）, slaughter（屠杀）, and looting（抢掠）.  
犬戎国主住进王宫，也痛快了一回。  
The Quanrong ruler moved into the royal palace and enjoyed himself thoroughly.  
申侯看在眼里，气在心里。  
Marquis Shen watched it happen and burned with anger inside.  
可他一句硬话也不敢说。  
Yet he did not dare utter a single harsh word.  
真翻脸，他那点兵根本打不过犬戎。  
If he truly turned against them, his small force would stand no chance against the Quanrong.  
这下申侯真傻了。  
Now Marquis Shen was truly stunned（发懵的）.  
该杀的没杀成。  
The people he wanted dead had not been killed.  
不该杀的，却杀了。  
And the ones he had not meant to kill had died instead.  
眼下他能做的，只剩两件事。  
At that point, there were only two things left that he could do.  
第一件，是护住自己的女儿。  
The first was to protect his daughter.  
第二件，是赶紧派人出城求援。  
The second was to send men out at once to seek help.  
直到这时，诸侯才知道。  
Only then did the lords realize  
这一次的烽火，是真的。  
that this time the beacon fire was real.  
其实诸侯并不是不爱国。  
In truth, the lords were not devoid（缺乏的） of loyalty.  
他们也不是不在乎京城和天子。  
Nor were they indifferent（冷漠的） to the capital and the Son of Heaven.  
只是幽王以前把信用玩光了。  
It was simply that King You had squandered（耗尽） all credibility before this.  
如今确认消息属实，诸侯还是出兵了。  
Now that the news had been confirmed true, the lords did send troops after all.  
这次来的有秦、晋、卫、郑四路。  
This time four powers came: Qin, Jin, Wei, and Zheng.  
而且都是国君亲自带队。  
And each was led by its ruler in person.  
众军急奔镐京。  
Their armies hurried toward Haojing.  
犬戎一看四路援军到了。  
When the Quanrong saw four relief armies arriving,  
也不敢恋战。  
they no longer dared prolong the battle（恋战）.  
他们趁着夜色撤走。  
They withdrew under cover of darkness（夜色掩护）.  
诸侯军随后追击，却没追上。  
The lords pursued them afterward, but could not catch them.  
至于褒后，被犬戎带走以后。  
As for Queen Bao, after she was carried off by the Quanrong,  
后来到底怎样，谁也说不清。  
no one can say for certain what became of her afterward.  
有人说她自尽了。  
Some say she killed herself.  
有人说她成了犬戎国主的女人。  
Some say she became the Quanrong ruler's woman.  
也有人说，褒洪德后来把她赎了回来。  
Others say that Bao Hongde later redeemed（赎回） her with ransom（赎金）.  
总之，说法很多。  
In short, many versions still circulate（流传）.  
说到底，褒姒其实是个很可怜的女人。  
In the final analysis（归根结底）, Bao Si was a pitiable（可怜的） woman.  
她生下来就差点被淹死。  
She had nearly been drowned at birth.  
小时候在穷人家长大。  
She grew up in a poor household.  
五岁上山采蘑菇。  
At five, she was gathering mushrooms in the hills.  
十四岁就进了宫。  
At fourteen, she entered the palace.  
后来先是和情人生离死别。  
Later she was torn away from the man she loved.  
再后来，丈夫被杀，儿子被杀。  
After that, her husband was killed, and so was her son.  
她自己也被掳走。  
She herself was carried off as well.  
可就是这样一个女人。  
And yet this very woman  
却被许多后人骂成了亡国祸水。  
was condemned（谴责） by many later generations as the beauty who ruined a dynasty（王朝）.  
这公平吗？  
Is that fair?  
这又到哪里说理去？  
And where could she ever go to plead her case?

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`主语从句`、`时间状语从句`、`倒装`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `Only then did the lords realize that this time the beacon fire was real`。<br>
难点拆解：`Only then` 放句首触发部分倒装；`that this time ... was real` 是宾语从句；`Now that the news had been confirmed true ...` 是时间兼原因色彩很强的从句，说明诸侯为何终于出兵。<br>
常用短语：
- `stand no chance against`：完全不是……的对手
- `under cover of darkness`：趁着夜色
- `for certain`：确定地
- `plead one's case`：为自己申辩

## 第五章 春秋开始了
## Chapter 5: The Spring and Autumn Era Begins

### 周平王
### King Ping of Zhou

局势一变，连汉奸也会把自己装成忠臣。  
When the tide turns, even collaborators（汉奸） try to pass themselves off as loyalists（忠臣）.  
四国诸侯的大军正要进入镐京。  
The armies of the four feudal lords were about to enter Haojing.  
申侯先打开了城门。  
Marquis Shen opened the city gates first.  
这也算是里应外合。  
That could be called coordination from inside and out.  
不过城里其实也没什么可接应的了。  
In truth, there was not much left inside to coordinate with.  
申侯装出一副无辜样子。  
Marquis Shen put on an innocent face.  
亲自出去迎接四国国君。  
He personally went out to greet the rulers of the four states.  
然后一把鼻涕一把泪地控诉犬戎。  
Then, in a flood of tears, he denounced the crimes of the Quanrong.  
他说，要不是自己拼命周旋。  
He claimed that if he had not done everything in his power,  
城里的公卿早就被灭门了。  
the nobles in the city would all have been wiped out.  
顺便，他也轻描淡写地检讨了自己。  
At the same time, he offered a mild little self-criticism（自我批评）.  
说自己是好心办了坏事。  
He said he had meant well and only made a terrible mistake.  
瞎了眼，才信了草原上的白眼狼。  
He had been blind enough to trust wolves from the grasslands.  
奇怪的是，竟然没人追究他。  
Strangely enough, no one pressed the matter against him.  
这倒不是因为他表演得多好。  
That was not because his performance was especially convincing（令人信服的）.  
而是因为他手里还有一张牌。  
It was because he still held one crucial card.  
这张牌，就是废太子宜臼。  
That card was the deposed crown prince Yijiu.  
幽王死了。  
King You was dead.  
伯服也死了。  
Bofu was dead as well.  
算来算去，能继位的只剩宜臼。  
However one counted, Yijiu was the only one left who could inherit the throne.

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`条件状语从句`、`宾语从句`、`定语从句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `Yijiu was the only one left who could inherit the throne`。<br>
难点拆解：`if he had not done ...` 是条件状语从句；`He claimed that ...` 里的 `that ...` 是宾语从句；`who could inherit the throne` 用定语从句补充说明 `the only one left`。<br>
常用短语：
- `pass oneself off as`：把自己装成……
- `press the matter against`：追究……
- `do everything in one's power`：尽全力
- `the only one left`：剩下的唯一一个

于是，一个临时的“治安委员会”作出了一致决定。  
So a temporary "public order committee" reached a unanimous decision（一致决定）.  
宜臼即位，当周天子。  
Yijiu would take the throne as Son of Heaven.  
从这一天起，他就是周平王。  
From that day on, he was King Ping of Zhou.  
平王登基以后，先尊母亲申后为太后。  
After taking the throne, King Ping first honored his mother, Queen Shen, as Queen Dowager.  
又想把外公申侯晋封为申公。  
He then wanted to raise his grandfather Marquis Shen to Duke of Shen.  
这里得先说一句。  
A brief explanation is needed here.  
周朝的诸侯爵位一共分五等。  
The Zhou feudal ranks were divided into five grades.  
从高到低，依次是公、侯、伯、子、男。  
From highest to lowest, they were duke, marquis, earl, viscount（子爵）, and baron（男爵）.  
公是最高一级。  
Duke was the highest rank.  
当年武王灭商以后，大规模分封诸侯。  
When King Wu overthrew Shang, he enfeoffed（分封） lords on a large scale.  
真正能封到公爵的，没有几个。  
Very few were granted the rank of duke.  
所以，平王这次想给申侯晋爵。  
So when King Ping tried to promote Marquis Shen this time,  
这份赏赐其实很重。  
the proposed reward carried enormous weight.  
申侯却连连摆手。  
Marquis Shen waved his hands again and again.  
他说自己罪孽太重，不敢受。  
He said his guilt was too heavy and he dared not accept it.  
这一回，他倒还算有点自知之明。  
For once, he showed a bit of self-awareness（自知之明）.  
平王想想，也就不再勉强。  
King Ping thought it over and did not press him further.  
接着，他开始论功行赏。  
Then he began rewarding merit.  
卫武侯晋为卫武公。  
The Marquis of Wei was raised to Duke Wu of Wei.  
还留在周朝做大司徒。  
He also remained at Zhou as Grand Minister of Works.  
晋文侯虽然没晋爵。  
The Marquis Wen of Jin was not promoted in title.  
却拿到了河内之地。  
But he was given the region of Henei.  
郑世子掘突继承父爵。  
Juetu of Zheng inherited his father's rank.  
又得了千亩良田。  
He also received a thousand mu of good farmland.  
还留在朝中做正卿。  
He stayed at court as a chief minister.  
秦襄公原本只是周的附庸。  
Duke Xiang of Qin had originally been only a Zhou vassal（附庸）.  
这时却被正式封为伯爵。  
At this point he was formally enfeoffed as an earl.  
从此跻身诸侯之列。  
From then on, Qin entered the ranks of the feudal lords.  
这一来，四路诸侯都没白跑。  
This time, the four lords had certainly not made the trip for nothing.  
说到底，被领导忽悠几回。  
When all was said and done, being fooled by a leader a few times  
有时也未必全是坏事。  
did not always turn out to be entirely bad.  
因为熬到最后，往往还能领赏。  
If you lasted to the end, there might even be rewards waiting.  
申侯又看中了掘突。  
Marquis Shen also took a liking to Juetu.  
觉得这年轻人精明能干，仪表不凡。  
He thought the young man was clever, capable, and impressive in bearing（仪表）.  
正好自己还有个小女儿。  
He happened to have a younger daughter as well.  
于是托卫武公做媒。  
So he asked Duke Wu of Wei to act as a go-between（媒人）.  
把小女儿嫁给了掘突。  
He married that daughter to Juetu.  
这样一来，两家又成了亲戚。  
In this way, the two houses became relatives.  
申侯害死了幽王。  
Marquis Shen had caused King You's death.  
却又扶立了幽王的儿子。  
Yet he went on to enthrone King You's son.  
他害死了姬友。  
He had also caused Ji You's death.  
转头又把女儿嫁给了姬友的儿子。  
And then he married his daughter to Ji You's son.  
害了父亲，再把人情做给儿子。  
He destroyed the father and then did favors for the son.  
申侯也算春秋开局的一个奇人。  
Marquis Shen was truly a bizarre figure（奇人） at the opening of the Spring and Autumn age.

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`宾语从句`、`并列句`、`被动表达`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `the proposed reward carried enormous weight` 和 `Qin entered the ranks of the feudal lords`。<br>
难点拆解：`He said his guilt was too heavy ...` 和 `He thought the young man ...` 都带宾语从句；中间多组短句并列推进，像历史镜头一样往前切；`was formally enfeoffed` 用被动表达，更符合史书叙述口吻。<br>
常用短语：
- `carry enormous weight`：分量极重
- `press someone further`：继续逼某人接受
- `enter the ranks of`：进入……行列
- `when all is said and done`：说到底

### 迁都
### Moving the Capital

周平王心里其实很不痛快。  
King Ping of Zhou felt deeply unsettled（不安的）.  
不是所有当上天子的人都会觉得快活。  
Not everyone who becomes Son of Heaven automatically feels happy.  
他不痛快有两个原因。  
He had two reasons for feeling this way.  
第一个原因，国库被搬空了。  
The first reason was that the state treasury had been emptied out.  
犬戎把能搬的几乎都搬走了。  
The Quanrong had carried off nearly everything worth taking.  
宫殿和城墙也被糟蹋得不成样子。  
The palaces and walls had been ruined as well.  
到处脏乱不堪。  
The whole place was filthy and chaotic（混乱的）.  
连空气里都像带着羊膻味。  
Even the air seemed to carry the smell of sheep.  
第二个原因，更现实。  
The second reason was even more practical（现实的）.  
犬戎尝到甜头以后，隔三岔五就来骚扰抢掠。  
After tasting success, the Quanrong kept coming back to raid and plunder（抢掠）.  
没过几个月，周朝西边的岐、丰一带都丢了。  
Within a few months, Zhou had lost the western regions of Qi and Feng.  
镐京没了屏障，已经危在旦夕。  
With its outer shield gone, Haojing was in immediate peril（危急）.  
平王这才发现，当天子也会整天提心吊胆。  
King Ping realized that even a Son of Heaven could spend every day in fear.  
既然惹不起，不如躲开。  
If he could not fight them, he might as well move out of the way.  
他想起祖宗在东边还留着一块地方。  
He remembered that his ancestors had left a territory in the east.  
那就是洛邑。  
That place was Luoyi.  
第二天，他召来公卿议事。  
The next day he summoned the nobles and ministers.  
议题只有两个字：迁都。  
The agenda had only two words: move the capital.  
当年周成王体恤诸侯进京太远。  
In earlier days, King Cheng of Zhou had considered how far the lords had to travel to court.  
所以在天下中央营建了洛邑。  
So he built Luoyi in the center of the realm.  
每逢朝会，天子就去那里接见诸侯。  
Whenever court assemblies were held, the Son of Heaven received the lords there.  
那里的宫室制度，几乎和镐京一样齐全。  
Its palace complex was almost as complete as Haojing's.  
简单说，就是全套装修和家具都配好了。  
Put simply, it came with the full furnishings and fittings.  
搬过去就能住。  
You could move in and live there at once.  
而且两百多年过去，洛邑早已繁华起来。  
And after more than two centuries, Luoyi had already grown prosperous（繁华的）.  
甚至比镐京还热闹。  
It was even livelier than Haojing.  
谁不想去更安全、更热闹的地方睡个安稳觉呢？  
Who would not want to sleep peacefully in a safer and livelier place?  
公卿们一致同意。  
The ministers agreed unanimously（一致地）.  
搬。  
Move.  
镐京在西，洛邑在东。  
Haojing lay in the west, and Luoyi in the east.  
从西边的旧都迁到东边的新都。  
The court moved from the old western capital to the new eastern one.  
从这一刻起，西周就成了东周。  
From that moment on, Western Zhou became Eastern Zhou.  
天子搬家时，秦襄公出了大力。  
When the Son of Heaven moved, Duke Xiang of Qin contributed enormously（巨大地）.  
他出人出力，还亲自率三百乘战车护送。  
He provided manpower and resources, and personally escorted the court with three hundred chariots.  
简直像一家搬家公司兼武装押运公司。  
He was like a moving company and armed escort service rolled into one.  
平王很高兴。  
King Ping was delighted.  
他说，如今刚迁都，没什么好赏的。  
He said that having just moved the capital, he had little to bestow.  
不过岐、丰之地如今被犬戎占着。  
But Qi and Feng were now occupied by the Quanrong.  
只要秦能打回来，那片地就归秦所有。  
If Qin could retake them, the land would belong to Qin.  
秦襄公谢恩而去。  
Duke Xiang thanked him and departed.  
后来秦国果然击败犬戎，地盘大增。  
Later Qin did indeed defeat the Quanrong and vastly expand its territory.  
这也成了秦国崛起的重要一步。  
That became one of the crucial（关键的） steps in Qin's rise.

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`条件状语从句`、`时间状语从句`、`并列句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `If Qin could retake them, the land would belong to Qin`。<br>
难点拆解：`If Qin could retake them` 是条件状语从句；`Whenever court assemblies were held` 是时间状语从句；中间大量并列短句把迁都的现实压力和洛邑的优势连在一起，读起来更有推进感。<br>
常用短语：
- `move out of the way`：主动避开
- `at once`：立刻，马上
- `rolled into one`：合成一体
- `contribute enormously`：出力很大

周平王元年，也就是前770年。  
In the first year of King Ping, that is, 770 BCE,  
他登基才八个月，就东迁了。  
he moved the capital east only eight months after taking the throne.  
恰好距离岐山地震整整十年。  
It was exactly ten years after the earthquake at Mount Qi.  
可他为什么忍了八个月才走？  
But why had he waited eight months before leaving?  
原因很简单。  
The reason was simple.  
按周礼，人活着时不能先给自己准备后事。  
According to Zhou ritual, a living person could not prepare funeral matters in advance.  
寿衣、棺椁、陵墓，都要死后才能安排。  
Shrouds, coffins, outer caskets, and burial mounds all had to be arranged only after death.  
而且地位越高，下葬越慢。  
And the higher the rank, the longer the burial took.  
天子死后，先停丧七天。  
After a king died, the body lay in mourning for seven days.  
然后修陵墓，通知诸侯来会葬。  
Then the tomb was prepared and the lords were summoned to attend the burial.  
等人到齐了，七个月后才下葬。  
Only when everyone had arrived was he buried, seven months later.  
诸侯死后，五个月下葬。  
A feudal lord was buried after five months.  
大夫三个月。  
A minister after three.  
士和百姓两个月。  
Scholars and commoners after two.  
说得刻薄一点。  
To put it bluntly（刻薄地）,  
乞丐什么时候埋，就看野狗什么时候来。  
a beggar's burial depended on when the wild dogs showed up.  
不仅时间有差别。  
The timing was not the only thing strictly graded.  
棺和椁也有严格规定。  
There were also strict rules for coffins.  
里面一层叫棺，外面一层叫椁。  
The inner case was the coffin, and the outer one was the casket shell.  
按周礼，天子七重，诸侯五重，大夫三重，士两重。  
By Zhou ritual, a king had seven layers, a lord five, a minister three, and a gentleman two.  
所以平王只能先把幽王安葬。  
So King Ping had no choice but to bury King You first.  
等礼数都尽完了，才能正式搬家。  
Only after all the rites were completed could he officially move.  
这样一看，周朝这套规矩其实还有点道理。  
Seen this way, the Zhou system had a certain logic to it.  
至少不像后来的皇帝那样。  
At least it was not like later emperors,  
一登基就忙着给自己修大墓。  
who began building massive tombs the moment they took the throne.  
顺便说一句，幽王之所以谥为“幽”。  
Incidentally（顺便地）, King You received the posthumous title "You"  
正因为他死于非命。  
precisely because he met a violent end.  
从这里起，春秋的大幕算是真正拉开了。  
From here, the curtain on the Spring and Autumn period truly rose.

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`倒装`、`时间状语从句`、`被动表达`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `Only after all the rites were completed could he officially move`。<br>
难点拆解：`Only after ...` 放句首触发倒装；`the rites were completed` 是被动表达，突出“礼数完成”这一客观条件；前面 `After a king died ...` 也是典型时间状语从句。<br>
常用短语：
- `in advance`：提前
- `have no choice but to do`：别无选择，只能……
- `met a violent end`：死于非命
- `the moment ...`：一……就……

### 精彩春秋
### A Brilliant Spring and Autumn Age

三个人。  
Three men.  
周幽王、虢石父、申侯。  
King You of Zhou, Guo Shifu, and Marquis Shen.  
他们合力干下了两件大蠢事。  
Together they pulled off two monumental blunders（大错）.  
一件是烽火戏诸侯。  
One was lighting the beacons to fool the lords.  
一件是引狼入室。  
The other was letting the wolf into the house.  
正是这两件蠢事。  
It was these two follies  
一起葬送了西周。  
that together buried Western Zhou.  
也动摇了周王朝的根基。  
They also shook the very foundations（根基） of the Zhou dynasty.  
可西周一亡，春秋战国反而开场了。  
Yet once Western Zhou ended, the Spring and Autumn and Warring States ages began.  
那是一个满是智慧、才华与豪气的时代。  
It was an age overflowing with intelligence（智慧）, talent, and bold spirit.  
听起来很怪。  
That sounds strange.  
可历史往往就是这样推进的。  
But history often moves ahead in exactly such ways.  
这并不是毫无道理。  
And it is not without reason.  
周王室衰落之后。  
After the royal house of Zhou declined,  
周礼也开始败坏。  
the Zhou rites began to decay（衰败） as well.  
更重要的是，诸侯不再敬畏中央。  
More importantly, the feudal lords no longer stood in awe of the center.  
中央强盛的时候。  
When the central power was strong,  
诸侯的思想和行为都被卡得很死。  
the lords' thinking and behavior were tightly constrained（限制）.  
他们得按中央的规矩办事。  
They had to act by central rules.  
这固然稳定。  
That certainly brought stability.  
却也压掉了个性、想象力和进取心。  
But it also crushed individuality, imagination, and ambition（进取心）.  
社会就容易停滞。  
A society like that easily falls stagnant（停滞的）.  
等中央衰了。  
Once the center weakened,  
地方诸侯终于能按自己的想法办事。  
the local lords could finally act on ideas of their own.  
野心家、阴谋家，也就纷纷冒出来了。  
Ambitious men and schemers emerged one after another.  
旧禁锢被打破。  
Old restraints were smashed.  
改革、开放、搞活，都跟着来了。  
Reform, openness, and revitalization（振兴） all came with them.  
从经济到政治，从物质到精神。  
From the economy to politics, from material life to ideas,  
春秋无疑是一个黄金时代。  
the Spring and Autumn period was undoubtedly a golden age.  
可旧秩序一旦失灵。  
But once the old order failed,  
新的平衡也只能靠战争重建。  
a new balance could only be rebuilt through war.  
于是你来我往，分分合合。  
So states fought, allied, split, and reunited.  
整个春秋，几乎就是一部战争史。  
The whole Spring and Autumn era was, in large measure, a history of war.  
后人总说，春秋无义战。  
Later generations often said, "There was no righteous war in the Spring and Autumn period."  
可这句话本身就有问题。  
But that statement itself is problematic（有问题的）.  
古今中外，战争本来就是为利益。  
In every age and in every land, war has basically been fought for gain（利益）.  
杀人的游戏，和“义”并没有什么关系。  
Games of killing have little to do with righteousness（正义）.  
既然有战争，就得想办法活下去。  
And once war exists, people must find ways to survive.  
要活下去，就得发展。  
To survive, they must develop.  
于是英雄也就上场了。  
And that is when heroes stepped onto the stage.  
接下来要出场的第一个英雄。  
The first hero to appear next  
名字叫姬寤生。  
was named Ji Wusheng.  
也就是后来的郑庄公。  
He was the man later known as Duke Zhuang of Zheng.  
史书对他的评价并不一致。  
Historical judgment on him has never been uniform（一致的）.  
但只要看看他的事迹。  
But if we look at what he actually did,  
我们大概会重新认识他。  
we may come to see him in a new light.

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`时间状语从句`、`宾语从句`、`并列句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `Once the center weakened, the local lords could finally act on ideas of their own`。<br>
难点拆解：`Once the center weakened` 是时间状语从句；`Later generations often said that ...` 里含宾语从句；整段大量并列短句把“旧秩序崩坏”和“新时代开启”两个层次接在一起。<br>
常用短语：
- `stand in awe of`：敬畏……
- `act on ideas of one's own`：按自己的想法行事
- `one after another`：一个接一个
- `come to see ... in a new light`：重新认识……

### 深谋远虑者
### A Far-Sighted Schemer

郑国，从一开始就不是一个简单的国家。  
From the very beginning, Zheng was no simple state.  
说它是个阴谋之国，也行。  
You could even call it a state built on calculation（算计）.  
说它深谋远虑，也行。  
Or, more charitably（宽厚地）, a state of far-sighted planning.  
宣王有个弟弟，叫姬友。  
King Xuan had a younger brother named Ji You.  
此人英武聪明，也吃得了苦。  
He was brave, intelligent, and capable of hardship.  
宣王时，姬友担任司徒。  
During King Xuan's reign, Ji You served as Minister of Land.  
也就是掌管土地的大臣。  
That was the official in charge of land administration.  
他的地位很高。  
His position was very high.  
宣王后来把他封在郑。  
King Xuan later enfeoffed him in Zheng.  
爵位是伯。  
His rank was earl.  
那时的郑，还不在今天河南郑州一带。  
At that time, Zheng was not yet in the region of modern Zhengzhou.  
它在陕西，紧挨着西戎。  
It lay in Shaanxi, right next to the western tribes.  
地方不大，处境却危险。  
Its territory was small, and its situation was dangerous（危险的）.  
到了幽王时，西戎越来越强。  
By King You's time, the western tribes were growing stronger and stronger.  
幽王自己又不务正业。  
And King You himself neglected proper rule.  
姬友看得很清楚。  
Ji You saw the situation clearly.  
周朝迟早会衰下去。  
Zhou would decline sooner or later.  
这个时候，人就得给自己找后路。  
At such a moment, a man had to secure a way out.  
什么叫英雄？  
What makes a hero?  
知道什么时候进，什么时候退。  
Knowing when to advance and when to retreat.  
也知道什么叫荣辱轻重。  
And knowing the weight of honor and disgrace.  
于是姬友私下去找伯阳父。  
So Ji You secretly went to see Boyangfu.  
伯阳父是周朝最聪明的人之一。  
Boyangfu was one of the smartest men in Zhou.  
几杯酒下肚，两人就说到正题。  
After a few cups of wine, they got to the point.  
姬友问得非常直接。  
Ji You's question was extremely direct.  
国家乱成这样。  
With the state in such chaos,  
自己怕被牵连丢命。  
he feared being dragged down and losing his life.  
还能去哪里找一块地保命？  
Where could he still find a tract of land to save himself?  
国家真救不了，就先救自己。  
If the state truly could not be saved, then one should save oneself first.  
可问题是，周朝的地都分得差不多了。  
The trouble was that Zhou's lands had already been mostly divided up.  
所以他才来向伯阳父请教。  
That was exactly why he had come to ask Boyangfu.  
伯阳父也没有藏着掖着。  
Boyangfu did not hold anything back.  
他把形势分析得很透。  
He analyzed the whole situation with great clarity（清楚）.  
西边有西戎，难缠。  
To the west were the western tribes, troublesome and swift.  
南边有楚国，打不过。  
To the south was Chu, too strong to fight.  
北边有晋国，惹不起。  
To the north was Jin, too lofty to provoke.  
东边有齐国和鲁国，也都碰不得。  
To the east were Qi and Lu, neither of which should be touched.  
只有中间那一带。  
Only the central region remained.  
那里有东虢和郐两个小国。  
There lay two small states, Eastern Guo and Kuai.  
一个是子爵，一个是男爵。  
One was a viscount and one a baron.  
爵位都低。  
Both held low rank.  
而且两国君主都不得人心。  
And neither ruler was loved by his people.  
与其硬碰大国。  
Instead of clashing head-on with a great state,  
不如先在那里弄一块地。  
why not first obtain a piece of land there?  
将来时机一到，再把两国吃掉。  
When the right time came, the two states could then be swallowed up.  
那不就做大了？  
Would that not make Zheng powerful?  
姬友一听，顿时明白了。  
The moment Ji You heard this, he understood.  
他立刻照着做。  
He acted at once.  
先拿重金去贿赂东虢和郐的君主。  
He first used heavy bribes to buy off the rulers of Eastern Guo and Kuai.  
然后再以中央官员的身份。  
Then, under the authority of a central official,  
要求他们献出一些土地。  
he asked them to contribute some land.  
两国一来收了好处。  
Since the two rulers had taken the gifts,  
二来也想巴结中央。  
and also wanted to curry favor（巴结） with the center,  
果然各自献出了十座城。  
they did indeed each hand over ten cities.  
姬友再回头告诉幽王。  
Ji You then informed King You  
说自己已经找好地方，想把郑国迁过去。  
that he had found a new site and wished to move Zheng there.  
幽王乐得做个顺水人情。  
King You was happy to grant an easy favor.  
当场就答应了。  
He approved it on the spot.  
于是，郑国国民整体迁到了今天的河南荥阳一带。  
And so the people of Zheng moved as a whole to the area of modern Xingyang in Henan.  
这就是后来的郑国。  
That was the Zheng state of later history.  
姬友就是郑桓公。  
Ji You himself was Duke Huan of Zheng.  
他也成了郑姓、段姓、游姓等许多姓氏的始祖。  
He also became the founding ancestor（始祖） of many surnames, including Zheng, Duan, and You.  
郑国的事安排好了。  
Ji You arranged Zheng's affairs well.  
可他自己却没躲过劫数。  
But he himself did not escape his doom（劫数）.  
后来姬友战死。  
Later Ji You died in battle.  
儿子掘突继位。  
His son Juetu succeeded him.  
果然又灭了东虢和郐。  
And, just as predicted, he later destroyed Eastern Guo and Kuai.  
郑国于是做大。  
Zheng thus became a major power.  
这一切，都在伯阳父当年的计算里。  
All of it had lain within Boyangfu's calculations years before.

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`条件状语从句`、`宾语从句`、`定语从句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `If the state truly could not be saved, then one should save oneself first`。<br>
难点拆解：`If the state truly could not be saved` 是条件状语从句；`Ji You then informed King You that ...` 里的 `that ...` 是宾语从句；`neither of which should be touched` 这一类表达带有定语从句意味，用来补充说明前面的国家。<br>
常用短语：
- `secure a way out`：给自己留后路
- `hold nothing back`：毫无保留
- `clash head-on with`：正面硬碰……
- `curry favor with`：巴结……

### 第一位男主角
### The First Male Lead

春秋的第一位男主角，就要登场了。  
The first male lead of the Spring and Autumn era was about to appear.  
照中国历史的老规矩。  
By the old conventions of Chinese history,  
英雄出生总得带点传奇色彩。  
heroes are usually born with a touch of legend（传奇）.  
要么母亲梦见神人。  
Either the mother dreams of a divine being,  
要么天上打雷显灵。  
or thunder from heaven marks the birth.  
总之，得神神怪怪一下。  
In short, something supernatural is expected.  
可这一位恰好相反。  
But this one was the exact opposite.  
幽王被杀那一年。  
In the year King You was killed,  
郑武公和申侯的小女儿订了婚。  
Duke Wu of Zheng became engaged to the younger daughter of Marquis Shen.  
那姑娘年纪还小。  
The girl was still young at the time.  
直到郑武公十年，也就是前761年。  
Not until the tenth year of Duke Wu, in 761 BCE,  
郑武公才正式娶她为妻。  
did he formally marry her.  
史书称她为武姜。  
The histories call her Wu Jiang.  
因为她是武公的夫人，又姓姜。  
That was because she was Duke Wu's wife and her clan name was Jiang.  
四年后，她生下第一个儿子。  
Four years later, she bore her first son.  
武姜是个很强悍的女人。  
Wu Jiang was a formidable（强悍的） woman.  
传说她曾赤手空拳打死过野猪。  
Legend said she had once killed a wild boar with her bare hands.  
她怀胎十月，一直没有要生的样子。  
She carried the child for ten months with no sign of labor.  
她也不当回事。  
She did not think much of it.  
那天，她睡得很沉。  
That day she was sleeping deeply.  
梦里还在打狼。  
In her dream, she was fighting wolves.  
忽然听见孩子的哭声。  
Suddenly she heard a baby's cry.  
起初她以为还在做梦。  
At first she thought she was still dreaming.  
后来哭声越来越真。  
Then the crying grew more and more real.  
她睁眼一看，孩子已经生出来了。  
When she opened her eyes, the child had already been born.  
而且还是个男孩。  
And it was a boy.  
因为是在睡醒时生的。  
Because he had been born as she woke from sleep,  
所以取名叫寤生。  
he was named Wusheng.  
寤，就是醒来的意思。  
The character *wu* means "to wake."  
后世还有一种说法。  
Later generations also offered another explanation.  
说寤生其实是逆生。  
They said Wusheng really referred to a breech birth（逆产）.  
也就是脚先出来。  
That is, the feet came out first.  
按申国的说法。  
In the traditions of Shen,  
这样的孩子不吉利。  
such a child was inauspicious（不吉利的）.  
所以武姜一点也高兴不起来。  
So Wu Jiang could not feel happy at all.  
她干脆就给孩子起了这么个名字。  
She simply gave him that name.  
那时武公正在东周朝廷任正卿。  
At the time, Duke Wu was serving at the Eastern Zhou court as a chief minister.  
地方上的事顾不过来。  
He could not attend to household matters in detail.  
儿子叫什么，他也不太在乎。  
What his son was called did not matter much to him.  
三年以后，武姜又生了第二个儿子。  
Three years later, Wu Jiang gave birth to a second son.  
这一回，过程就正常多了。  
This time the process was much more ordinary.  
孩子叫段。  
The boy was named Duan.  
从这以后，武姜偏心就偏得很明显了。  
From then on, Wu Jiang's favoritism became painfully obvious.  
她不喜欢大儿子寤生。  
She did not like her elder son Wusheng.  
却格外宠爱小儿子段。  
But she adored her younger son Duan.  
说得夸张一点。  
To put it a little sharply,  
小儿子像亲生的。  
the younger son seemed like her real child.  
大儿子倒像隔壁家的。  
The elder one seemed almost borrowed from next door.  
后来武公临终时。  
Later, when Duke Wu was dying,  
武姜还试着劝他改立段为继承人。  
Wu Jiang even tried to persuade him to make Duan the heir.  
武公一口拒绝。  
Duke Wu flatly refused.  
嫡长子继承，是祖宗定下的规矩。  
Succession by the eldest legitimate son was the rule handed down by the ancestors.  
什么都能坏，规矩不能坏。  
Many things could break, but that rule could not.  
没过多久，武公就去世了。  
Not long afterward, Duke Wu died.  
那一年是前744年。  
That was 744 BCE.  
寤生十三岁。  
Wusheng was thirteen.  
段十岁。  
Duan was ten.

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`原因状语从句`、`时间状语从句`、`被动表达`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `Because he had been born as she woke from sleep, he was named Wusheng`。<br>
难点拆解：`Because ...` 是原因状语从句；`as she woke from sleep` 又在从句里补充时间；`was named Wusheng` 用被动表达“被取名为……”，这是英语里很常见的历史叙述写法。<br>
常用短语：
- `a touch of`：一点点……
- `think much of`：把……太当回事
- `flatly refuse`：断然拒绝
- `be handed down by`：由……传下来

这里顺便补个知识点。  
A brief note is worth adding here.  
在周代，死也分很多说法。  
In the Zhou period, even death had different terms.  
周王死，叫崩。  
When a Zhou king died, it was called *beng*.  
诸侯死，叫薨。  
When a feudal lord died, it was called *hong*.  
大夫死，叫卒。  
A minister's death was called *zu*.  
士死，叫不禄。  
A gentleman's death was called *bulu*.  
平民死，才直接叫死。  
Only common people simply "died."  
所以严格说，武公不是死了。  
So strictly speaking, Duke Wu did not merely "die."  
应该说，他薨了。  
More precisely, the term for his death was *hong*.  

### 忍
### Enduring in Silence

郑庄公即位时，只有十三岁。  
When Duke Zhuang of Zheng came to the throne, he was only thirteen.  
按虚岁算，也不过十四。  
Even by traditional reckoning, he was only fourteen.  
段十岁。  
Duan was ten.  
因为后来封在共城。  
Because he was later enfeoffed at Gongcheng,  
所以人们也叫他共叔段。  
people later called him Gongshu Duan.  
庄公刚即位没几天。  
Only a few days after Duke Zhuang took the throne,  
姜太后就找上门来兴师问罪。  
Queen Dowager Jiang came to confront him aggressively（气势汹汹地）.  
她说，你们兄弟两个都是我的儿子。  
She said, "You two are both my sons.  
你继承了父亲的爵位，地盘几百里。  
You inherited your father's rank and hundreds of li of land.  
你弟弟却只有共城那么点地方。  
Yet your younger brother has only a scrap like Gongcheng.  
你忍心吗？  
Can you really bear that?"  
从小到大，她一直骂寤生。  
From childhood onward, she had always cursed Wusheng.  
现在他当了国君。  
Now that he was ruler,  
她不好再骂得太难听。  
she could not curse him quite so openly.  
于是就直呼其名。  
So she simply called him by name.  
庄公一点也不顶嘴。  
Duke Zhuang did not argue at all.  
他说，娘说得对。  
He said his mother was right.  
弟弟想要哪里，尽管说。  
If his younger brother wanted land, she had only to name it.  
姜太后先要制邑。  
Queen Dowager Jiang first asked for Zhi.  
制邑是郑国要地。  
Zhi was a strategic stronghold（要地） of Zheng.  
也就是后来的虎牢关一带。  
It was the area later known as Hulao Pass.  
庄公却说，这块地真不能给。  
Duke Zhuang replied that this land truly could not be granted.  
因为父亲临终前特地交代过。  
His father had specifically instructed so before dying.  
制邑是国之要冲。  
Zhi was a vital pass of the state.  
不能分封给任何人。  
It could not be enfeoffed to anyone.  
除了制邑，别的地方都可以。  
Anywhere else would be possible.  
姜太后一听，就改口要京城。  
Hearing this, Queen Dowager Jiang shifted and asked for Jingcheng.  
京城也是郑国大城。  
Jingcheng was also a major city of Zheng.  
虽然险要略逊一筹。  
Though slightly less strategic（战略性的）,  
其他方面却并不差。  
it was no worse in other respects.  
庄公心里其实更舍不得京城。  
In fact, Duke Zhuang was even more reluctant to give up Jingcheng.  
可母亲步步紧逼。  
But his mother kept pressing him.  
还拿把弟弟赶回姥姥家来威胁。  
She even threatened to send Duan back to his maternal grandfather's house.  
庄公最后还是答应了。  
In the end, Duke Zhuang still agreed.  
他说，母亲要给弟弟的东西。  
He said that if it was something his mother wished to give his brother,  
自己哪里舍得不答应。  
how could he bear to refuse?  
第二天，他召集群臣宣布此事。  
The next day he summoned the ministers and announced it.  
上卿祭足当场反对。  
The chief minister Jizu objected at once.  
京城这么大的城。  
Jingcheng was such a large city.  
怎么能分出去？  
How could it be parceled out?  
庄公只说一句。  
Duke Zhuang answered with only one sentence.  
这是太后的意思。  
It was the Queen Dowager's will.  
祭足急了。  
Jizu grew anxious.  
他明白这样纵容下去，早晚要出事。  
He understood that if this indulgence（纵容） continued, trouble would come sooner or later.  
庄公却说了一句后来极有名的话。  
But Duke Zhuang spoke words that later became famous.  
多行不义必自毙。  
He who does too much evil will destroy himself.  
你先等着看吧。  
Just wait and watch.  
从这以后，叔段也越来越不像个普通公子了。  
From then on, Duan looked less and less like an ordinary prince.  
人们渐渐把他叫作京城大叔段。  
People gradually began calling him Great Uncle Duan of Jingcheng.  
庄公不是不知道母亲不喜欢自己。  
It was not that Duke Zhuang failed to know his mother disliked him.  
也不是不知道京城不该给弟弟。  
Nor did he fail to know that Jingcheng should not have been given away.  
他只是更清楚一件事。  
He simply knew one thing even more clearly.  
他现在必须忍。  
For the moment, he had to endure.

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`条件状语从句`、`宾语从句`、`反问句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `if it was something his mother wished to give his brother, how could he bear to refuse?`。<br>
难点拆解：前半句是条件状语从句；`his mother wished to give his brother` 里面又有宾语从句式的信息承载；后半句用反问句，不是单纯提问，而是在表示“我只能答应”。<br>
常用短语：
- `come to the throne`：即位
- `object at once`：当场反对
- `sooner or later`：迟早
- `wait and watch`：等着看

## 第六章 亲娘靠不住
## Chapter 6: Even Your Own Mother Cannot Be Trusted

### 太后的心情
### The Queen Dowager's State of Mind

周朝有个规矩。  
Zhou had a rule.  
凡是分到封地的人。  
Anyone who received a fief（封地）  
除非还在中央任职。  
unless he still held office at the center,  
否则就必须去封地居住。  
had to go live in that fief.  
这是个很好的规矩。  
It was a very good rule.  
它能少掉很多猜疑和尴尬。  
It spared people a great deal of suspicion（猜疑） and awkwardness.  
当年周武王分封诸侯时就是这样。  
That was how matters had worked when King Wu first enfeoffed the lords.  
哪怕是姜太公。  
Even Jiang Taigong,  
一受封也得立刻启程就国。  
once enfeoffed, had to set out at once for his state.  
周公和召公虽然留在中央辅政。  
The Duke of Zhou and the Duke of Shao stayed at the center to assist the king,  
但那是因为他们另有公职。  
but that was because they held central office.  
对诸侯子弟来说，这条规矩也一样。  
The rule applied equally to the sons of lords.  
一旦分了地。  
Once a prince received territory,  
除非还任大臣。  
unless he still served as a minister,  
否则不管多大年纪都得过去。  
he had to go there no matter how young he was.  
就算还会尿床，也得回自己的封地尿去。  
Even if he still wet the bed, he had to go wet it in his own domain.  
所以叔段得了京城以后。  
So after Duan received Jingcheng,  
也只好老老实实离开荥阳。  
he had no choice but to obediently leave Xingyang.  
他得去京城住。  
He had to go live in Jingcheng.  
太后当然舍不得。  
Of course the Queen Dowager could not bear it.  
可她也没办法。  
But she had no way around it.  
她甚至想跟着一起去。  
She even wanted to go with him.  
偏偏祖宗规矩又不许。  
But ancestral rules forbade it.  
后宫太后就得住在后宫。  
A queen dowager of the royal house had to remain in the inner palace.  
世上的事，总是有得有失。  
In this world, every gain comes with a loss.

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`条件状语从句`、`让步状语从句`、`并列句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `Anyone who received a fief had to go live in that fief`。<br>
难点拆解：`unless he still held office at the center` 是条件状语从句；`Even if he still wet the bed ...` 带让步色彩；整段前后都是规则解释，所以用了很多并列短句，把制度一条条说清楚。<br>
常用短语：
- `have no choice but to do`：别无选择，只能……
- `at once`：立刻
- `go live in`：去……居住
- `come with a loss`：伴随着损失

叔段去京城那天。  
On the day Duan left for Jingcheng,  
太后一直送到宫门口。  
the Queen Dowager escorted him all the way to the palace gate.  
叔段哭了。  
Duan cried.  
他才十岁。  
He was only ten.  
他不明白为什么一定要离开母亲。  
He did not understand why he absolutely had to leave his mother.  
太后也哭了。  
The Queen Dowager cried too.  
叔段一直是她的心肝宝贝。  
Duan had always been the apple of her eye（心肝宝贝）.  
从没有离开过她半步。  
He had never been away from her for even half a step.  
临走前，她一遍遍叮嘱奶娘和师傅。  
Before he left, she repeatedly instructed the wet nurses and tutors.  
要好好照顾叔段。  
They were to take good care of Duan.  
宫里最老成的宫女。  
The most experienced palace maids,  
最好的被褥衣服。  
the finest bedding and clothes,  
连夜壶和厨子。  
even the best chamber pot and her favorite cook,  
她都一股脑给了叔段。  
she bundled all of them off with Duan.  
庄公也送了弟弟一颗夜明珠。  
Duke Zhuang also sent his younger brother a luminous pearl（夜明珠）.  
只是他没有亲自来送。  
He just did not come in person.  
因为他知道，母亲这时候看见自己只会更不高兴。  
He knew that if his mother saw him at a moment like this, she would only be more displeased（不高兴的）.  
叔段走后很长一段时间。  
For a long time after Duan left,  
太后心情都很差。  
the Queen Dowager remained in a foul mood.  
她脾气越来越大。  
Her temper grew worse and worse.  
宫女常常挨罚。  
Palace maids were often punished.  
谁都知道，她是在想叔段。  
Everyone knew it was because she missed Duan.  
隔三岔五，她就派人往京城送东西。  
Every few days, she sent someone to Jingcheng with gifts.  
等人回来，又要把叔段近况听上一遍又一遍。  
When the messengers returned, she made them recount Duan's condition again and again.  
听说叔段过得好时，她才稍微安心一点。  
Only when she heard that Duan was doing well did she feel a little calmer.  
可日子一长，她整个人都变得忧郁了。  
But as time dragged on, she herself grew gloomy（忧郁的）.  
喜怒无常。  
Her moods turned erratic（反复无常的）.  
用今天的话说，大概就是抑郁了。  
In modern terms, one might say she had grown depressed（抑郁的）.  
而她一见庄公就更烦。  
And whenever she saw Duke Zhuang, she felt even more irritated.  
她常想。  
She often thought to herself,  
要是当国君的是段就好了。  
If only Duan had been the ruler.  
那样自己就能天天见到他了。  
Then she could have seen him every day.  
哪像现在。  
Unlike now,  
天天只看见这个让自己心烦的大儿子。  
when she saw only the elder son who made her miserable.  
庄公其实也想哄母亲开心。  
Duke Zhuang did in fact try to cheer his mother up.  
可不管他做什么。  
But no matter what he did,  
太后总能挑出毛病来。  
the Queen Dowager could always find fault（挑毛病） with it.  
女人一旦打心眼里不喜欢一个人。  
Once a woman truly dislikes someone from the bottom of her heart,  
对方做什么都不顺眼。  
nothing that person does will ever look right.

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`条件状语从句`、`宾语从句`、`倒装`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `Only when she heard that Duan was doing well did she feel a little calmer`。<br>
难点拆解：`Only when ...` 放句首触发部分倒装；`that Duan was doing well` 是宾语从句；最后 `Once a woman truly dislikes ...` 又是条件状语从句，用来概括她后来的心理状态。<br>
常用短语：
- `the apple of one's eye`：掌上明珠，心肝宝贝
- `find fault with`：挑……的毛病
- `from the bottom of one's heart`：打心底里
- `no matter what`：无论什么

### 庄公的算盘
### Duke Zhuang's Calculations

虽然只有十三岁。  
Though he was only thirteen,  
庄公心里其实什么都明白。  
Duke Zhuang understood everything in his heart.  
父亲武公长期在周朝做上卿。  
His father, Duke Wu, had long served at Zhou as a chief minister.  
斗争经验非常丰富。  
He had immense experience in political struggle（斗争）.  
每次回家。  
Whenever he came home,  
都会把朝廷里的事讲给大儿子听。  
he told his eldest son about affairs at court.  
告诉他中央的斗争。  
He taught him that struggles at the center  
比地方上更激烈，也更残酷。  
were fiercer and more brutal than local ones.  
从道理到案例，都讲得很细。  
From general principles to actual cases, he explained everything in detail.  
那时庄公虽然年纪小。  
Duke Zhuang had been young at the time.  
却把这些都记下了。  
But he remembered all of it.  
如今父亲死了。  
Now his father was dead.  
轮到他自己把这些教导用起来了。  
And it was his turn to put those lessons to use.  
母亲来给段要京城时。  
When his mother came asking Jingcheng for Duan,  
他为什么一口答应？  
why did he agree so readily?  
因为他算得很清楚。  
Because he had calculated matters very clearly.  
母亲只是个女人。  
His mother was, in his judgment, only a woman.  
主意未必多稳。  
Her plans were not necessarily steady.  
弟弟也才十岁。  
And his younger brother was only ten.  
一个女人加一个孩子。  
One woman and one child,  
就算拿到了京城，又能成多大气候？  
even with Jingcheng in hand, how much of a force could they truly become?  
可如果不给。  
But if he refused,  
麻烦就大了。  
the trouble would be enormous.  
母亲一定不会善罢甘休。  
His mother would never let the matter rest.  
天天闹事还是小事。  
Daily scenes would be the lesser problem.  
最怕的是她被逼急了。  
What he feared most was that she might be pushed too far.  
说不定哪天就给自己下毒。  
She might poison him one day when he least expected it.  
到时候自己怎么死都不知道。  
Then he might die without even knowing how it happened.  
可把京城给了段以后。  
But once Jingcheng was given to Duan,  
母亲便没什么理由再闹。  
his mother would have less reason to keep making trouble.  
她再不喜欢自己。  
No matter how much she disliked him,  
不到万不得已，大概也不会害亲儿子。  
she would probably not harm her own son unless driven to extremes.  
所以这块地，他给得很痛快。  
That was why he gave away the city so readily.  
段走以后，庄公开始做两手准备。  
After Duan left, Duke Zhuang began making two sets of preparations.  
第一手，是感化。  
The first was moral persuasion（感化）.  
他常去看太后。  
He frequently visited the Queen Dowager.  
也常给弟弟送好东西。  
He also often sent good things to his brother.  
他想，毕竟是亲娘和亲弟弟。  
He thought that after all, they were his own mother and brother.  
要是能把关系慢慢捂热。  
If he could slowly warm the relationship back up,  
一家人和和气气，不也挺好吗？  
would it not be better for the whole family to live in peace?  
第二手，是防备。  
The second was precaution（防备）.  
他在京城安插了自己的人。  
He planted his own people in Jingcheng.  
严密监视叔段的一举一动。  
They closely watched Duan's every move.  
同时，他重用祭足和公子吕。  
At the same time, he placed great trust in Jizu and Prince Lü.  
前者管国家。  
The former ran the state.  
后者练军队。  
The latter trained the army.  
闲下来时，还会找周边小国练练兵。  
When there was spare time, they even used nearby small states for military drills.  
好让郑国始终保持战斗力。  
That way Zheng would keep its fighting strength intact（完整的）.  
可随着太后越来越厌恶自己。  
But as the Queen Dowager grew more and more hostile（敌意的） toward him,  
庄公也越来越明白。  
Duke Zhuang understood more and more clearly  
第一手大概是没希望了。  
that the first approach was probably hopeless.  
今后的重心，只能放在第二手上。  
From then on, the main weight would have to fall on the second.

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`原因状语从句`、`条件状语从句`、`反问句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `Because he had calculated matters very clearly, he gave away the city so readily`。<br>
难点拆解：`Because ...` 先交代庄公的思路；`If he could slowly warm the relationship back up ...` 是条件状语从句；`would it not be better ...?` 用反问表达“这样当然更好”。<br>
常用短语：
- `let the matter rest`：把事情放下，不再闹
- `when one least expects it`：在最想不到的时候
- `place great trust in`：非常信任……
- `fall on`：落到……上

### 叔段的行动
### Duan Makes His Move

十岁时，叔段什么都不懂。  
At ten, Duan understood nothing.  
刚到京城时。  
When he first arrived in Jingcheng,  
他连一个人睡都不敢。  
he did not even dare sleep alone.  
每天都要挤着奶娘睡。  
Every night he crowded in beside his wet nurse.  
起初，太后派来的人总说她很想他。  
At first, the people the Queen Dowager sent simply said that she missed him terribly.  
后来，这些人开始抱怨庄公总让她生气。  
Later, they began to complain that Duke Zhuang always upset her.  
再后来，传来的话几乎就全是在骂庄公了。  
Later still, the messages were almost entirely curses against Duke Zhuang.  
叔段一开始还挺想哥哥。  
At first Duan still missed his elder brother.  
慢慢地，就不想了。  
Gradually he stopped.  
再往后，甚至开始恨他。  
After that, he even began to hate him.  
等到十八岁。  
By the time he was eighteen,  
他已经恨庄公恨得很深。  
his hatred of Duke Zhuang ran deep.  
他相信哥哥对母亲不好。  
He believed his brother treated their mother badly.  
简直禽兽不如。  
He believed him scarcely better than a beast.  
而哥哥对自己的好。  
And his brother's kindness toward him,  
也不过是糖衣炮弹。  
he took as nothing but sugared bullets（糖衣炮弹）.  
表面在笑，心里却藏着刀。  
There was a smile on the surface and a knife underneath.  
他认定，哥哥早晚会除掉自己。  
He was convinced that sooner or later his brother would eliminate（除掉） him.  
于是，叔段决定动起来。  
So Duan decided to make his move.  
他说自己要把母亲从水深火热里救出来。  
He told himself that he would rescue his mother from misery.  
京城北面有北鄙，西面有西鄙。  
To the north of Jingcheng lay the Northern Border towns, and to the west the Western Border towns.  
这两座城原本都不归京城管。  
Neither of these cities originally belonged to Jingcheng.  
叔段却打定主意，要把它们划到自己名下。  
Duan resolved to bring them under his own authority（管辖）.  
这算是投石问路。  
It was a way of testing the waters（投石问路）.  
他把两城的守官召来。  
He summoned the officials of both cities.  
一拍桌子就说。  
Slapping the table, he declared,  
从今以后，你们归我管。  
"From today on, you answer to me.  
赋税都交到京城来。  
All taxes are to be sent to Jingcheng.  
不用再交给荥阳了。"  
You no longer send them to Xingyang."  
谁都知道太后最宠叔段。  
Everyone knew that the Queen Dowager doted on Duan above all else.  
谁也都知道，只要是太后的要求。
