And everyone also knew that once it was something the Queen Dowager wanted,  
庄公多半不会硬顶。  
Duke Zhuang was unlikely to resist openly.  
两城官员摸摸乌纱帽。  
The officials touched their hats.  
又摸摸自己的脖子。  
Then they touched their necks.  
荥阳虽是正统。  
Xingyang might be the legitimate seat,  
可离得远。  
but it was far away.  
京城虽是越权。  
Jingcheng might be overstepping,  
可离得近。  
but it was near.  
除了服从，他们也没别的路。  
They had no road left but obedience（服从）.  
整个郑国很快都知道了这件事。  
Before long, all Zheng knew what had happened.  
庄公当然也知道。  
Duke Zhuang naturally knew as well.  
他还特地去见了一趟母亲。  
He even made a special visit to his mother.  
太后本来已经准备好大吵一场。  
The Queen Dowager had already prepared for a huge quarrel.  
她以为大儿子是来兴师问罪的。  
She assumed her eldest son had come to accuse her.  
没想到庄公绝口不提叔段。  
To her surprise, Duke Zhuang did not mention Duan at all.  
只简单问候几句，就走了。  
He exchanged only a few polite words and left.  
叔段也在等。  
Duan was waiting too.  
他等哥哥来责备自己。  
He waited for his brother to rebuke him.  
然后自己再认个错，把赋税还回去。  
Then he could pretend repentance and return the taxes.  
可庄公像什么都没发生一样。  
But Duke Zhuang behaved as if nothing had happened.  
不久，太后给叔段写来一封信。  
Not long after, the Queen Dowager sent Duan a letter.  
信里只有一句意思。  
Its message was simple.  
放手干，娘给你撑腰。  
Act boldly; your mother stands behind you.  
两年过去，叔段觉得粮草攒得差不多了。  
Two years passed, and Duan felt he had amassed enough grain and supplies（粮草）.  
可城再大，没有军队也不行。  
But no matter how large a city is, it cannot do much without an army.  
于是他开始借打猎的名义聚集人马。  
So he began gathering men and horses under the pretext（借口） of hunting.  
既聚人，也练兵。  
He gathered troops and drilled them at the same time.  
整个郑国都看得明明白白。  
The whole state of Zheng could see it clearly.  
叔段这是在整军备战。  
Duan was openly preparing for war.  
庄公当然也知道。  
Duke Zhuang certainly knew.  
可这一次，他连母亲那里都没去。  
But this time he did not even go to speak to his mother.  
太后很高兴。  
The Queen Dowager was delighted.  
她以为大儿子不是软弱，就是愚蠢。  
She took it as proof that her eldest son was either weak or foolish.  
叔段也很高兴。  
Duan was delighted too.  
他以为哥哥不是怕母亲，就是怕自己。  
He assumed his brother feared either their mother or him.  
庄公却只在心里对自己说了一个字。  
Yet Duke Zhuang said only one word to himself.  
忍。  
Endure.

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`宾语从句`、`时间状语从句`、`比较结构`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `everyone also knew that once it was something the Queen Dowager wanted, Duke Zhuang was unlikely to resist openly`。<br>
难点拆解：`that ...` 是宾语从句；`once it was something ... wanted` 表示时间和条件；后面的 `either ... or ...` 则是典型比较选择结构，用来写人物误判。<br>
常用短语：
- `make one's move`：开始行动
- `under the pretext of`：打着……的名义
- `act boldly`：放手去干
- `be unlikely to do`：不太可能做……

### 公子吕的抱怨
### Prince Lü's Complaint

庄公在忍。  
Duke Zhuang was enduring.  
可大夫们已经看不下去了。  
But the ministers could no longer bear to watch.  
公子吕去找祭足。  
Prince Lü went to see Jizu.  
他说，叔段欺人太甚。  
He said Duan had gone too far.  
这明摆着就是要谋反。  
It was obviously a rebellion（谋反） in the making.  
你是顾命大臣。  
You are a regent minister entrusted by the late ruler.  
总该去提醒主公一声吧。  
Surely you should warn our lord.  
祭足却笑了。  
Jizu only smiled.  
他说，不是自己不想说。  
He said it was not that he did not want to speak.  
而是他也在忍。  
He too was enduring.  
公子吕听不懂。  
Prince Lü did not understand.  
祭足就说。  
So Jizu told him  
你是庄公的叔叔。  
that since he was Duke Zhuang's uncle,  
不如亲自去问主公。  
he might as well ask the ruler himself.  
于是公子吕真的去了。  
So Prince Lü really did go.  
庄公热情接待了叔叔。  
Duke Zhuang received his uncle warmly.  
公子吕说话却不客气。  
But Prince Lü spoke bluntly（不客气地）.  
一个国家不能有两个权力中心。  
A state cannot endure two centers of power.  
你要是想把国家让给叔段。  
If you intend to give the state to Duan,  
那我现在就去投他。  
then I will go serve him right now.  
你要是不想给。  
But if you do not intend that,  
那就该早点把他除掉。  
then you should eliminate him sooner rather than later.  
总这样拖着。  
If you keep dragging things out like this,  
民心都会被拖散。  
the people's confidence will fall apart.  
庄公听完，只是笑。  
Duke Zhuang only smiled after hearing this.  
他说，叔叔别急。  
He said his uncle should not be anxious.  
自己心里有数。  
He knew exactly what he was doing.  
这一下，公子吕就明白了。  
At that point Prince Lü understood.  
庄公一直都在等。  
Duke Zhuang had been waiting all along.  
而且他等得很稳。  
And he had waited with remarkable composure（沉稳）.

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`条件状语从句`、`宾语从句`、`并列句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `If you do not intend that, then you should eliminate him sooner rather than later`。<br>
难点拆解：前面两个 `If ...` 都是条件状语从句；`He said ...` 和 `he knew exactly what he was doing` 里都含宾语从句信息；整段对话用并列句推动，很像戏剧对白。<br>
常用短语：
- `bear to watch`：忍心看下去
- `sooner rather than later`：宜早不宜迟
- `fall apart`：散掉，垮掉
- `all along`：一直以来

### 万事俱备
### Everything Is Ready

庄公忍到什么时候。  
How long Duke Zhuang would endure  
其实取决于叔段想走到哪一步。  
depended entirely on how far Duan meant to go.  
现在，叔段终于决定动手了。  
Now Duan had finally decided to act.  
他先出兵占了鄢和禀延。  
He first sent troops to seize Yan and Binyan.  
又把当地官员赶走。  
Then he expelled the local officials.  
整个郑国都震动了。  
The whole state of Zheng was shocked（震惊的）.  
要说抢两座边城。  
Seizing two border cities  
还勉强能算试探。  
might barely be called a probe.  
可现在连地方行政都改掉了。  
But now he had even replaced local administration.  
这就是赤裸裸地造反。  
That was open rebellion（公开叛乱）.  
庄公却还是不急。  
Duke Zhuang still did not hurry.  
他故意放出风声。  
He deliberately let it be known  
说自己准备去东周都城洛邑一趟。  
that he planned to make a trip to Luoyi, the Eastern Zhou capital.  
毕竟他还继承了父亲的大周上卿之职。  
After all, he had inherited his father's high office at Zhou.  
偶尔去首都转转，也说得过去。  
It was reasonable for him to visit the capital from time to time.  
太后听了非常高兴。  
The Queen Dowager was delighted when she heard this.  
她立刻派人给叔段送信。  
She immediately sent a messenger to Duan.  
约好在庄公离开的那一天。  
They agreed that on the very day Duke Zhuang left,  
叔段便出兵攻打荥阳。  
Duan would attack Xingyang.  
她自己则在城中做内应。  
She herself would serve as an inside accomplice（内应）.  
这下真是万事俱备。  
At last, everything seemed ready.  
只欠东风了。  
Only the east wind was lacking.  
可太后还是太不了解她的大儿子。  
But the Queen Dowager still understood her elder son far too little.  
送信的人刚出城，就被抓了。  
The messenger was seized the moment he left the city.  
身上的书信也被搜了出来。  
The letter on him was found as well.  
很快就送到了庄公手里。  
It was quickly delivered to Duke Zhuang.  
庄公看完，只说了两个字。  
After reading it, Duke Zhuang said only two words.  
可矣。  
That will do.  
一切都在他掌握之中。  
Everything was within his grasp（掌控）.  
这一次，万事真的俱备了。  
This time, everything truly was ready.  
而东风，也正好吹到了他这一边。  
And the east wind was now blowing on his side.

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`宾语从句`、`时间状语从句`、`并列句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `They agreed that on the very day Duke Zhuang left, Duan would attack Xingyang`。<br>
难点拆解：`that ...` 是宾语从句；`on the very day ... left` 把时间钉得很死；后面一连串并列句把“密信被截、庄公早有准备”的节奏连续推高。<br>
常用短语：
- `let it be known`：放出风声
- `from time to time`：偶尔，不时
- `the moment ...`：一……就……
- `within one's grasp`：在某人的掌控之中

### 解决问题
### Settling the Matter

郑庄公二十二年，也就是前722年。  
In the twenty-second year of Duke Zhuang, that is, 722 BCE,  
庄公三十五岁。  
Duke Zhuang was thirty-five.  
叔段三十二岁。  
Duan was thirty-two.  
这一年，叔段亲自率二百乘战车出京城。  
That year, Duan personally led two hundred chariots out of Jingcheng.  
浩浩荡荡向荥阳而去。  
In grand array, he marched toward Xingyang.  
在他看来。  
In his view,  
只要今天里应外合成功。  
once inside and outside forces joined successfully that day,  
哥哥就会永远留在周朝首都了。  
his elder brother would be kept away forever at the Zhou capital.  
他没想到的是。  
What he did not realize  
这个日子其实不是太后定的。  
was that the day had not really been fixed by the Queen Dowager.  
而是哥哥替他定好的。  
It had been fixed for him by his brother.  
哥哥早把一切都准备好了。  
His brother had prepared everything in advance.  
就等弟弟自己撞上来。  
He had merely been waiting for his younger brother to walk into it.  
叔段的大军刚出京城东门。  
As soon as Duan's army left the east gate of Jingcheng,  
公子吕的军队就从西门进了城。  
Prince Lü's troops entered through the west gate.  
等叔段知道老窝被端，慌忙回师时。  
By the time Duan learned his base had been taken and hurried to turn back,  
公子吕已经追到了眼前。  
Prince Lü had already caught up with him.  
两军对阵。  
The two armies drew up against each other.  
双方各有二百乘战车。  
Each side had two hundred chariots.  
公子吕先大喝一声。  
Prince Lü shouted first.  
他说，叔段谋反。  
He declared that Duan was in rebellion.  
自己奉郑侯之命讨逆。  
He himself had come by order of the Duke of Zheng to suppress the traitor.  
对面的人若是识相。  
If the men opposite had any sense,  
就地解散，各回各家。  
they should disband at once and go home.  
话音刚落。  
The words had barely left his mouth  
叔段那边居然响起一阵轻松的欢呼。  
when a burst of relieved cheering broke out on Duan's side.  
士兵们纷纷下车卸甲。  
The soldiers climbed down and took off their armor.  
拍拍屁股就回家了。  
Then they dusted themselves off and went home.  
从大道理上说。  
On the level of principle,  
公子吕代表的是合法政权。  
Prince Lü represented the legitimate government.  
从小道理上说。  
On the level of ordinary feeling,  
叔段手下大多是京城人。  
most of Duan's men came from Jingcheng.  
老窝都被占了。  
Their homes had been taken.  
谁还不赶紧回去看看老婆孩子？  
Who would not hurry back to check on wife and children?  
叔段一下就傻了。  
Duan was stunned at once.  
这仗还怎么打？  
How could this battle possibly be fought?  
他只好带着十几个随从狼狈逃走。  
He could only flee in disarray（狼狈地） with a dozen attendants.  
先逃到鄢。  
He first fled to Yan.  
公子吕立刻追到鄢。  
Prince Lü immediately pursued him there.  
叔段只好再逃。  
Duan had to flee again.  
一路逃得连撒尿都只能撒一半。  
He fled in such haste that even urinating had to be done in halves.  
最后他逃到共城。  
At last he reached Gongcheng.  
共城不大。  
Gongcheng was not large.  
但城还算坚固。  
But its defenses were solid enough.  
叔段终于喘了口气。  
At last Duan caught his breath.  
还把剩下那半泡尿解决了。  
He even finished the other half of the urine he had been holding.  
躺下之后，他忽然有些恍惚。  
After lying down, he suddenly felt dazed（恍惚的）.  
自己苦心经营二十年。  
He had spent twenty years building this cause.  
一夜之间，全没了。  
In a single night, it had all vanished.  
早知如此。  
Had he known it would come to this,  
当初老老实实待在共城，未必不好。  
he might have been better off simply staying quietly in Gongcheng from the start.  
城虽小。  
The city might have been small.  
可也够吃够穿。  
But it had enough to eat and enough to wear.  
至少不用落得今天这样。  
At least he would not have been reduced to this state today.  
可他很快又觉得奇怪。  
But soon he found something strange.  
公子吕的大军已经围住共城。  
Prince Lü's army had already encircled Gongcheng.  
却一直不攻城。  
Yet it did not attack.  
也不来劝降。  
Nor did it send anyone to urge surrender.  
叔段起初想不明白。  
At first Duan could not make sense of it.  
等了两个时辰以后。  
After waiting two hours,  
他忽然全明白了。  
he suddenly understood everything.  
哥哥不是不能杀他。  
It was not that his brother could not kill him.  
杀他其实容易得很。  
Killing him would in fact have been very easy.  
哥哥只是不想落下手刃亲弟的恶名。  
His brother simply did not want the infamy（恶名） of killing his own brother with his own hand.  
可哥哥也绝不会放他走。  
But his brother would never let him escape either.  
摆在他面前的路只剩两条。  
Only two roads remained before him.  
要么上吊。  
Either he hanged himself.  
要么自刎。  
Or he cut his own throat.  
叔段最后选了上吊。  
In the end, Duan chose hanging.  
死前，他叫来两个心腹。  
Before dying, he called two trusted attendants.  
他说，自己死后。  
He said that after his death,  
要他们带着儿子滑去卫国。  
they were to take his son Hua to the state of Wei.  
去投奔公子州吁。  
There they should seek shelter with Prince Zhouxu.  
那是他的朋友。  
Zhouxu was his friend.  
随从问，公子吕怎么会放他们走？  
The attendants asked how Prince Lü could possibly let them leave.  
叔段却说，会的。  
Duan said that he would.  
哥哥既然不肯亲手杀弟弟。  
Since his brother refused to kill his own younger brother by hand,  
更不会去杀一个年幼的侄子。  
he would be still less willing to kill a young nephew.  
把孩子放走。  
Letting the boy go  
既没有大后患。  
brought no great future trouble,  
还能显得自己宽宏大量。  
and would also make him seem magnanimous（宽宏大量的）.  
叔段把这一层，看得很明白。  
Duan saw this part very clearly.  
于是，叔段就这样死在了郑国。  
And so Duan died in Zheng.

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`时间状语从句`、`原因状语从句`、`比较结构`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `Since his brother refused to kill his own younger brother by hand, he would be still less willing to kill a young nephew`。<br>
难点拆解：`Since ... refused ...` 是原因状语从句；`be still less willing to ...` 是很典型的比较结构；整段前面还有 `As soon as ...`、`By the time ...` 这类时间状语从句，把追击过程写得很紧。<br>
常用短语：
- `in advance`：提前
- `draw up against each other`：列阵相对
- `at least`：至少
- `seek shelter with`：去投奔……

这里顺便说一句。  
A short explanation should be added here.  
姓段的人，大多把叔段看作祖先。  
Many people with the surname Duan regard him as an ancestor.  
为什么他的后代会姓段？  
Why did his descendants end up using Duan as a surname?  
周代时，姓和氏不是一回事。  
In the Zhou period, *xing* and *shi* were not the same thing.  
姬、姜、子这些，叫姓。  
Names like Ji, Jiang, and Zi were clan names.  
氏则常常来自三种来源。  
A lineage name, by contrast, usually came from three sources.  
可以来自父亲或祖父的字。  
It might come from a father's or grandfather's courtesy name.  
也可以来自封邑地名。  
It might come from the name of a fief.  
还可以来自祖上显赫的官职。  
Or it might come from a prominent ancestral office.  
周王的儿子叫王子。  
A Zhou king's son was called a royal prince.  
王子的儿子叫王孙。  
His son was called a royal grandson.  
诸侯的儿子叫公子。  
A feudal lord's son was called a prince of the state.  
公子的儿子叫公孙。  
That prince's son was called a grandson of the ducal house.  
叔段原本也可以叫公子段。  
Duan could originally have been called Prince Duan.  
可因为他的行为不像个体面的公子。  
But because his conduct did not suit a proper prince,  
《左传》就干脆叫他叔段。  
the *Zuo Commentary* simply calls him Shuduan.  
按五世亲尽的规矩。  
By the rule that kinship thins out after five generations,  
没能继位的公子后裔。  
the descendants of princes who did not inherit the throne  
往下传几代后，就得另立氏了。  
had to establish a separate lineage name after several generations.  
通常到了公孙的儿子这一代。  
Usually by the generation of a grandson's son,  
就会拿祖父的名字来作氏。  
the grandfather's personal name became the lineage name.  
所以公孙滑的儿子们。  
That is why the sons of Gongsun Hua  
后来就以段为氏。  
later took Duan as their family name.

### 郑庄公的愤怒
### Duke Zhuang's Anger

共城的城门后来打开了。  
Later the gates of Gongcheng opened.  
因为叔段已经死了。  
That was because Duan was already dead.  
庄公其实早就到了。  
Duke Zhuang had in fact arrived long before.  
他进城后，直接去了叔段的住处。  
After entering the city, he went straight to Duan's residence.  
叔段躺在床上，已经断气。  
Duan lay on the bed, no longer breathing.  
说心里话。  
To speak honestly,  
庄公其实一直很喜欢这个弟弟。  
Duke Zhuang had always been fond of this younger brother.  
他也只有这一个同母弟弟。  
He had only this one full brother from the same mother.  
弟弟聪明、英俊，又有礼貌。  
Duan had been clever, handsome, and well-mannered.  
庄公常常会想起他。  
Duke Zhuang often thought of him.  
可如今再见面。  
But now, when they met again,  
竟已是最后一面。  
it turned out to be the last.  
庄公扑到弟弟身上大哭。  
Duke Zhuang threw himself over his brother's body and burst into tears.  
这眼泪不是装出来的。  
Those tears were not an act.  
那一刻，他是真伤心了。  
In that moment, he was truly grieved.  
不管此前多冷静、多能忍。  
No matter how calm or restrained he had been before,  
亲眼看见弟弟的尸体。  
once he saw his brother's corpse with his own eyes,  
他还是忍不住悲从中来。  
he still could not stop grief from rising in him.  
他问近侍，叔段临死前说了什么。  
He asked the attendants what Duan had said before he died.  
近侍回答。  
They answered  
叔段说，是娘害了他。  
that Duan had said it was their mother who ruined him.  
庄公听后，只点了点头。  
After hearing this, Duke Zhuang only nodded.  
他知道，弟弟临死前终于想明白了。  
He knew that at the end, his brother had finally understood.  
可想到这里，他更怒了。  
But that understanding only made him angrier.  
他心里对母亲说。  
In his heart he said to his mother,  
你害死了自己的小儿子。  
You killed your younger son.  
也逼得大儿子背上杀弟的名声。  
And you forced your elder son to bear the name of fratricide（杀弟之名）.  
你算什么母亲？  
What kind of mother are you?  
这一刻，庄公终于忍到了头。  
At that moment, Duke Zhuang had finally reached the limit of endurance.  
忍到了尽头，也就再没有什么可忍的了。  
Once endurance reaches its limit, nothing remains to be endured.

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`宾语从句`、`时间状语从句`、`被动表达`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `He knew that at the end, his brother had finally understood`。<br>
难点拆解：`that ... had finally understood` 是宾语从句；`once he saw ...` 和 `At that moment ...` 都是在交代时间触发点；最后 `to be endured` 是被动意义的不定式，让结尾更凝练。<br>
常用短语：
- `be fond of`：喜欢……
- `burst into tears`：放声大哭
- `at the end`：到最后
- `reach the limit of`：达到……的极限

## 第七章 黄泉相见
## Chapter 7: Meeting at the Yellow Springs

关于庄公和叔段这一段。  
Most historical accounts tell the story of Duke Zhuang and Duan in roughly the same way.  
史书的口径其实很一致。  
Their judgment is surprisingly consistent（一致的）.  
都说郑庄公故意纵着弟弟。  
They all say that Duke Zhuang of Zheng deliberately indulged（纵容） his younger brother.  
最后又一步步逼死了他。  
And then pushed him, step by step, toward death.  
这种说法，其实并不公道。  
That version of the story is not really fair.  
庄公给过叔段很长时间。  
Duke Zhuang gave Duan a very long stretch of time.  
让他收手，也让他回头。  
He gave him time to stop and time to turn back.  
二十二年，还不够长吗？  
Was twenty-two years not long enough?  
如果庄公真想杀弟弟。  
If Duke Zhuang had truly wanted his brother dead,  
难道还用苦等二十二年找理由？  
would he really have needed to wait twenty-two years for an excuse?  
根子上的问题，不在叔段一人。  
The root of the trouble did not lie in Duan alone.  
更在庄公那个古怪的母亲。  
It lay even more in Duke Zhuang's distorted（扭曲的） mother.  
她能因为和儿子无关的事。  
Over something that had nothing to do with her son,  
把亲生儿子恨到那个地步。  
she could still hate her own child to such an extent（程度）.  
庄公其实没什么可选的。  
Duke Zhuang, in truth, had almost no real choice.  
他一直在让。  
He kept giving way.  
一直让到退无可退。  
He kept yielding until there was nowhere left to retreat.  
没错，他一直在装。  
Yes, he was always pretending.  
可那些伪装，说到底都是自保。  
But in the final analysis（归根结底）, every layer of disguise was self-protection.  
作者还开了个玩笑。  
The author even makes a joke about it.  
庄公为什么叫庄公？  
Why was he called Duke Zhuang?  
因为这一局里，他才是坐庄的人。  
Because in this whole game, he was the one holding the bank（坐庄）.  
叔段不过是个散户。  
Duan was nothing more than a small retail player（散户）.  
散户怎么斗得过庄家？  
How could a lone small player ever beat the house?  
再说到“忍”这个字。  
And then there was that single word: endure.  
真要给“忍”找位祖师爷。  
If one were to name a founding master（祖师爷） of endurance,  
郑庄公大概跑不了。  
Duke Zhuang of Zheng would be hard to leave off the list.

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`条件状语从句`、`宾语从句`、`反问句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `If Duke Zhuang had truly wanted his brother dead, would he really have needed to wait twenty-two years for an excuse?`。<br>
难点拆解：前半句是条件状语从句；后半句用反问句，不是在真的发问，而是在强调“根本没这个必要”；`They all say that ...` 里又带着宾语从句，用来概括史书观点。<br>
常用短语：
- `step by step`：一步一步地
- `to such an extent`：到这种程度
- `in the final analysis`：归根结底
- `give way`：退让，让步

### 黄泉之约
### The Oath of the Yellow Springs

太后在后宫里急得团团转。  
The Queen Dowager was pacing anxiously（焦急地） in the inner palace.  
她原本和叔段约好了里应外合。  
She had arranged with Duan to work from inside and outside together.  
可宫门已经全被关死。  
But the palace gates had all been sealed shut.  
外头的卫士守得严严实实。  
Guards stood watch outside with absolute strictness（严密）.  
这时候，谁也别想进出。  
At that point, no one was getting in or out.  
不管你是太后，还是公主。  
It did not matter whether you were the Queen Dowager or a princess.  
太后心里隐约觉得不妙。  
Deep down, the Queen Dowager sensed that something was wrong.  
而且越想越慌。  
The more she thought, the more frightened（慌乱的） she became.  
终于，宫门开了。  
At last, the palace gate opened.  
进来的却不是叔段。  
But the man who entered was not Duan.  
来的人，是公子吕。  
It was Prince Lü.  
公子吕拿出一大堆绢帛。  
Prince Lü took out a whole pile of silk letters.  
那时写信，用的就是绢。  
In those days, silk was used for writing letters.  
这本来就已经够奢侈了。  
That alone was already luxurious（奢侈的） enough.  
太后一看，整个人都僵住了。  
The instant the Queen Dowager saw them, she went rigid（僵住的）.  
那全是她写给叔段的信。  
They were all letters she had written to Duan.  
信里写满了辱骂庄公的话。  
They were filled with abuse directed at Duke Zhuang.  
也写满了煽动叔段造反的话。  
They were also full of incitement（煽动） urging Duan to rebel.  
到了这一步，她终于明白。  
At that moment, she finally understood  
自己是看错了大儿子。  
that she had completely misjudged her eldest son.  
她颤着声音问了一句。  
In a trembling voice, she asked only one question.  
“段呢？”  
"Where is Duan?"  
公子吕冷冷答道。  
Prince Lü answered coldly,  
“死了。”  
"Dead."  
太后又问。  
She asked again,  
“怎么死的？”  
"How did he die?"  
“上吊自尽。”  
"He hanged himself."  
“死前说了什么？”  
"What did he say before he died?"  
“他说，是你害了他。”  
"He said you were the one who destroyed him."  
太后当场哭了出来。  
The Queen Dowager burst into tears on the spot.  
她一边哭，一边承认。  
As she wept, she admitted it herself.  
说到底，真是自己害死了儿子。  
In the end, it truly was she who had killed her son.  
一直到这个时候。  
Only at that point  
她才真正明白自己错在哪里。  
did she truly understand where she had gone wrong.  
随后她又问起庄公。  
Then she asked about Duke Zhuang.  
公子吕说，主公没脸来见你。  
Prince Lü said that his lord could not bear to face her.  
所以请你搬去颍地居住。  
So she was to move to Ying and live there.  
太后低声说。  
In a low voice, the Queen Dowager said  
不是他没脸见我。  
that it was not he who lacked the face to meet her.  
是我没脸见他。  
It was she who had no face to meet him.  
她又问，庄公还说了什么。  
She then asked what else Duke Zhuang had said.  
公子吕转述了那句重话。  
Prince Lü repeated the harsh words.  
“不及黄泉，无相见也。”  
"Until Yellow Springs are reached, there shall be no meeting between us."  
换成今天的话。  
In modern language,  
就是到死也别再见了。  
it meant: we will not see each other again until death.  
悲痛、羞愧、自责，全压了上来。  
Grief, shame, and self-reproach（自责） crashed down on her all at once.  
于是太后上路，去了颍。  
So the Queen Dowager set out for Ying.

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`宾语从句`、`时间状语从句`、`倒装`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `Only at that point did she truly understand where she had gone wrong`。<br>
难点拆解：`Only at that point` 放在句首，会触发部分倒装；`where she had gone wrong` 是宾语从句；前面多组短促问答本身又形成时间推进，让情绪一层层压上来。<br>
常用短语：
- `burst into tears`：当场哭出来
- `have no face to do`：没脸做……
- `all at once`：一下子，同时
- `set out for`：动身前往……

### 郑庄公的烦恼
### Duke Zhuang's Trouble

叔段死了。  
Duan was dead.  
太后也被送去了颍。  
And the Queen Dowager had been sent away to Ying.  
照理说，庄公该能睡个安稳觉了。  
By all logic（按理说）, Duke Zhuang ought finally to have slept soundly.  
那种芒刺在背的感觉。  
That feeling of having a thorn in his back  
总算该消失了。  
should at last have disappeared.  
可怪就怪在这里。  
But this was exactly the strange part.  
他反而睡不着了。  
He could not sleep anymore.  
有些人斗争惯了。  
Some people become so used to struggle  
没了斗争，反倒不习惯。  
that they feel unsteady once it is gone.  
可庄公后来发现。  
But Duke Zhuang later discovered  
自己更大的烦恼还不在这里。  
that his greater trouble lay elsewhere.  
他把亲娘赶走了。  
He had driven away his own mother.  
哪怕母亲再不对。  
No matter how wrong she had been,  
那也是亲娘。  
she was still his mother.  
想到这一层，庄公就后悔。  
When he thought of that, Duke Zhuang regretted it.  
就像他当初也后悔逼死弟弟一样。  
Just as he had once regretted driving his brother to death.  
于是他心里越来越苦。  
So his heart grew heavier and heavier.  
常常借酒浇愁。  
He often tried to drown his sorrow（愁苦） in wine.  
喝多了，还会唱歌。  
When he drank too much, he even sang.  
至于唱的是什么。  
As for what he sang,  
今天已经没人知道了。  
no one knows anymore.  
大概就是那种。  
It was probably the sort of thing  
有娘的孩子最好，没娘的孩子像草。  
that says a child with a mother is blessed, while one without her is like grass.  
他其实很想把太后接回来。  
He truly wanted to bring the Queen Dowager back.  
可偏偏自己发过誓。  
But he had made an oath.  
而且古人把誓言看得很重。  
And the ancients treated oaths with great seriousness（严肃）.  
因为他们敬畏鬼神。  
They stood in awe of spirits and gods.  
发了誓，就不敢随便反悔。  
Once a vow was made, they did not dare break it lightly.  
庄公于是陷进了死结。  
So Duke Zhuang found himself trapped in a dead knot（死结）.  
越没办法，越想办法。  
The less he could solve it, the more he obsessed over solving it.  
越想办法，也就越苦恼。  
And the more he obsessed, the more miserable（苦恼的） he became.  
最后，他只好找来两个人商量。  
In the end, he could only summon two men to discuss it.  
一个是公子吕。  
One was Prince Lü.  
一个是祭足。  
The other was Jizu.  
公子吕一听是太后的事。  
The moment Prince Lü heard it involved the Queen Dowager,  
立刻就来气。  
he flared up at once.  
在他眼里，那个女人就是个老妖婆。  
In his eyes, that woman was nothing but an old monster.  
要换成自己。  
If it had been his choice,  
早把她送回申国去了。  
he would have sent her back to Shen long ago.  
祭足却只说。  
Jizu, however, only said  
这是家务事。  
that this was a family matter.  
自己不好插手。  
He was not in a position to meddle.  
庄公听完，只能长叹。  
After hearing this, Duke Zhuang could do nothing but sigh.  
连祭足都说没办法。  
If even Jizu said there was no way,  
那看来就真没办法了。  
then perhaps there truly was no way.  
可庄公没注意到一点。  
But Duke Zhuang failed to notice one thing.  
祭足先说的是“家务事”。  
Jizu's first words had been "family matter."  
他未必是真的没主意。  
That did not necessarily mean he had no idea.  
他只是太清楚。  
He simply knew too well  
别人的家务事，一旦掺和进去。  
that once you step into another man's family affairs,  
很容易最后落得里外不是人。  
you can easily end up offending everyone.  
整个郑国，大概也没人比祭足更精。  
In the whole state of Zheng, few were more shrewd（精明的） than Jizu.  
但不愿直接插手。  
Yet not wanting to interfere directly  
并不等于他不会帮庄公。  
did not mean he would not help Duke Zhuang in another way.

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`让步状语从句`、`宾语从句`、`条件状语从句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `No matter how wrong she had been, she was still his mother`。<br>
难点拆解：`No matter how wrong she had been` 是典型让步状语从句；`Duke Zhuang later discovered that ...` 和 `he only said that ...` 里都有宾语从句；后面 `once you step into ...` 则是条件状语从句。<br>
常用短语：
- `by all logic`：按理说
- `drown one's sorrow in wine`：借酒浇愁
- `stand in awe of`：敬畏……
- `not be in a position to do`：不便于做……

### 祭足的妙计
### Jizu's Clever Plan

祭足回到家以后。  
After Jizu returned home,  
先去洗了个脚。  
he first washed his feet.  
偏偏就是在洗脚的时候。  
And it was precisely while washing them  
他想出了主意。  
that the plan came to him.  
所以作者顺手感叹。  
So the author cannot resist joking  
常洗脚，果然还是有点好处。  
that regular foot-washing apparently has its advantages（好处）.  
祭足在家里排行老二。  
Jizu was second among the brothers in his family.  
所以也叫祭仲。  
So he was also called Jizhong.  
至于“祭足”这个名字。  
As for the name Jizu itself,  
字面上就像“好好伺候脚”。  
on the surface it almost sounds like "take good care of the feet."  
再加上他偏偏又爱洗脚。  
And since he really did love foot-washing,  
作者干脆拿他开玩笑。  
the author simply teases him  
说他都快能当洗脚业祖师爷了。  
and says he might as well be the patron founder（祖师） of the foot-bath trade.  
主意想定以后。  
Once the idea was settled,  
祭足立刻吩咐手下去请人。  
Jizu immediately ordered a man to fetch someone.  
请谁？  
Whom?  
请考叔。  
Ying Kaoshu.  
考叔不是“专门考试的叔叔”。  
Kaoshu, of course, was not some "uncle in charge of examinations."  
他的名字叫考叔。  
Kaoshu was simply his name.  
他住在颍地。  
He lived in Ying.  
因此又叫颍考叔。  
So he was also called Ying Kaoshu.  
他在颍城做个小官。  
He held a minor office in Ying.  
家里穷得叮当响。  
His family was poor enough to rattle（穷得发响）.  
可这个人有一样极出名。  
But there was one thing for which he was famous.  
他特别孝顺。  
He was extraordinarily filial（孝顺的）.  
孝到什么地步？  
How filial?  
《左传》都直接夸他“纯孝”。  
The *Zuo Commentary* praises him outright as "pure in filial devotion."  
祭足见了考叔。  
When Jizu met Kaoshu,  
第一句话就问。  
his very first question was this:  
“你娘还好吗？”  
"Is your mother well?"  
为什么一开口先问他娘？  
Why ask about his mother first?  
因为你要不先问这句。  
Because if you failed to begin there,  
考叔大概还真不高兴。  
Kaoshu might genuinely have been displeased.  
考叔说，娘很好。  
Kaoshu said his mother was doing well.  
祭足又问。  
Jizu then asked  
想不想升官。  
whether he wanted a promotion（升官）.  
考叔说，想。  
Kaoshu said yes.  
官做大一点。  
If his office rose even a little,  
分到的粮食就能多一些。  
he would receive more grain.  
祭足再问。  
Jizu asked again  
想不想发财。  
whether he wanted to grow rich.  
考叔还是说，想。  
Kaoshu again said yes.  
有了钱，母亲也能吃上肉。  
With money, his mother might finally eat meat.  
于是祭足对他说。  
So Jizu said to him  
自己可以教他一条升官发财的路。  
that he could show him a path to promotion and wealth.

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`时间状语从句`、`宾语从句`、`条件状语从句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `it was precisely while washing them that the plan came to him`。<br>
难点拆解：这里用了强调结构 `it was ... that ...`；`whether he wanted ...` 是宾语从句；`if you failed to begin there` 则是条件状语从句，用来解释祭足为何一开口就问母亲。<br>
常用短语：
- `cannot resist doing`：忍不住做……
- `on the surface`：表面上
- `outright`：直接地，明白地
- `show someone a path to`：给某人指一条通往……的路

### 考叔的猫头鹰
### Kaoshu's Owls

转眼过去了一年。  
A year passed in the blink of an eye.  
这一年里，庄公的心情一直不好。  
Throughout that year, Duke Zhuang remained in low spirits.  
这天忽然有人来报。  
One day, a report suddenly came in.  
说颍地来了个小官。  
A minor official had arrived from Ying.  
名字叫考叔。  
His name was Kaoshu.  
他还带了两只猫头鹰。  
And he had brought two owls.  
说要献给主公。  
He said they were for the ruler.  
庄公一开始心烦得很。  
At first Duke Zhuang was in too foul a mood.  
听了就说，不见。  
On hearing this, he said he would not see him.  
可话刚出口。  
But the words had hardly left his mouth  
他又忽然觉得不对劲。  
before he suddenly felt that something was odd.  
于是马上改口。  
So he immediately changed his mind.  
说，还是让他来吧。  
He said Kaoshu should be admitted after all.  
庄公为什么会觉得奇怪？  
Why did Duke Zhuang find it strange?  
因为按周朝的礼制。  
Because under Zhou ritual,  
初次见面是要带见面礼的。  
one was expected to bring a proper gift at a first meeting.  
天子送酒。  
The Son of Heaven gave ritual wine.  
诸侯送圭玉。  
Feudal lords gave jade tablets.  
卿送羔羊。  
High ministers gave lambs.  
大夫送雁。  
Senior officers gave geese.  
士送野鸡。  
Gentlemen gave pheasants.  
平民送鸭子。  
Commoners gave ducks.  
每样礼物都有说法。  
Every gift carried symbolic（象征的） meaning.  
考叔按身份，算是士。  
By rank, Kaoshu counted as a gentleman.  
照理说，他该送野鸡。  
By rights, he should have brought pheasants.  
可他偏偏带来两只猫头鹰。  
Yet he had brought two owls instead.  
这才是庄公真正好奇的地方。  
That was what genuinely aroused Duke Zhuang's curiosity（好奇）.  
庄公决定在官邸接见考叔。  
Duke Zhuang decided to receive Kaoshu in his residence.  
顺便也让我们看看。  
And this lets us see, in passing,  
春秋时君主怎样接待客人。  
how rulers received guests in the Spring and Autumn age.  
考叔进殿以后先行礼。  
After entering the hall, Kaoshu bowed first.  
庄公也要回礼。  
Duke Zhuang had to return the courtesy as well.  
宾主随后相对而坐。  
Host and guest then sat facing each other.  
说是坐。  
To call it sitting, however,  
其实更准确地说是跪坐。  
it was more accurately kneeling in a seated posture.  
两人中间隔着一张矮案。  
A low table stood between them.  
没有后来那么多繁琐臭规矩。  
There were none of the later suffocating（繁琐压人的） rules.  
这也是春秋贤士容易一鸣惊人的原因之一。  
That was one reason talented men in the Spring and Autumn era could so easily make a name for themselves.  
和君主说话时，大家至少还是平起平坐。  
At least when they spoke to a ruler, they still sat on equal level.  
压力没那么大。  
The pressure was not so crushing.  
因此也更敢直话直说。  
So they dared to speak more directly.  
庄公先开口致谢。  
Duke Zhuang spoke first to offer thanks.  
大老远送两只鸟来，辛苦了。  
It had been a long journey to deliver two birds.  
考叔却不绕弯子。  
Kaoshu did not bother with indirection（拐弯抹角）.  
他说，这两只鸟叫鸮。  
He said the birds were owls.  
白天眼神不好。  
Their sight was poor in the day.  
夜里倒很厉害。  
At night, however, it was sharp.  
大东西看不清。  
They could not see large things clearly.  
小东西却盯得很准。  
Yet they fixed on small things with precision（精准）.  
更可恶的是。  
More hateful still, he said,  
它小时候吃着母亲嘴里的虫子长大。  
they grew up by eating insects from their mother's beak.  
长大以后，却反过来吃母亲。  
But once grown, they turned around and devoured（吞食） their own mother.  
所以这种鸟是恶鸟。  
That was why they were evil birds.  
我特地把它们杀了。  
I killed them specially,  
送来请主公吃它们的肉。  
he said, so that the ruler might eat their flesh.  
这里得顺便替猫头鹰说句公道话。  
At this point, fairness requires a word in the owl's defense.  
猫头鹰其实并不吃母亲。  
Owls do not in fact eat their mothers.  
只是古人冤枉了它们。  
The ancients simply slandered（冤枉） them.  
问题是，庄公听到这里。  
The point, however, was that when Duke Zhuang heard this,  
脸色一下就变了。  
his face changed at once.  
考叔句句像在骂他。  
Every sentence sounded as though it were aimed at him.  
就像在说。  
It was as if Kaoshu were saying  
你不就是这种猫头鹰吗？  
that he himself was exactly that sort of owl.  
人若心里有愧。  
When a man carries shame in his heart,  
就会怕那些戳中自己的人。  
he fears those who strike the truth home.  
庄公正是如此。  
Duke Zhuang was exactly such a man.  
所以他对考叔，不是愤怒。  
So what he felt toward Kaoshu was not anger  
而是敬畏。  
but a kind of awe（敬畏）.  
于是庄公决定回礼。  
So Duke Zhuang decided to give a return gift.  
送什么？  
What did he send?  
不是圭玉。  
Not jade tablets.  
而是一只羊。  
But a sheep.  
而且还是蒸全羊。  
And not just any sheep, but a whole steamed one.  
这待遇已经算超规格了。  
That was treatment well above Kaoshu's rank.  
羊一端上来。  
The moment the sheep was brought out,  
考叔口水都快下来了。  
Kaoshu almost began to drool.  
他也不顾烫。  
He did not even care that it was hot.  
一把扯下一只羊腿。  
He tore off a leg in one yank.  
转手就往怀里塞。  
And promptly stuffed it into his robe.  
庄公看得直皱眉。  
Duke Zhuang frowned at the sight.  
他心想，这人怎么这么没规矩。  
He thought to himself that this man was terribly ill-bred（没规矩的）.  
考叔却说。  
Kaoshu explained,  
自己家里还有母亲。  
that he still had a mother at home.  
想把这份赏赐带回去给母亲吃。  
He wanted to take this reward back for her to eat.  
庄公一下就被打动了。  
Duke Zhuang was moved at once.  
多孝顺的儿子。  
What a filial son.  
多难得的臣子。  
What a rare servant.  
他忍不住叹气。  
He could not help sighing.  
考叔便问，为何叹气。  
Kaoshu then asked why he sighed.  
庄公这回也不再瞒着。  
This time Duke Zhuang hid nothing.  
把自己的烦恼全都说了。  
He laid out all his trouble.  
说自己很想母亲。  
He said he missed his mother greatly.  
可又怕违背誓言。  
Yet he feared breaking his oath.  
真不知道该怎么办。  
He truly did not know what to do.  
考叔听完，立刻答道。  
After hearing this, Kaoshu answered at once.  
只要掘地见泉。  
If one dug into the earth until spring water appeared,  
那不就是黄泉吗？  
would that not count as the Yellow Springs?  
这样一来，不就能见了？  
Then would the two not be able to meet?

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`条件状语从句`、`宾语从句`、`反问句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `If one dug into the earth until spring water appeared, would that not count as the Yellow Springs?`。<br>
难点拆解：`If one dug ...` 是条件状语从句；`He thought to himself that ...` 和 `He said he missed his mother ...` 都带宾语从句；后面连续两个反问句，是在提出机智办法，而不是单纯发问。<br>
常用短语：
- `by rights`：照理说
- `in passing`：顺便
- `strike the truth home`：一下戳中要害
- `lay out`：把……摊开说明

### 黄泉相见
### Meeting at the Yellow Springs

感人的一幕，终于要上演了。  
At last, the moving scene was ready to unfold（展开）.  
幕后总导演其实是祭足。  
The true mastermind behind the scenes was Jizu.  
考叔则是前台执行的人。  
Kaoshu was the man who carried it out in front.  
祭足先派人去勘地。  
Jizu first sent men to survey the ground.  
他们在颍地寻找水脉足的地方。  
They searched in Ying for a place rich in underground water.  
最后选中了牛脾山下。  
In the end, they chose a spot below Mount Niupi.  
这也顺手说明。  
That also suggests, in passing,  
春秋初年的人已经很懂找水了。  
that people in the early Spring and Autumn period already knew a fair amount about locating water.  
接着，考叔带着五百人开挖。  
Then Kaoshu led five hundred workers to start digging.  
地下水并不难找。  
Groundwater was not hard to reach.  
没挖多久，就见了泉。  
They had not dug for long before water appeared.  
安排停当以后。  
Once all was set in order,  
考叔先请太后下坑。  
Kaoshu first invited the Queen Dowager down into the pit.  
太后顺着梯子下去。  
She climbed down by ladder.  
随后，又请庄公到场。  
Afterward, Duke Zhuang was invited to the site as well.  
庄公站在坑边，心里激动得很。  
Standing at the edge of the pit, Duke Zhuang was deeply stirred.  
于是高声唱出一句。  
So he cried out in a loud voice,  
“大隧之内，其乐也融融。”  
"Within this great tunnel, how warm the joy must be."  
意思其实很简单。  
The meaning was simple enough.  
大概就是在问。  
He was more or less asking,  
娘啊，到了这里，你高兴吗？  
"Mother, now that you are here, are you happy?"  
太后在坑里听见，也激动得不得了。  
Hearing this from בתוך the pit, the Queen Dowager was overcome as well.  
她立刻应了一句。  
She immediately answered,  
“大隧之外，其乐也泄泄。”  
"Outside the great tunnel, how freely the joy flows."  
意思也很明白。  
Its meaning was just as clear.  
儿子啊，你也高兴吧？  
"My son, you too must be happy."  
黄泉之下，母子终于重逢。  
Under the sign of the Yellow Springs, mother and son were at last reunited（重逢）.  
那一刻，两人抱头痛哭。  
At that moment, the two embraced and wept bitterly.  
庄公亲手把母亲扶了上来。  
Duke Zhuang personally helped his mother climb back up.  
然后母子同车，回了荥阳。  
Then mother and son rode back to Xingyang in the same carriage.  
“黄泉相见”这个成语。  
The idiom "meet at the Yellow Springs"  
也就是从这里来的。  
comes from this very episode.  
只是它后来的常用意思。  
Its later common meaning, however,  
和这个结局恰好相反。  
turned out to be almost the opposite of this ending.  
这件事办成以后。  
Once the matter had been settled,  
考叔成了头号功臣。  
Kaoshu became the chief contributor（头号功臣）.  
庄公把他提拔为大夫。  
Duke Zhuang promoted him to the rank of officer.  
一个基层小官。  
A minor local official  
就这样升成了朝中重臣。  
had thus been raised into a central pillar（重臣） of government.  
他后来和公孙阏一起掌兵。  
He later shared military command with Gongsun E.

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`时间状语从句`、`被动表达`、`并列句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `Once the matter had been settled, Kaoshu became the chief contributor`。<br>
难点拆解：`Once the matter had been settled` 是时间状语从句，其中 `had been settled` 是被动表达；后面多组并列短句把勘地、挖坑、相见、回城这一连串动作快速推进起来。<br>
常用短语：
- `be rich in`：富于，富含
- `cry out`：高声喊出
- `at last`：终于
- `share command with`：与……共同掌兵

### 第一美男
### The First Great Handsome Man

这里顺便说个人。  
This is a good place to mention another man in passing.  
公孙阏。  
Gongsun E.  
他还有个更响的名字。  
He also had a more famous name.  
叫公孙子都。  
He was called Gongsun Zidu.  
后来公子吕退休。  
Later, after Prince Lü retired,  
子都就接了他的位子。  
Zidu took over his position.  
算起来，他是庄公的堂弟。  
By family reckoning, he was Duke Zhuang's cousin.  
又是高门子弟。  
He was also the son of a powerful house.  
一般人一看这种出身。  
When people see a background like that,  
很容易就会觉得。  
they easily assume  
他不过是靠门第往上爬。  
that he rose only by family influence（门第关系）.  
其实不是。  
In fact, that was not true.  
子都是真有本事。  
Zidu genuinely had ability.  
而且不只会打仗。  
And his strengths went beyond warfare.  
他高大、英俊、武艺强。  
He was tall, handsome, and highly skilled in arms.  
射箭尤其厉害。  
He was particularly formidable（强悍的） with the bow.  
在郑国，他本来就是有名的勇士。  
In Zheng, he was already famous as a warrior.  
可他更出名的。  
But what made him even more famous  
还是那张脸。  
was his face.  
他是春秋有名的美男子。  
He was one of the famous beauties among men of the Spring and Autumn era.  
这不是随口夸的。  
That is not casual praise.  
《诗经》提到过他。  
The *Book of Songs* mentions him.  
《孟子》也提到过他。  
Mencius mentions him too.  
意思都差不多。  
And the point is much the same in both.  
天下人都知道子都长得好。  
Everyone under heaven knew Zidu was handsome.  
要是有人连这都不知道。  
If someone did not even know that,  
那简直像是瞎了眼。  
it was almost as if he had no eyes.  
所以到后来。  
That is why later on,  
“子都”几乎就成了俊男的代号。  
"Zidu" almost became a byword（代称） for a handsome man.  
长得好，又能打。  
He was good-looking and he could fight.  
出身也好，官也高。  
His birth was high and his office high as well.  
这样的男人，谁不想嫁？  
What woman would not be tempted（心动的） by such a man?  
只是这么好的人。  
And yet even such a man  
也有个不小的毛病。  
had a serious flaw.  
那毛病后面再说。  
We will come to that flaw later.  
所以，遇见大帅哥时。  
So when one meets a dazzlingly handsome man,  
最好也别高兴得太早。  
it is wise not to be pleased too soon.

### 卫国战争
### The War of Wei

太后回来以后。  
After the Queen Dowager returned,  
庄公的日子顺了很多。  
Duke Zhuang's life became much smoother.  
拨乱反正之后。  
Once disorder had been set right,  
国家也重新走上了轨道。  
the state itself returned to its proper course.  
造反的弟弟已经死了。  
The rebellious brother was dead.  
母亲也回来了。  
His mother had returned.  
大臣们都很尽力。  
The ministers were all doing their utmost.  
国家一天比一天兴旺。  
The state was growing more prosperous（兴盛的） day by day.  
孩子们也在一天天长大。  
His children too were growing up.  
庄公那时确实很高兴。  
At that time, Duke Zhuang was genuinely pleased.  
有时在郑国待腻了。  
Sometimes, when he grew tired of staying in Zheng,  
他还会去周王朝的朝廷转转。  
he would even visit the Zhou court.  
感受一下中央气氛。  
He would enjoy the atmosphere at the center.  
顺便过一把中央领导的瘾。  
And indulge himself for a moment in feeling like a central statesman（中央重臣）.  
可和平这种东西。  
But peace, as a thing,  
从来都不太经久。  
never lasts very long.  
战争随时可能落下来。  
War can fall on people at any moment.  
就在讨伐叔段四年以后。  
Four years after Duan had been crushed,  
竟然有人找上门来讨伐庄公。  
someone came to campaign against Duke Zhuang instead.  
是谁？  
Who was it?  
是一个五国联盟。  
It was a five-state coalition（联盟）.  
以卫国为首。  
Wei led it.  
另外还有陈、宋、蔡、鲁。  
Alongside Wei stood Chen, Song, Cai, and Lu.  
因为是卫国挑头。  
Since Wei was the instigator（牵头者）,  
这场仗就被叫作卫国战争。  
the conflict came to be called the War of Wei.  
那卫国为什么忽然杀上门？  
But why did Wei suddenly come marching over?  
说到底，还是和叔段有关系。  
In the final reckoning, it still had to do with Duan.  
当初叔段的儿子公孙滑。  
Duan's son Gongsun Hua  
曾投奔了卫国的公子州吁。  
had once taken refuge（投奔） with Prince Zhouxu of Wei.  
偏偏不久前，州吁弑兄夺位。  
Not long before, Zhouxu had murdered his brother and seized the throne.  
自己做了卫国国君。  
He had made himself ruler of Wei.  
于是他就打着替公孙滑讨公道的旗号。  
So he raised the banner of seeking justice for Gongsun Hua.  
起兵来打郑国。  
Under that banner, he attacked Zheng.  
其他国家为什么也跟来凑热闹？  
And why did the others join the excitement?  
也各有各的理由。  
Each had its own reasons.  
宋国那边。  
In Song,  
公子冯前不久逃到了郑国避难。  
Prince Feng had recently fled to Zheng for asylum（避难）.  
宋殇公因此也想打郑。  
For that reason, Duke Shang of Song also wanted war with Zheng.  
陈国和郑国一向不太对付。  
Chen and Zheng had never been especially friendly.  
蔡国是小国，乐得跟着站队。  
Cai was a small state and was happy to line up behind others.  
鲁国则更干脆。  
Lu was even more straightforward.  
它是收了州吁的钱。  
It had been paid by Zhouxu.  
弄清这层以后。  
Once this was understood,  
庄公不但不怕。  
Duke Zhuang was not afraid in the least.  
反而笑了。  
He actually laughed.  
在他看来。  
In his eyes,  
这五国就算绑在一块。  
even if these five states were tied together,  
也未必打得过郑国。  
they still might not defeat Zheng.  
更何况这还只是乌合之众。  
And this was only a rabble（乌合之众） hastily thrown together.  
公孙子都早就憋着打仗。  
Gongsun Zidu had long been itching to fight.  
一听敌人送上门。  
The moment he heard that enemies had come to the door,  
立刻请战。  
he immediately asked for battle.  
庄公却摆手。  
But Duke Zhuang only waved him off.  
说不用急。  
There was no need to hurry.  
子都问，主公有什么妙计。  
Zidu asked what clever plan his lord had in mind.  
庄公就把话递给祭足。  
Duke Zhuang handed the question to Jizu.  
想看看祭足和自己是不是想到一处去了。  
He wanted to see whether Jizu's mind had gone to the same place as his own.  
祭足一笑，只说了一个字。  
Jizu smiled and said only one word.  
忍。  
Endure.  
子都还不服。  
Zidu was not convinced.  
祭足干脆和他打赌。  
So Jizu made a wager with him outright.  
只要郑国坚守不战。  
If Zheng simply held its walls and refused battle,  
五天之内。  
within five days  
联军自己就会撤。  
the allied army would withdraw on its own.  
要是五天后他们还不走。  
If they were still there after five days,  
自己这个上卿就让给子都。  
he would give up his own office of chief minister to Zidu.  
于是庄公关上城门。  
So Duke Zhuang shut the city gates.  
派人照常守城。  
He posted men to guard the walls as usual.  
自己和群臣该喝酒喝酒。  
He and the ministers drank wine as they pleased.  
该看戏看戏。  
They watched performances as they pleased.  
一点也不慌。  
There was not a trace of panic.  
五天以后。  
Five days later,  
庄公让子都上城看看。  
Duke Zhuang told Zidu to go look from the wall.  
子都一看，直接傻眼。  
Zidu looked and was stunned.  
东门外的五国联军。  
The five-state army outside the eastern gate  
已经撤得干干净净。  
had withdrawn completely.  
巡逻士兵说，他们昨天就走了。  
The patrol soldiers said they had left the day before.  
卫国战争，就这样结束了。  
And just like that, the War of Wei was over.  
庄公和祭足为什么算得这么准？  
Why had Duke Zhuang and Jizu judged the matter so accurately?  
这就得回头去讲卫国了。  
To answer that, we have to turn back and talk about Wei.

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`条件状语从句`、`宾语从句`、`并列句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `If Zheng simply held its walls and refused battle, the allied army would withdraw on its own`。<br>
难点拆解：`If Zheng simply held ...` 是条件状语从句；`Zidu asked what ...` 和 `He wanted to see whether ...` 都带宾语从句；后面大量并列短句把五国联军的来由一条条摊开。<br>
常用短语：
- `take refuge with`：投奔……
- `line up behind`：站到……一边
- `itch to do`：憋着想做……
- `on one's own`：自己，自动地

## 第八章 亲爹也靠不住
## Chapter 8: Even Your Own Father Cannot Be Trusted

### 卫国的来历
### The Origins of Wei

说起卫国。  
To talk about Wei,  
还得从武王灭商说起。  
we have to begin with King Wu's conquest（征服） of Shang.  
周灭商以后。  
After Zhou overthrew Shang,  
把朝歌一带封给了纣王的儿子武庚禄父。  
it enfeoffed（分封） the region around Chaoge to King Zhou's son, Wugeng Lüfu.  
那片地方大致在今天河南北部。  
That region lay roughly in what is now northern Henan.  
与此同时。  
At the same time,  
周王朝又派了两个人过去。  
the Zhou court sent two more men there.  
一个是管叔。  
One was Guanshu.  
一个是蔡叔。  
The other was Caishu.  
名义上是辅佐武庚。  
In name they were there to assist Wugeng.  
实际上却更像监视。  
In reality, they were much closer to supervisors（监督者）.  
后来武王去世。  
Later King Wu died.  
成王继位时年纪还小。  
King Cheng was still young when he took the throne.  
于是朝政落到周公旦手里。  
So the affairs of state fell into the hands of the Duke of Zhou.  
也不知管叔和蔡叔。  
Whether Guanshu and Caishu  
是不服周公。  
resented（不服） the Duke of Zhou,  
还是脑子真不太清楚。  
or simply lacked good sense,  
总之两人竟和武庚一起造反。  
in any case, the two actually joined Wugeng in rebellion.  
周公也不跟他们客气。  
The Duke of Zhou showed them no leniency（宽容）.  
很快就把这场乱事平掉了。  
He quickly crushed the uprising.  
平乱之后。  
After restoring order,  
周公把这块地方封给了弟弟康叔封。  
he enfeoffed the region to his younger brother Kang Shu Feng.  
这个国家，就叫卫。  
That state was called Wei.  
卫康叔也就成了卫国第一代君主。  
Kang Shu of Wei thus became the first ruler of Wei.  
他后来也被许多姓氏奉为始祖。  
He was later revered（尊为） as an ancestor by many surnames.  
康叔当时年纪不大。  
Kang Shu was still young at the time.  
周公怕他不会治国。  
The Duke of Zhou feared he might not know how to govern.  
于是专门写了几篇文告教他。  
So he wrote several texts specifically to instruct him.  
其中就有《梓材》《康诰》《酒诰》。  
Among them were the *Zicai*, the *Kanggao*, and the *Jiugao*.  
康叔倒也争气。  
Kang Shu, to his credit, proved equal to the task.  
他按着哥哥的办法治国。  
He governed according to his elder brother's guidance.  
把卫国收拾得井井有条。  
He brought Wei into fine order（井然有序）.  
连商朝遗民都心悦诚服。  
Even the former Shang people submitted with willing hearts（心悦诚服）.  
那时的卫国。  
At that time, Wei  
简直是天下的样板国家。  
was practically the model state of the realm.  
各国都往那儿派人。  
Other states kept sending people there.  
像今天去取经一样。  
It was much like sending study delegations（考察团） there today.  
为了表彰康叔。  
To reward Kang Shu,  
成王还把他调入中央。  
King Cheng even brought him into the central court.  
让他当了司寇。  
He appointed him Sikou.  
差不多相当于最高司法长官。  
That was roughly the post of highest judicial official.  
所以说，卫国的祖宗。  
So when all is said and done,  
来头确实很硬。  
Wei's founding lineage was indeed formidable（来头很硬的）.

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`时间状语从句`、`宾语从句`、`并列句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `After Zhou overthrew Shang, it enfeoffed the region around Chaoge to Wugeng Lüfu`。<br>
难点拆解：`After Zhou overthrew Shang` 是时间状语从句；`The Duke of Zhou feared he might not know how to govern` 里有宾语从句；整段用并列句连续交代卫国来历，信息密度大但节奏要稳。<br>
常用短语：
- `in name`：名义上
- `in reality`：实际上
- `show no leniency`：毫不宽容
- `to one's credit`：值得称道的是

### 公子州吁篡位
### Prince Zhouxu Seizes the Throne

公子州吁是个什么人？  
What kind of man was Prince Zhouxu?  
简单说。  
Put simply,  
他就是卫国版的叔段。  
he was Wei's version of Duan.  
只不过他比叔段幸运一点。  
He was only slightly luckier than Duan.  
因为卫国的国君，不是郑庄公。  
That was because the ruler of Wei was not Duke Zhuang of Zheng.  
卫武公死后。  
After Duke Wu of Wei died,  
国位传给了卫庄公。  
the throne passed to Duke Zhuang of Wei.  
卫庄公的正妻生了个儿子。  
The duke's principal wife bore him a son.  
名叫完。  
His name was Wan.  
那就是世子。  
He was the heir apparent（世子）.  
庄公另有一个妾。  
Duke Zhuang also had a concubine.  
那个妾生的儿子。  
The son she bore  
就是州吁。  
was Zhouxu.  
州吁很聪明。  
Zhouxu was clever.  
长得也帅。  
He was handsome too.  
还特别喜欢打仗。  
And he especially loved warfare.  
庄公很喜欢这个儿子。  
Duke Zhuang was very fond of him.  
甚至让他带兵。  
He even gave him troops to command.  
在卫国，州吁的地位。  
In Wei, Zhouxu's standing  
和郑国的叔段差不多。  
was much like Duan's in Zheng.  
州吁有个铁哥们。  
Zhouxu had a close companion（铁哥们）.  
叫石厚。  
His name was Shi Hou.  
石厚的父亲，是卫国大夫石碏。  
Shi Hou's father was Shi Que, a minister of Wei.  
偏偏石碏特别看不上州吁。  
Shi Que, however, thoroughly disliked Zhouxu.  
他见儿子和州吁走得近。  
Whenever he saw his son growing close to Zhouxu,  
就再三警告。  
he warned him again and again  
说此人靠不住。  
that this was not a trustworthy man.  
石厚却根本听不进去。  
Shi Hou would not listen at all.  
在他看来。  
In his eyes,  
州吁早晚会当国君。  
Zhouxu would one day become ruler sooner or later.  
自己当然要提前押宝。  
Naturally, he should place his bet early.  
父子两个谁也说服不了谁。  
Father and son could persuade neither the other nor themselves to yield.  
石碏一气之下。  
In a fit of anger,  
甚至把儿子关了起来。  
Shi Que even had his son confined（软禁）.  
石厚却翻墙跑了。  
But Shi Hou climbed over the wall and ran away.  
索性直接住到州吁那里去。  
He simply went to live with Zhouxu.  
也算为了前途，不要亲情了。  
It was his way of trading family ties for political prospects（前途）.  
这里顺便提一句。  
An aside is worth adding here.  
石姓不少人。  
Many people surnamed Shi  
都会把石碏当祖先。  
trace their line back to Shi Que.  
庄公活着的时候。  
While Duke Zhuang of Wei was still alive,  
石碏就劝过他。  
Shi Que had already warned him  
不要太重用州吁。  
not to favor Zhouxu too heavily.  
否则一旦羽翼太丰。  
Otherwise, once his wings were too fully grown,  
将来一定是祸。  
he would become a calamity（祸患）.  
可庄公压根不听。  
But Duke Zhuang would not listen in the least.  
还嫌石碏多管家务事。  
He even thought Shi Que was meddling in family business.  
石碏想想也明白。  
Shi Que understood well enough  
这种家务事，最难管。  
that family matters are the hardest to handle.  
看不惯，索性退休回家。  
Unable to bear it, he simply retired home.  
等到庄公死后。  
After Duke Zhuang died,  
世子完继位。  
the heir Wan took the throne.  
那就是卫桓公。  
He became Duke Huan of Wei.  
卫桓公很看不惯州吁。  
Duke Huan could not stand Zhouxu.  
于是把他赶回封地。  
So he drove him back to his own territory.  
州吁却不是省油的灯。  
Zhouxu was no easy man to suppress（压住）.  
没过多久。  
Before long,  
他就组织了一支亡命之徒。  
he organized a band of desperate criminals（亡命徒）.  
找机会杀进卫国都城。  
He found an opportunity to strike into Wei's capital.  
又刺杀了卫桓公。  
Then he assassinated（刺杀） Duke Huan of Wei.  
事情做到这一步。  
After matters reached that point,  
州吁干脆自己做了国君。  
Zhouxu simply made himself ruler.  
古书里把这种事叫“弑”。  
In ancient texts, that kind of act is called *shi*, regicide（弑君）.  
作者拿现代话打趣。  
The author jokes in modern language  
说这就叫“篡党夺权”。  
and says this is what you would call "usurping power."  
回头再看郑庄公。  
Looking back at Duke Zhuang of Zheng,  
要是他当年不除掉叔段。  
if he had not dealt with Duan,  
他的下场多半就是卫桓公。  
his fate would probably have been Duke Huan's.

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`条件状语从句`、`宾语从句`、`被动表达`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `if he had not dealt with Duan, his fate would probably have been Duke Huan's`。<br>
难点拆解：这里用了与过去事实相反的条件状语从句；`Shi Que had already warned him that ...` 里面是宾语从句；`He was driven back ...` 这类被动意义在这一段里也很多，适合历史叙述。<br>
常用短语：
- `be fond of`：偏爱……
- `place one's bet early`：提前押宝
- `be no easy man to suppress`：不是容易压住的人
- `look back at`：回过头来看……

### 石厚的妙计
### Shi Hou's Clever Plan

常有人说。  
People often say  
打天下容易，坐天下难。  
that winning power is easier than sitting securely on it.  
州吁虽然没听过这种总结。  
Zhouxu had never heard the saying phrased exactly that way.  
可他心里其实明白。  
But he understood the truth of it well enough.  
篡位不难。  
Usurping the throne was not the hard part.  
难的是让大家服气。  
The hard part was making everyone accept it.  
要想坐稳位置。  
If he wanted to sit securely,  
就得想个法子立威。  
he needed some way to establish authority（立威）.  
问题是，他并没有什么高明办法。  
The problem was that he had no especially brilliant plan.  
请大家吃顿饭。  
Treating everyone to a feast  
顶多只能热闹一阵。  
would at most create a little excitement.  
给官员涨点待遇。  
Raising officials' pay and benefits a little  
也未必能压住人心。  
would not necessarily steady popular feeling.  
正发愁时。  
While he was worrying over it,  
石厚来了。  
Shi Hou arrived.  
石厚果然有主意。  
Shi Hou, as expected, had a plan.  
他说，眼下最要紧的是立威。  
He said the most urgent thing now was to establish fear and prestige.  
本来最简单的办法。  
In principle, the easiest method  
就是杀几个人。  
would have been to kill a few people.  
可现在没有合适借口。  
But at the moment they had no suitable pretext（借口）.  
乱杀人容易落个暴君名声。  
Random killing would too easily earn them the name of tyrant.  
那怎么办？  
So what was to be done?  
打仗。  
War.  
找个国家打一仗。  
Choose a state and fight it.  
打仗有两个好处。  
War, he said, had two advantages（好处）.  
第一，能让国内都看见主公有本事。  
First, it would let the whole country see that the ruler had real power.  
第二，能把国内矛盾。  
Second, it could shift domestic tensions  
转到对外的矛盾上去。  
onto conflict with an outside enemy.  
这样大家就会更容易团结起来。  
That way people would find it easier to unite.  
州吁一听，眼睛都亮了。  
Zhouxu's eyes lit up the moment he heard this.  
他说，打谁？  
He asked whom they should attack.  
石厚又替他想好了。  
Shi Hou had already thought that through as well.  
大国太强，惹不起。  
Great powers were too strong to provoke.  
太小的国家又不值得打。  
Very small states were not worth attacking.  
最好找个实力差不多的。  
The best target would be a state of roughly equal strength.  
而且最好还要师出有名。  
And better still if the campaign had a righteous-looking cause（名义）.  
郑国正合适。  
Zheng was perfect.  
郑庄公杀弟逐母。  
Duke Zhuang of Zheng had driven off his mother and brought about his brother's death.  
光凭这一点。  
That alone  
就够拿出来当口实。  
was enough to serve as a pretext.  
更何况公孙滑现在就在卫国。  
And Gongsun Hua was now in Wei as well.  
正好可以借着替他申冤。  
That gave them a convenient excuse to avenge him.  
名义就更好看了。  
The banner would look even more respectable.  
确定了方向。  
Once the direction was fixed,  
接下来就是拉盟友。  
the next step was to gather allies.  
单靠卫国自己。  
Wei by itself  
既未必打得赢。  
might not win,  
也不好看。  
and in any case it would not look impressive.  
石厚粗粗一算。  
Shi Hou made a quick calculation.  
鲁、陈、蔡都不难拉。  
Lu, Chen, and Cai would not be hard to pull in.  
给点好处就行。  
A few favors would do it.  
真正麻烦的是宋国。  
The real trouble was Song.  
宋国爵位高。  
Song held a high rank.  
架子也大。  
Its airs were correspondingly grand（架子大的）.  
轻易未必肯动。  
It would not move lightly.  
石厚却又推荐了一个人。  
But Shi Hou recommended yet another man.  
这个人叫宁翊。  
His name was Ning Yi.  
他说这人特别会游说。  
He said this man was especially good at persuasion（游说）.  
而且对宋国近况很熟。  
And he knew Song's recent circumstances very well.  
只要派他去。  
If he was sent,  
宋国多半能被说动。  
Song would probably be persuaded.

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`条件状语从句`、`宾语从句`、`并列句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `If he wanted to sit securely, he needed some way to establish authority`。<br>
难点拆解：`If he wanted ...` 是条件状语从句；`He said the most urgent thing now was ...` 带宾语从句和表语结构；中间两条“第一、第二”则是典型并列展开。<br>
常用短语：
- `sit securely`：坐稳位置
- `earn the name of`：落下……名声
- `serve as a pretext`：充当借口
- `pull in`：拉拢，拉进来

### 宋国的故事
### The Story of Song

既然说到了宋国。  
Since Song has now entered the story,  
那就顺手把它的来历也讲一下。  
we may as well explain its origins too.  
宋国在今天河南商丘一带。  
Song lay around present-day Shangqiu in Henan.  
都城是睢阳。  
Its capital was Suiyang.  
当年周武王灭商以后。  
After King Wu destroyed Shang,  
把纣王的哥哥微子启封在了宋。  
he enfeoffed Weizi Qi, the elder brother of King Zhou, in Song.  
而且封的是公爵。  
And he granted him the rank of duke.  
由此也能看出来。  
That alone shows  
微子启这个人，名声确实很硬。  
that Weizi Qi's reputation was indeed formidable.  
微子启很贤。  
Weizi Qi was a virtuous（贤明的） man.  
这种贤，后来还传了下来。  
And that virtue was, for a time, inherited.  
传到宋宣公时。  
By the time of Duke Xuan of Song,  
宣公也很贤。  
Duke Xuan too was virtuous.  
他临死前没有把位子留给儿子。  
Before dying, he did not leave the throne to his son.  
而是传给了弟弟穆公。  
Instead, he passed it to his younger brother Duke Mu.  
穆公也很贤。  
Duke Mu was virtuous as well.  
等到穆公临终。  
And when Duke Mu neared death,  
他也没有把君位给自己的儿子公子冯。  
he likewise did not pass the throne to his own son, Prince Feng.  
而是还给了宣公的儿子。  
Instead, he returned it to Duke Xuan's son.  
那就是宋殇公。  
That son was Duke Shang of Song.  
贤明有时最大的问题。  
The greatest problem with virtue, at times,  
就在于你总会碰上不贤明的人。  
is that sooner or later it collides with people who are not virtuous at all.  
宋殇公不是贤人。  
Duke Shang was no virtuous man.  
公子冯也不是。  
Nor was Prince Feng.  
这两个人彼此都恨得要命。  
The two loathed（痛恨） each other intensely.  
穆公其实早就看出来了。  
Duke Mu had in fact seen this long before.  
所以临死前先把公子冯安排去守边。  
So before his death, he arranged for Prince Feng to guard the frontier.  
意思其实很明白。  
The meaning was plain enough.  
真要出事，你就赶紧跑。  
If trouble really came, he should run.  
果然，殇公刚一上台。  
Sure enough, the moment Duke Shang took power,  
公子冯就逃到了郑国。  
Prince Feng fled to Zheng.  
他知道，殇公一定不会放过自己。  
He knew Duke Shang would never spare him.  
这种逃亡，在春秋时叫政治避难。  
This kind of flight was political asylum（政治避难） in the Spring and Autumn age.  
那时诸侯对于来投的人。  
At that time, rulers generally treated political refugees  
大体都愿意收留。  
with a willingness to shelter them.  
不收，反倒显得不够仗义。  
To refuse them could make a state look ungenerous（不仗义的）.  
当然，待遇会降一级。  
Of course, their treatment would usually be reduced by one grade.  
公子冯在宋国相当于卿。  
Prince Feng had ranked like a high minister in Song.  
到了郑国。  
But once in Zheng,  
他只能按大夫规格安置。  
he was settled at the level of an officer.  
郑庄公给了他长葛这座小城。  
Duke Zhuang of Zheng gave him the small city of Changge.  
宋殇公一直想把公子冯抓回去。  
Duke Shang of Song always wanted Prince Feng seized and brought back.  
可他又怕郑国不好惹。  
But he also feared that Zheng was not easy to provoke.  
一方面怕打不过郑。  
On one hand, he feared he could not defeat Zheng.  
另一方面又怕公子冯借郑兵反打回来。  
On the other, he feared Prince Feng might borrow Zheng's troops and strike back.  
所以真要打郑国。  
So when it came to attacking Zheng,  
宋国的心其实比卫国还急。  
Song's desire was, in truth, even stronger than Wei's.  
宁翊也正是抓住了这一点。  
This was exactly the point Ning Yi seized upon.  
他一到宋国。  
The moment he reached Song,  
把大意一说。  
he explained the basic proposal.  
宋殇公立刻就高兴了。  
Duke Shang was delighted at once.  
他早就憋着要借机打郑国。  
He had long been waiting for a chance to attack Zheng.  
把公子冯抓回来。  
And to bring Prince Feng back in chains.  
这样一来。  
With that,  
五国联军的架子也就搭起来了。  
the framework of the five-state coalition was in place.  
可这支联军。  
But this coalition  
从一开始就没多少真心。  
had little real unity from the very start.  
卫、宋、陈、蔡、鲁五国联军。  
The combined armies of Wei, Song, Chen, Cai, and Lu  
浩浩荡荡杀到郑国。  
marched grandly to Zheng.  
一路上却秋毫无犯。  
Yet along the way they scrupulously（谨慎地） avoided any serious action.  
他们甚至懒得去占别的城。  
They could not even be bothered to seize other cities.  
因为州吁和石厚本就没想真打。  
That was because Zhouxu and Shi Hou had never truly meant to wage a real campaign.  
他们更像是在做样子。  
They were staging a performance more than a war.  
宋国倒是真想打。  
Song, to be fair, did want real fighting.  
可它实力不够。  
But it lacked the strength.  
也不敢单独往前冲。  
Nor did it dare charge ahead alone.  
联军于是围住郑国东门。  
So the coalition surrounded Zheng's eastern gate.  
却迟迟不肯进攻。  
Yet it delayed and delayed without attacking.  
宋殇公其实想谈条件。  
Duke Shang of Song actually wanted terms.  
只要郑国交出公子冯。  
If Zheng would hand over Prince Feng,  
他就愿意撤兵。  
he was willing to withdraw.  
可问题是。  
The problem was that  
郑国压根不出来。  
Zheng never came out.  
你要派人过去谈。  
If anyone tried to approach for talks,  
城上就是一通乱箭。  
a shower of arrows came down from the walls.  
一连拖了五天。  
So things dragged on for five straight days.  
什么进展也没有。  
There was no progress at all.  
最先扛不住的。  
The first side to lose patience  
还是卫国。  
was Wei.  
石厚提醒州吁。  
Shi Hou reminded Zhouxu  
再不回去。  
that if they stayed out much longer,  
就怕后方生变。  
the rear might erupt in trouble.  
州吁一想，也是。  
Zhouxu thought it over and agreed.  
他毕竟刚刚篡位。  
He had, after all, only just usurped（篡夺） the throne.  
根基还不稳。  
His position was not yet secure.  
果然，卫国一撤。  
And indeed, once Wei withdrew,  
其他四国也只能跟着散。  
the other four states could only break up as well.  
回到国内以后。  
Once they got home,  
卫国军队还要顺手吹一吹。  
Wei's troops naturally had to boast a little.  
说自己把郑庄公打得不敢出门。  
They claimed they had frightened Duke Zhuang into staying behind his gates.  
可吹归吹。  
Boasting was boasting,  
卫国人对州吁还是不服。  
but the people of Wei still did not accept Zhouxu.  
州吁也因此更烦。  
That only deepened Zhouxu's anxiety.  
而这时，石厚又出了一个主意。  
And at this point, Shi Hou offered yet another plan.

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`让步状语从句`、`条件状语从句`、`宾语从句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `Even if the coalition marched grandly to Zheng, it had little real unity from the very start`。<br>
难点拆解：`Even if ...` 是让步状语从句；`If Zheng would hand over Prince Feng ...` 是条件状语从句；`He knew Duke Shang would never spare him` 这类句子里都有宾语从句，适合训练“转述别人想法”的英语。<br>
常用短语：
- `sooner or later`：迟早
- `in chains`：押解回来
- `to be fair`：平心而论
- `break up`：散掉，解散

### 石老爷子的坏主意
### Old Master Shi's Bad Idea

做好人不容易。  
Being a good man is not easy.  
做坏人，其实也不容易。  
Being a bad man is not easy either.  
不管做好人还是坏人。  
Whether one chooses goodness or wickedness,  
最怕的都不是做。  
the hardest part is not the doing itself.  
最怕的是做得不彻底。  
The hardest part is failing to do it thoroughly（彻底地）.  
州吁就是这样。  
Zhouxu was exactly that kind of man.  
他明明是坏人。  
He was clearly a bad man.  
却又总想让大家觉得自己是好人。  
Yet he still wanted everyone to believe he was a good one.  
明明是篡位。  
What he had done was usurpation（篡位）.  
却偏偏还想要个名正言顺。  
And yet he still wanted a proper cloak of legitimacy（正当性）.  
这种坏人，往往最容易出事。  
That kind of villain is often the one most likely to run into disaster.  
石厚提出的主意是什么？  
So what was the plan Shi Hou proposed?  
很简单。  
It was simple.  
去请他爹石碏出山。  
Bring his father, Shi Que, out to advise them.  
石厚自己很清楚。  
Shi Hou himself knew perfectly well  
老爷子比自己高明。  
that the old man was cleverer than he was.  
虽然他以前不听父亲的话。  
Though he had never listened to his father before,  
可这并不妨碍他承认父亲厉害。  
that did not prevent him from admitting his father's superiority（高明）.  
州吁一听，也觉得有道理。  
Zhouxu thought the idea made sense too.  
干脆不派人请了。  
Instead of sending a messenger,  
他决定亲自登门。  
he decided to go in person.  
这样既算礼贤下士。  
That would count as honoring talent humbly（礼贤下士）.  
又显得自己没架子。  
And it would show that he put on no airs.  
州吁带着石厚去了石碏家。  
So Zhouxu went to Shi Que's house with Shi Hou.  
石碏见他们上门。  
When Shi Que saw them arrive,  
也只能客客气气接待。  
he had no choice but to receive them politely.  
州吁开门见山。  
Zhouxu went straight to the point.  
他说自己想知道。  
He said he wished to know  
怎样才能让百姓服气。  
how to make the people accept him.  
石碏回了几句话。  
Shi Que replied in only a few sentences.  
他说，要是能去朝见周王。  
He said that if Zhouxu could go and pay court to the Zhou king,  
由周王正式承认他。  
and if the Zhou king formally recognized him,  
那地位自然就稳了。  
his position would naturally be steadier.  
州吁又问。  
Zhouxu asked again  
自己凭什么能见到周王。  
how he could hope to gain such an audience.  
石碏就替他指了条路。  
Shi Que then pointed out a path.  
先去见陈桓公。  
He should first go see Duke Huan of Chen.  
因为陈桓公和周王关系好。  
Duke Huan of Chen enjoyed good relations with the Zhou king.  
只要请他代为说话。  
If he could be persuaded to speak on Zhouxu's behalf,  
事情多半就能办成。  
the matter would probably succeed.  
这个主意，听着挺像个好主意。  
On the surface, it sounded like a fine idea.  
可细想一下。  
But think a little harder,  
其实坏得很。  
and it was a terrible one.  
第一，州吁刚刚篡位。  
First, Zhouxu had only just seized the throne.  
位置还不稳。  
His position was still shaky.  
这种时候最不该做的。  
At such a moment, the last thing he should have done  
就是到处乱跑。  
was wander off abroad.  
第二，靠暴力拿来的东西。  
Second, anything gained by violence  
通常还是得靠暴力守。  
usually has to be defended by violence as well.  
急着去找名分。  
To rush off looking for legitimacy  
未必真有用。  
was not necessarily useful.  
第三，也是最要命的一点。  
Third, and most fatal of all,  
陈国和卫国本来就是亲戚。  
Chen and Wei were related by marriage.  
可这层亲戚关系。  
But that very kinship  
对州吁一点也不友好。  
was hardly friendly to Zhouxu.  
被州吁杀掉的卫桓公。  
The Duke Huan of Wei whom Zhouxu had killed  
正是陈桓公的外甥。  
was Duke Huan of Chen's nephew.  
这么一位舅舅。  
And this uncle  
会真心替你说话吗？  
was supposed to plead sincerely for you?  
可这么明摆着的事。  
Yet obvious as all this was,  
州吁和石厚偏偏都没看透。  
neither Zhouxu nor Shi Hou saw through it.  
一方面，他们太想向善向正。  
On one hand, they were too eager to look proper and righteous.  
另一方面，州吁也没想到。  
On the other, Zhouxu never imagined  
自己都这么礼贤下士了。  
that after he had shown such humility,  
石碏竟然还是不肯买账。  
Shi Que still would not be moved.  
石厚倒是隐约有点怀疑。  
Shi Hou, to be fair, felt a faint suspicion（怀疑）.  
可他转念又想。  
But then another thought came to him.  
虎毒还不食子。  
Even a tiger does not eat its cubs.  
亲爹总不会害自己的儿子吧。  
Surely a father would not destroy his own son.  
于是君臣二人。  
So ruler and minister  
就这样高高兴兴上路了。  
set out in high spirits.

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`条件状语从句`、`宾语从句`、`反问句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `if the Zhou king formally recognized him, his position would naturally be steadier`。<br>
难点拆解：`if ... recognized him` 是条件状语从句；`He said that ...`、`He wished to know how ...` 都是宾语从句；`would he really plead sincerely for you?` 则是反问，用来点破这个主意有多危险。<br>
常用短语：
- `go straight to the point`：开门见山
- `speak on someone's behalf`：替某人说话
- `on the surface`：表面上
- `set out in high spirits`：兴高采烈地上路

### 大义灭亲
### Righteousness Above Kin

三天之后。  
Three days later,  
州吁和石厚带着金银珠宝出发了。  
Zhouxu and Shi Hou set out with gold, silver, and jewels.  
身边还带着一百多人。  
They were accompanied by more than a hundred followers.  
一路直奔陈国。  
They headed straight for Chen.  
可他们不知道。  
What they did not know  
是在他们动身前一天。  
was that on the day before they departed,  
石碏早已先派了人去陈国。  
Shi Que had already sent a man ahead to Chen.  
还捎了一封急信。  
He also sent an urgent letter.  
信里写得很重。  
The letter spoke in severe terms（严厉的语气）.  
州吁靠暴力篡位。  
It said Zhouxu had seized power by violence.  
还杀了陈桓公的外甥。  
He had even murdered Duke Huan of Chen's nephew.  
这样的人罪该万死。  
A man like that deserved ten thousand deaths.  
如今我把他骗到陈国来。  
Now, wrote Shi Que, he had lured Zhouxu to Chen.  
请您当场拿下。  
He asked Chen to seize him on the spot.  
替外甥报仇。  
That would avenge the nephew.  
也替卫国除害。  
And rid Wei of a scourge（祸害）.  
陈桓公看完信以后。  
After reading the letter,  
Duke Huan of Chen hesitated.  
he hesitated.  
于是马上召集大臣开会。  
So he immediately summoned his ministers for deliberation（商议）.  
朝臣意见分成两派。  
Opinion split into two camps.  
一派觉得，这是机会。  
One side said this was an opportunity.  
反正只是杀几个人。  
After all, it meant only killing a few men.  
又不费什么力。  
It would cost little effort.  
另一派却说。  
The other side argued  
这太像粗暴干涉别国内政。  
that this looked too much like crude interference（干涉） in another state's affairs.  
何况别人是来拜会你的。  
Besides, the men had come as guests.  
你若下手，总有些说不过去。  
If you killed them, it would be difficult to justify（说不过去）.  
陈桓公听完，最后一拍大腿。  
After hearing both sides, Duke Huan of Chen slapped his thigh and decided.  
杀。  
Kill them.  
他心里其实也不痛快。  
He too had his grievances（怨气）.  
上次鼓动大家去打郑国。  
The last time they had all been stirred up to attack Zheng,  
州吁是带头闹事的人。  
Zhouxu had led the affair.  
结果真正撤兵时。  
Yet when withdrawal came,  
也是他先跑。  
he had also been first to run.  
这种人，确实不地道。  
Such a man, in truth, was not honorable at all.  
于是陈国埋伏好了刀斧手。  
So Chen stationed hidden executioners（刀斧手） in readiness.  
只等州吁上门。  
They waited only for Zhouxu to arrive.  
州吁一行却毫不知情。  
Zhouxu's party knew nothing of this.  
他们一路高高兴兴到了陈国。  
They reached Chen in high spirits.  
陈桓公命人迎接。  
Duke Huan ordered men to receive them.  
又一路把他们带进朝堂。  
Then they were led all the way into the court hall.  
两国君主先寒暄。  
The two rulers exchanged formal greetings（寒暄）.  
州吁随后开始献礼。  
Then Zhouxu began presenting gifts.  
这一箱金子。  
One chest held gold.  
那一箱银子。  
Another held silver.  
还有珠宝和鹿皮。  
There were jewels and deer hides as well.  
等礼物都摆完。  
When all the gifts had been laid out,  
陈桓公忽然问了一句。  
Duke Huan of Chen suddenly asked  
“没有了？”  
"Is that all?"  
州吁答说，没有了。  
Zhouxu answered that there was nothing more.  
陈桓公便下令。  
So Duke Huan gave the order.  
“拿下。”  
"Seize them."  
州吁当场就傻住了。  
Zhouxu was stunned on the spot.  
说到底。  
When all was said and done,  
他还是太嫩。  
he was still too green（不成熟的） as a political player.  

这里顺便插一句。  
This is a good moment for another aside.  
陈国的来头也不小。  
Chen too had no small pedigree（来历）.  
妫满是舜的后代。  
Gui Man was descended from the ancient ruler Shun.  
周武王把大女儿嫁给了他。  
King Wu of Zhou gave his eldest daughter to him in marriage.  
后来又把他封在陈。  
Later he also enfeoffed him in Chen.  
陈胡公就是妫满。  
Gui Man was posthumously known as Duke Hu of Chen.  
很多姓氏都把他当祖先。  
Many surnames trace their ancestry to him.  
陈国因为地方不大。  
Because Chen was not a large state,  
又不是周王室宗亲。  
and because it was not a direct branch of the Zhou royal house,  
行事一向比较谨慎。  
it usually behaved with caution（谨慎）.  
陈桓公也延续了这种作风。  
Duke Huan of Chen continued that style.  
他虽然抓了州吁。  
Although he arrested Zhouxu,  
却不想在自己这里动手。  
he did not want the execution carried out in his own state.  
更不想亲自动手。  
Still less did he want to do it personally.  
于是他派人去卫国。  
So he sent a message to Wei.  
让卫国自己来领人。  
Wei was to send its own people to take custody.  
卫国这边收到消息后。  
When the message reached Wei,  
石碏立刻召集公卿商议。  
Shi Que at once gathered the ministers to deliberate.  
有几个意见很快就定下来了。  
Several points were quickly settled.  
第一，不能把州吁活着带回卫国。  
First, Zhouxu could not be brought back alive to Wei.  
真要带回来。  
If he were actually brought back,  
州吁的余党说不定还会闹事。  
his remaining followers might create fresh trouble.  
第二，执行的人也得赶快定。  
Second, the executioners had to be chosen quickly.  
右宰丑主动请命。  
Youzai Chou volunteered.  
那就由他去杀州吁。  
So he was assigned to kill Zhouxu.  
接下来，问题落到石厚身上。  
Then the question fell on Shi Hou.  
他当然也是主犯。  
He too was obviously a principal offender（主犯）.  
按理同样该杀。  
By rights, he should die as well.  
可他偏偏是石碏的儿子。  
Yet he was Shi Que's son.  
于是有人开始替他说情。  
So some began pleading on his behalf.  
说他还年轻。  
They said he was still young.  
也许只是跟错了人。  
Perhaps he had only followed the wrong man.  
要是给他一个改过机会。  
If he were given a chance to repent,  
以后也许还能成材。  
he might still become useful someday.  
可石碏一点也不松口。  
Shi Que did not yield in the least.  
他说别人不知道。  
He said others might not know,  
自己却最清楚。  
but he himself knew best.  
州吁干的那些坏事。  
The wicked schemes Zhouxu carried out  
多半都是石厚出的主意。  
were in large part Shi Hou's ideas.  
石厚甚至比州吁更该死。  
Shi Hou deserved death even more than Zhouxu.  
你们若不肯杀。  
If the others would not kill him,  
那我就亲自去。  
then I myself will go.  
话说到这一步。  
Once matters had been put that starkly,  
众人也就不再劝了。  
the others stopped pleading.  
最终，卫国派出两个人。  
In the end, Wei sent two men.  
右宰丑去杀州吁。  
Youzai Chou was to kill Zhouxu.  
石碏的家臣獳羊肩。  
And Nuyang Jian, a retainer of Shi Que,  
去杀石厚。  
was to kill Shi Hou.  
至于动手地点。  
As for the place of execution,  
两国干脆商量定在边界。  
the two states simply fixed on their border.  
这样谁也不用太难做。  
That way, no one had to look too directly responsible.  
到了临刑时。  
When the moment of execution arrived,  
右宰丑先问州吁。  
Youzai Chou first asked Zhouxu  
还有什么遗言。  
whether he had any last words.  
州吁仰天长叹。  
Zhouxu sighed toward the heavens.  
说自己是被小人算计了。  
He said he had been undone by petty men（小人）.  
从前只知道组织未必靠得住。  
He had once known that institutions might fail a man.  
没想到朋友也靠不住。  
He had not expected his friends to fail him as well.  
轮到石厚时。  
When it was Shi Hou's turn,  
獳羊肩也问他。  
Nuyang Jian asked him the same question.  
石厚摇头苦笑。  
Shi Hou shook his head with a bitter smile.  
他说，不只是朋友靠不住。  
He said it was not merely that friends could not be trusted.  
更没想到，连亲爹也靠不住。  
He had never imagined that even his own father could not be trusted.  
《左传》最后感叹道。  
At the end, the *Zuo Commentary* remarks  
这就叫“大义灭亲”。  
that this is what "placing righteousness above kin" means.  
这个成语，也就从这里来。  
That is where the idiom comes from.  
可这种做法就一定对吗？  
But is such conduct necessarily right?  
作者说，也未必。  
The author says not necessarily.  
后面的故事里，还会再看见。  
Later stories will give us more to think about.

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`时间状语从句`、`宾语从句`、`让步状语从句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `Although he arrested Zhouxu, he did not want the execution carried out in his own state`。<br>
难点拆解：`Although he arrested ...` 是让步状语从句；`He said he had been undone ...`、`He asked whether ...` 都带宾语从句；`When the moment of execution arrived` 则是时间状语从句，把结尾推进到高潮。<br>
常用短语：
- `on the spot`：当场
- `take custody`：接手看管
- `put something starkly`：把话说得很硬，很明白
- `place righteousness above kin`：大义灭亲

## 第九章 荒淫无度
## Chapter 9: Unrestrained Debauchery

新台鲜明，河水浩荡。  
Bright stands the New Tower, while the river stretches vast and full.  
本想嫁个温柔郎君。  
She had hoped for a gentle bridegroom.  
却偏偏碰上缩脖丑汉。  
Instead she ended up with a hunchback（驼背者） of a man.  
新台高高，黄河平远。  
High rises the New Tower, while the Yellow River lies broad and level.  
本想嫁个称心郎君。  
She had dreamed of the right husband.  
却偏偏碰上癞皮丑夫。  
Yet what she got was a loathsome（令人厌恶的） brute.  
下网原本想捞鱼。  
The nets had been cast in hopes of fish.  
结果网里却是怪物。  
Instead, something monstrous（怪异的） came up in them.  
本想嫁个好郎君。  
She had hoped to marry well.  
却偏偏嫁了个缩脖老丑男。  
But she was given an ugly, crook-necked old husband.  
这几句，出自《诗经·邶风·新台》。  
These lines come from "Xintai" in the *Airs of Bei* section of the *Book of Songs*.  
这首诗，说的正是下面的故事。  
And the story it points to is exactly the one that follows.

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`宾语从句`、`并列句`、`结果表达`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `She had hoped for a gentle bridegroom, but instead ended up with a hunchback of a man`。<br>
难点拆解：前半句 `had hoped for ...` 表示原本期待；后半句 `ended up with ...` 表示实际结果，形成强反差；英语里这种“期待 A，结果却是 B”的写法很常见。<br>
常用短语：
- `end up with`：最后得到，结果碰上
- `point to`：指向，暗示
- `in hopes of`：怀着……的希望
- `exactly the one that follows`：正是下面接着要说的那个

### 勾搭后妈
### Taking Up with His Stepmother

石碏大义灭亲以后。  
After Shi Que placed righteousness above kin,  
卫国把逃到邢国的公子晋迎了回来。  
Wei welcomed back Prince Jin, who had taken refuge in Xing.  
他是卫桓公的同母弟弟。  
He was Duke Huan of Wei's full younger brother.  
他即位以后，就是卫宣公。  
Once he took the throne, he became Duke Xuan of Wei.  
要是谁以为正统一定更好。  
If anyone thinks legitimacy must always mean goodness,  
那就大错特错了。  
he is badly mistaken.  
这个卫宣公。  
This Duke Xuan of Wei  
论坏，比州吁只多不少。  
was every bit as bad as Zhouxu, and perhaps worse.  
说句公道话。  
To speak fairly,  
石碏大义灭亲，最后却弄回这么个人。  
Shi Que destroyed his own kin for justice, only to bring back a man like this.  
实在不值。  
It was hardly worth it.  
宣公没有州吁那么能干。  
Duke Xuan was not as capable as Zhouxu.  
身边也没有石厚那样的帮手。  
Nor did he have an accomplice（同伙） like Shi Hou at his side.  
可在荒淫和无耻上。  
But in lechery（淫荡） and shamelessness（无耻）,  
他却更胜一筹。  
he surpassed him.  
他父亲庄公有个小妾。  
His father Duke Zhuang had a concubine.  
名叫夷姜。  
Her name was Yi Jiang.  
她年轻，又生得漂亮。  
She was young and beautiful.  
宣公很早就和她勾搭上了。  
Duke Xuan had begun an affair（私通） with her long before.  
等庄公一死。  
Once Duke Zhuang died,  
宣公索性把夷姜偷偷运出宫。  
Duke Xuan simply smuggled Yi Jiang out of the palace.  
另外找地方把她藏了起来。  
Then he hid her away in another place.  
后来夷姜生了个儿子。  
Later Yi Jiang bore him a son.  
名字叫急子，也叫伋。  
The boy was named Jizi, also called Ji.  
等宣公自己当上国君。  
After Duke Xuan himself became ruler,  
他又把夷姜立成了夫人。  
he raised Yi Jiang to the rank of consort.  
急子也就成了世子。  
And Jizi became the heir.  
等急子长到十六七岁。  
By the time Jizi was sixteen or seventeen,  
已经是天下闻名的美少年。  
he was already famous throughout the realm as a beautiful youth.  
他眉清目秀，风度翩翩。  
He was fine-featured and graceful.  
文武双全。  
He excelled in both letters and arms.  
剑术尤其出色。  
His swordsmanship（剑术） was especially superb.  
人们都说。  
People all said  
卫国这位世子，是天下第一公子。  
that Wei's heir was the finest prince under heaven.  
宣公有这么个儿子。  
With a son like this,  
自然想给他找门顶好的亲事。  
Duke Xuan naturally wanted to find him the best possible marriage.  
于是，他把这件事交给了右公子姬职。  
So he entrusted the matter to Right Prince Ji Zhi.  
右公子接了差事后细细一盘算。  
After taking the assignment, Right Prince thought it through carefully.  
急子这么出众。  
Jizi was so exceptional  
自然得配天下最漂亮的姑娘。  
that he deserved the most beautiful girl under heaven.  
一打听。  
After asking around,  
大家都说齐僖公的大女儿最美。  
everyone said Duke Xi of Qi's eldest daughter was the beauty to beat.  
于是右公子带着礼物和文书去了齐国。  
So Right Prince took gifts and formal papers to Qi.  
齐僖公一听是给急子求婚。  
The moment Duke Xi heard it was a marriage proposal for Jizi,  
答应得非常痛快。  
he agreed readily.  
临行前。  
Before the envoy left,  
他还特意把大女儿叫出来见了一面。  
he even had his eldest daughter come out to be seen.  
好让右公子放心。  
That way Right Prince could feel reassured.  
右公子回国以后。  
After returning to Wei,  
把这位齐国公主夸到了天上。  
Right Prince praised the princess to the skies.  
说她和急子正是天造地设。  
He said she and Jizi were a match made in heaven.  
卫宣公听着听着。  
As Duke Xuan listened,  
心里却起了贼念。  
a thief's desire began to stir within him.  
右公子做梦也没想到。  
Right Prince never dreamed  
宣公竟会对未来儿媳动心。  
that Duke Xuan would lust after his own future daughter-in-law.

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`条件状语从句`、`宾语从句`、`比较结构`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `If anyone thinks legitimacy must always mean goodness, he is badly mistaken`。<br>
难点拆解：`If anyone thinks ...` 是条件状语从句；`everyone said that ...`、`He said that ...` 都带宾语从句；`every bit as bad as ...` 是很常见的比较结构，用来强调“至少一样坏”。<br>
常用短语：
- `take refuge in`：逃到……避难
- `to speak fairly`：说句公道话
- `think something through`：把事情想透
- `a match made in heaven`：天造地设的一对

### 新台，强占儿媳
### The New Tower and the Seizing of a Son's Bride

卫宣公在淇水边修了一座行宫。  
Duke Xuan built a retreat by the Qi River.  
名字就叫新台。  
He named it the New Tower.  
对外的说法很好听。  
The public explanation sounded perfectly respectable（体面的）.  
说是给朝中公卿消暑度假的。  
It was supposedly for ministers to rest and escape the summer heat.  
其实，他另有打算。  
In truth, he had another design（图谋）.  
新台修得又快又华丽。  
The New Tower was built quickly and lavishly（奢华地）.  
设计漂亮。  
Its design was elegant.  
用料也都是最好的。  
And the materials used were the best available.  
等新台一修好。  
Once the New Tower was finished,  
宣公就开始动手了。  
Duke Xuan began to act.  
他把急子叫来。  
He summoned Jizi.  
说宋国国君近日做寿。  
He said the ruler of Song was soon celebrating a birthday.  
你替我走一趟。  
"Go in my place.  
也算出去历练历练。  
It will do you good to see the world a little."  
急子就这样被打发去了宋国。  
And so Jizi was sent off to Song.  
右公子也一同随行。  
Right Prince went with him as well.  
等急子一走。  
As soon as Jizi was gone,  
宣公又找来左公子泄。  
Duke Xuan summoned Left Prince Xie.  
命他去齐国迎亲。  
He ordered him to go fetch the bride from Qi.  
临走前还特意叮嘱。  
Before he departed, Duke Xuan gave special instructions.  
别把人送去都城。  
The bride was not to be taken into the capital.  
直接送到新台来。  
She was to be brought straight to the New Tower.  
我得先替急子把把关。  
"I must inspect her for Jizi first."  
齐国公主一路风尘来到卫国。  
The princess of Qi came all the way to Wei through travel and dust.  
她知道自己要嫁的人。  
She knew the man she was to marry  
是天下闻名的急子。  
was the famed Jizi.  
心里自然又羞又喜。  
Naturally, she was shy and delighted.  
等她到了新台。  
When she reached the New Tower,  
看见宫殿华丽新巧。  
she saw a palace both splendid and new.  
还以为是急子特意为她准备的。  
She even thought Jizi had prepared it specially for her.  
心里便更激动了。  
That made her all the more excited.  
可这场婚礼。  
Yet the wedding itself  
总透着一点古怪。  
carried an air of oddness（古怪）.  
排场并不热闹。  
It was not especially lively.  
宾客也不算多。  
There were not many guests either.  
她虽觉得奇怪。  
Though she found it strange,  
也没有深想。  
she did not think too much of it.  
拜完天地以后。  
Once the rites were done,  
夫妻便进了洞房。  
the bride and groom entered the bridal chamber.  
新郎一进去，就吹灭了灯。  
The groom blew out the lamp as soon as he came in.  
接着就是脱衣、上床。  
Then came the undressing and the bed.  
公主只觉得。  
The princess felt only this:  
有两只肥厚的大手抱住了自己。  
two thick, heavy hands closed around her.  
随后，一身肥肉压了下来。  
Then a weight of fat flesh pressed down on her.  
她忍不住失望地问。  
In disappointment（失望）, she could not help asking,  
急子，你怎么这么胖？  
"Jizi, how can you be so fat?"  
回答她的。  
What answered her  
只有粗重的喘息声。  
was nothing but coarse breathing.  
她又问。  
She asked again,  
急子，你怎么不说话？  
"Jizi, why won't you speak?"  
回给她的。  
What came back to her  
仍然只是喘息。  
was still only breath.  
她心里越发失望。  
Her disappointment deepened.  
她原以为急子。  
She had imagined Jizi  
该是个瘦削健美的少年。  
as a lean, vigorous youth.  
可床上的人。  
But the man in the bed  
却又肥又老道。  
was both fat and practiced.  
手法熟练。  
His hands were experienced.  
花样也多。  
His tricks were many.  
哪里像个没成过亲的少年。  
He was nothing like an unmarried boy.  
这一夜，也就这样过去了。  
And so the night passed.

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`时间状语从句`、`宾语从句`、`结果表达`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `As soon as Jizi was gone, Duke Xuan summoned Left Prince Xie`。<br>
难点拆解：`As soon as Jizi was gone` 是时间状语从句，表示前脚一走后脚就动手；`She thought Jizi had prepared it specially for her` 是宾语从句；后面多组短句连用，突出公主从期待到失望的落差。<br>
常用短语：
- `escape the summer heat`：避暑
- `as soon as`：一……就……
- `think too much of`：太往心里去，太当回事
- `nothing but`：只有，不过是

天一亮。  
At daybreak,  
公主就在鼾声里醒了过来。  
the princess woke to the sound of snoring.  
她转头去看自己的夫君。  
She turned to look at her husband.  
不看还好。  
Had she not looked, it might have been better.  
这一看，浑身都起了鸡皮疙瘩。  
But one glance brought gooseflesh all over her body.  
身边躺着的。  
The man lying beside her  
哪里是什么急子。  
was no Jizi at all.  
肥头大耳。  
He had a bloated face and large ears.  
满脸麻子。  
His face was covered in pockmarks.  
张着嘴。  
His mouth hung open.  
嘴角还淌着口水。  
And drool ran from the corner of it.  
这哪里像十七岁的急子。  
How could this be the seventeen-year-old Jizi?  
简直就是只挨霜的癞蛤蟆。  
He looked like nothing so much as a frost-bitten toad.  
公主尖叫了一声。  
The princess let out a scream.  
那丑人醒了。  
The ugly man woke.  
还冲她嘿嘿一笑。  
And gave her a greasy grin.  
她颤着声音问。  
Her voice trembling, she asked,  
你，你是急子？  
"Y-you are Jizi?"  
对方笑着说。  
The other man laughed and said,  
我是急子。  
"I am Jizi's..."  
——他爹。  
"father."  
公主当场就昏了过去。  
The princess fainted on the spot.  
于是，也就有了《诗经》里的那首《新台》。  
And that is how the poem "New Tower" entered the *Book of Songs*.

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`虚拟条件句`、`倒装`、`被动表达`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `Had she not looked, it might have been better`。<br>
