难点拆解：这里用了省略 `if` 的虚拟条件倒装；`The princess fainted on the spot` 是主干动作；最后 `entered the Book of Songs` 用被动意义很强的历史叙述收束结果。<br>
常用短语：
- `at daybreak`：天一亮
- `nothing so much as`：简直像……
- `on the spot`：当场
- `voice trembling`：声音发颤

霸占儿媳以后。  
After seizing his own son's bride,  
宣公也不知是心虚。  
whether from guilt  
还是想找个清静地方慢慢享受。  
or from a desire to enjoy her in peace,  
总之就赖在新台不走了。  
Duke Xuan stayed on at the New Tower and would not leave.  
齐国公主纵然出身大国。  
Though the princess came from a great state,  
此刻落到宣公手里。  
once she had fallen into Duke Xuan's hands,  
也实在没有办法。  
she had no way out.  
她哭过，求过。  
She cried and pleaded.  
连寻死都试过。  
She even tried to kill herself.  
可都没有效果。  
None of it had any effect（效果）.  
最后她只能认命。  
In the end, she had no choice but to accept her fate.  
后来连齐僖公知道了这件事。  
Even when Duke Xi of Qi later learned what had happened,  
也只能接受现实。  
he could only accept reality.  
生米已经煮成熟饭。  
The rice had already been cooked.  
事情再没有回头路。  
There was no turning back.  
从这以后。  
From then on,  
齐国公主不再是急子的未婚妻。  
the princess of Qi was no longer Jizi's betrothed（未婚妻）.  
而成了宣公的夫人宣姜。  
She became Duke Xuan's wife, Xuan Jiang.  
勾搭后妈，又强占儿媳。  
He took up with his father's woman, then seized his son's bride.  
这个卫宣公。  
This Duke Xuan of Wei  
真是三代通吃。  
truly devoured three generations in one sweep.

回到都城是什么时候？  
And when did he finally return to the capital?  
一直等到宣姜肚子已经大起来。  
Not until Xuan Jiang's belly had begun to swell.  
回城以后。  
After returning to the capital,  
宣公做的第一件事。  
the very first thing Duke Xuan did  
就是赶紧再给急子娶个老婆。  
was hurry to find Jizi another wife.  
这也算是补偿。  
That counted as compensation, of a sort.  
急子默默接受了。  
Jizi accepted it in silence.  
他是孝子。  
He was a filial son.  
他愿意把最好的让给父亲。  
He was willing to yield the best to his father.  
更何况。  
Besides,  
他其实也没得选。  
he had no real choice.  
急子成亲以后。  
After Jizi married,  
便搬出了后宫。  
he moved out of the inner palace.  
宣公急着给他娶妻。  
Duke Xuan's haste in arranging that marriage  
真正的用意并不复杂。  
had a purpose that was not complicated.  
一是让儿子搬远一点。  
First, he wanted his son out of the way.  
二是免得急子和宣姜碰面。  
Second, he wanted to prevent meetings between Jizi and Xuan Jiang.  
说得再直白一点。  
To put it more bluntly,  
他怕儿子把本来属于自己的女人勾走。  
he feared his son might lure away the woman who had originally been meant for him.  
宣姜因此更郁闷了。  
Xuan Jiang grew even more depressed（郁闷的） because of it.  
整天躲在房里，不肯出门。  
She stayed hidden in her chambers all day and would not come out.  
这倒正合宣公的心意。  
That, of course, suited Duke Xuan perfectly.

### 偶遇，重燃希望
### A Chance Encounter Rekindles Hope

转眼之间。  
Before long,  
宣姜肚子越来越大。  
Xuan Jiang's belly grew larger and larger.  
十月怀胎，一朝分娩。  
Ten months pregnant, then one day delivered.  
她生下了一个可爱的小公子。  
She gave birth to an adorable young prince.  
名字叫寿。  
His name was Shou.  
孩子的出生。  
The child's birth  
让宣姜的心情好了不少。  
lightened Xuan Jiang's mood considerably.  
不管怎么说。  
Whatever else might be said,  
孩子总是可爱的。  
the child was lovable.  
慢慢地。  
Gradually,  
她开始愿意抱着孩子出来走走。  
she became willing to come outside carrying him.  
看看花草，逗逗鱼虫。  
She would look at flowers and tease fish and insects.  
世界其实很小。  
The world is, in truth, very small.  
所以宣姜碰上急子。  
So Xuan Jiang running into Jizi  
几乎是注定要发生的。  
was almost bound to happen.  
那一天，阳光正好。  
That day, the sunlight was just right.  
微风也很轻。  
The breeze was light as well.  
宫女抱着孩子。  
A maid was carrying the child.  
宣姜在宫里慢慢散步。  
Xuan Jiang was strolling slowly through the palace grounds.  
迎面走来一个年轻公子。  
A young prince came walking toward her.  
谁？  
Who?  
正是急子。  
Jizi himself.  
虽然他早就搬了出去。  
Though he had long since moved out,  
还是常常会回来探望母亲。  
he still often came back to visit his mother.  
两人一照面。  
The moment they saw one another,  
都愣住了。  
both froze.  
宣姜先在心里惊了一下。  
Xuan Jiang was struck first in her heart.  
这就是急子？  
So this was Jizi?  
她眼前的少年。  
The youth before her  
玉树临风，英气逼人。  
was straight as jade and full of heroic grace.  
比传闻里还要好看。  
He was even more beautiful than rumor had promised.  
急子那边也一样。  
Jizi was no different.  
他早知道宣姜漂亮。  
He had always known Xuan Jiang was beautiful.  
却没想到她会漂亮到这个地步。  
But he had never imagined beauty like this.  
即便才生完孩子不久。  
Even though she had not long since given birth,  
她仍然艳光四射。  
she still dazzled the eye.  
两人四目一对。  
Their eyes met.  
脸都红了。  
And both faces flushed.  
连旁边几个宫女都看呆了。  
Even the maids nearby were dumbstruck（看呆的）.  
这简直就是传说里的金童玉女。  
They looked like the very golden boy and jade girl of legend.  
急子慌忙走开。  
Jizi hurried away.  
连头也不敢回。  
He did not dare turn his head.  
因为他分明能感觉到。  
Because he could clearly feel  
宣姜灼热的目光。  
Xuan Jiang's burning gaze  
一直扑在自己后背上。  
still striking his back.  
从那次偶遇以后。  
After that chance meeting,  
两个人都陷进了痛苦里。  
both of them fell into anguish（痛苦）.  
宣姜原本已经认命。  
Xuan Jiang had already resigned herself to fate.  
可这一回。  
But this time,  
她心里的希望又烧起来了。  
hope flared back to life in her heart.  
急子才是她要的人。  
Jizi was the man she wanted.  
才是她本来就该得到的人。  
He was the man she had originally been meant to have.  
她甚至想。  
She even thought  
如果能和急子一起走。  
that if she could leave with Jizi,  
就算逃到边地。  
even if they fled to the borderlands,  
下田种地。  
worked the fields,  
也心甘情愿。  
she would be perfectly willing.  
宣姜是个敢想也敢做的人。  
Xuan Jiang was a woman bold in both thought and deed.  
她很快就下了决心。  
She made up her mind quickly.  
一定要找机会和急子私奔。  
She would find a chance to elope（私奔） with Jizi.  
急子那边却更挣扎。  
On Jizi's side, the struggle was fiercer.  
人不是草木。  
Human beings are not wood or grass.  
面对这样的美人。  
Before a beauty like this,  
谁也很难泰然自若。  
it is hard for anyone to remain entirely calm.  
急子从前虽然一忍再忍。  
Jizi had endured and endured before.  
也曾认过命。  
He had even tried to accept his lot.  
可现在。  
But now  
他忘不掉宣姜的容貌。  
he could not forget Xuan Jiang's face.  
更忘不掉她那双勾人的眼睛。  
Still less could he forget her spellbinding（勾人的） eyes.  
他心里像打翻了五味瓶。  
It was as if the five flavors had been spilled inside him.  
酸甜苦辣咸全乱在一起。  
Sour, sweet, bitter, hot, and salty all mixed together.  
他忍不住想。  
He could not help thinking  
她原本就该是我的女人。  
that she had originally been meant to be his woman.  
可到底该怎么办。  
But what exactly should he do?  
他不像宣姜那样果断。  
He was not as decisive as Xuan Jiang.  
但他又实在压不住。  
Yet he truly could not suppress  
想再见她的冲动。  
the urge to see her again.

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`时间状语从句`、`宾语从句`、`分词结构`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `The moment they saw one another, both froze`。<br>
难点拆解：`The moment they saw one another` 是时间状语从句；`He could not help thinking that ...` 里 `that ...` 是宾语从句；后面 `burning gaze` 这种分词色彩很强的表达，能让画面更鲜明。<br>
常用短语：
- `be bound to happen`：注定会发生
- `flare back to life`：重新燃起来
- `cannot help doing`：忍不住做……
- `remain entirely calm`：始终保持镇定

尽管想法上还有差别。  
Though there were still differences in what they wanted,  
行动上却已经有了默契。  
their actions had already fallen into tacit agreement（默契）.  
宣姜开始常去夷姜那里坐。  
Xuan Jiang began visiting Yi Jiang more often.  
明里是聊天。  
On the surface, she went to chat.  
暗里是想碰上急子。  
In truth, she hoped to run into Jizi.  
急子也去得更勤了。  
Jizi too began visiting more frequently.  
表面上是看母亲。  
On the surface, he was visiting his mother.  
实际上也是想碰上宣姜。  
In reality, he hoped to meet Xuan Jiang as well.  
偏偏这时的宣公。  
And Duke Xuan, at this very time,  
已经放松了警惕。  
had relaxed his vigilance（警惕）.  
他以为有了公子寿。  
He assumed that now Xuan Jiang had borne Prince Shou,  
宣姜就会彻底跟定自己。  
she would remain completely devoted to him.  
两人确实遇见过。  
The two did indeed meet.  
而且不止一次。  
And not just once.  
每次碰见。  
Every time they crossed paths,  
宣姜都会暗送秋波。  
Xuan Jiang sent furtive glances（秋波） and silent messages.  
急子有时紧张。  
Jizi sometimes grew tense.  
有时只能微笑。  
Sometimes he could do no more than smile.  
因为夷姜在场。  
Because Yi Jiang was there,  
两人也只能做到这一步。  
that was as far as they could go.  
终于有一天。  
At last, one day,  
两人又在夷姜屋里碰上。  
they met again in Yi Jiang's room.  
偏偏夷姜刚好出去方便。  
And Yi Jiang had happened to step out for a moment.  
屋里只剩他们两个。  
The room was left to just the two of them.  
宣姜再也忍不住。  
Xuan Jiang could no longer hold back.  
她开门见山地问。  
She asked bluntly,  
为什么我每次给你使眼色。  
"Why is it  
你都装作没看见？  
that every time I send you a look, you pretend not to see it?"  
急子不敢直视她。  
Jizi did not dare look directly at her.  
他怕自己一看就把持不住。  
He feared that one look would undo his restraint（克制）.  
只说自己真的没看见。  
He said only that he truly had not seen.  
宣姜马上追问。  
Xuan Jiang pressed on at once.  
难道我不够美？  
"Am I not beautiful enough?"  
急子说，不，你很美。  
Jizi said, "No. You are very beautiful."  
宣姜又问。  
Xuan Jiang asked again,  
难道你不知道。  
"Do you not know  
我原本就是你的女人？  
that I was originally your woman?"  
急子顿了一下。  
Jizi paused.  
低声说。  
Then said in a low voice,  
可是，那是我父亲。  
"But he is my father."  
宣姜立刻反问。  
Xuan Jiang shot back at once,  
你父亲又怎样？  
"And what of your father?  
我是你的。  
I am yours.  
你为什么不带我走？  
Why won't you take me away?"  
急子一时答不上来。  
For a moment Jizi had no answer.  
其实这个念头。  
In truth, the thought  
并不是没在他心里动过。  
had not failed to move through him before.  
他只是一直下不了决心。  
He simply had never been able to decide.  
最后，他问出自己最大的顾虑。  
At last, he voiced his greatest concern.  
那公子寿怎么办？  
"What about Prince Shou?"  
宣姜几乎想也不想。  
Xuan Jiang answered almost without thinking.  
带走。  
"Take him with us."  
急子更慌了。  
Jizi grew more alarmed.  
可公子寿是我弟弟啊。  
"But Prince Shou is my younger brother."  
这话里的意思已经很明白。  
The meaning of those words was already clear.  
那样会乱了纲常。  
It would throw kinship and propriety（纲常） into chaos.  
偏偏就在这时。  
And precisely then,  
夷姜回来了。  
Yi Jiang returned.  
于是，对话也就断了。  
And the conversation was cut short.

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`宾语从句`、`条件状语从句`、`反问句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `He feared that one look would undo his restraint`。<br>
难点拆解：`that one look would undo ...` 是宾语从句；`every time I send you a look` 也带时间条件色彩；后面连续几个反问句不是为了求答案，而是为了逼对方表态。<br>
常用短语：
- `on the surface`：表面上
- `press on`：继续追问
- `have no answer`：一时答不上来
- `cut short`：中途打断

### 私奔，晚了一步
### Eloping, One Step Too Late

那次谈话以后。  
After that conversation,  
急子的心更乱了。  
Jizi's heart became even more unsettled（不安的）.  
父亲和美人之间。  
Between his father and the woman he desired,  
他只能选一个。  
he could choose only one.  
这是个极难的选择。  
It was a brutally difficult choice.  
有时他梦见。  
Some nights he dreamed  
自己带着宣姜远走高飞。  
that he had fled far away with Xuan Jiang.  
有时又梦见。  
On other nights he dreamed  
自己正色拒绝她。  
that he was refusing her sternly.  
他迟迟下不了决心。  
He could not make up his mind.  
一晃，就是两个多月。  
In a flash, more than two months were gone.  
这两个月里。  
During those two months,  
他再也不敢去夷姜那里。  
he no longer dared go to Yi Jiang's quarters.  
他怕见到宣姜。  
He feared seeing Xuan Jiang.  
更怕见到她那双会勾人的眼睛。  
More than that, he feared those enticing（勾魂的） eyes.  
有困惑时，人总会想起老师。  
When troubled, a man naturally thinks of his teacher.  
急子终于鼓起勇气。  
At last, Jizi gathered his courage.  
把自己的苦恼全说给右公子听。  
He told Right Prince all his distress.  
老师，我到底该怎么办？  
"Master, what should I do?"  
右公子没有立刻回答。  
Right Prince did not answer at once.  
他先问急子。  
He first asked Jizi,  
你真那么看重世子的位子吗？  
"Do you truly value the position of heir so highly?"  
急子说，自己兴趣不大。  
Jizi said he cared little for it.  
右公子听完以后。  
After hearing that, Right Prince  
态度就很干脆了。  
took an immediate and firm stand（态度鲜明）.  
那就带她走。  
"Then take her and go.  
也不用逃到戎狄去。  
There is no need to flee among the Rong tribes.  
直接去齐国就好。  
Go straight to Qi.  
齐侯原本就想让你做女婿。  
The duke of Qi originally wanted you as his son-in-law.  
他不会怪你。  
He will not blame you."  
右公子其实一直对这件事耿耿于怀。  
Right Prince had brooded over this affair all along.  
在他心里。  
In his heart,  
宣公实在不是个东西。  
Duke Xuan was a thoroughly despicable（卑鄙的） man.  
急子却还有顾虑。  
Jizi still had his scruples（顾虑）.  
这样做，名分上太难听。  
"If we do this, it will sound terrible in terms of status and name."  
右公子却把话说得更直。  
But Right Prince spoke even more bluntly.  
还谈什么名分？  
"What talk is there of proper names now?  
你难道不知道。  
Do you not know  
你母亲当年原本也是你祖父的人？  
that your mother herself had once belonged to your grandfather?  
你父亲做得出来。  
Your father could do such a thing.  
你怎么反倒不敢？  
Why should you alone not dare?  
更何况。  
Besides,  
那原本就是你的妻子。  
she was originally your wife."  
右公子前前后后。  
All in all,  
做了许多思想工作。  
Right Prince spent a long time persuading him.  
最后终于把急子的心说动了。  
At last he moved Jizi's heart.  
急子终于点头。  
Jizi finally nodded.  
好，我听老师的。  
"Very well. I will do as you say."  
决心一定。  
Once the decision was made,  
急子就开始准备。  
Jizi began to prepare.  
盘缠、衣物、路线。  
Travel money, clothing, and route—  
样样都备妥了。  
all were made ready.  
又过了十多天。  
Another ten days passed.  
急子进宫看母亲。  
Jizi went into the palace to visit his mother.  
表面上是看母亲。  
On the surface, he was visiting his mother.  
实际上却是来找宣姜。  
In truth, he had come to look for Xuan Jiang.  
母子说话时。  
While mother and son were talking,  
急子顺口问起宣姜近来怎么样。  
Jizi casually asked how Xuan Jiang had been lately.  
夷姜答道。  
Yi Jiang answered,  
她一个多月没来过了。  
"She has not come by in more than a month."  
急子吃了一惊。  
Jizi started in surprise.  
忙问为什么。  
He hurried to ask why.  
夷姜答得很平常。  
Yi Jiang answered matter-of-factly,  
她又怀上了。  
"She is pregnant again."  
急子只觉得脑子轰的一声。  
Jizi felt his head ring with a dull roar.  
就在那一刻。  
In that instant,  
他知道一切都晚了。  
he knew it was all too late.  
从前只有一个公子寿。  
Before, there had only been Prince Shou.  
这已经够麻烦。  
That had already been trouble enough.  
如今又多了一个未出生的孩子。  
Now there was another child yet unborn.  
将来就是两个弟弟。  
Soon there would be two younger brothers.  
这还怎么带她走？  
How could he possibly take her away now?  
更何况她还大着肚子。  
And besides, she was already heavily pregnant.  
等她把孩子生下来。  
By the time she gave birth,  
一切机会都早已凉透了。  
every chance would long since have gone cold.

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`条件状语从句`、`宾语从句`、`反问句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `By the time she gave birth, every chance would long since have gone cold`。<br>
难点拆解：`By the time she gave birth` 是时间状语从句；`He said he cared little for it`、`He answered that ...` 这些都是宾语从句；`How could he possibly take her away now?` 是强烈反问，用来表现绝望。<br>
常用短语：
- `make up one's mind`：下定决心
- `all along`：一直以来
- `matter-of-factly`：平静而直接地
- `go cold`：凉掉，失去可能

机会果然凉透了。  
And indeed, the chance went cold for good.  
宣姜后来成了两个孩子的母亲。  
Xuan Jiang later became the mother of two sons.  
她终于死了私奔的心。  
At last she gave up the thought of elopement.  
急子则不只是死心。  
As for Jizi, it was more than resignation.  
简直像连心都死了。  
It was as if his heart itself had died.  
转眼间。  
In time,  
孩子们也长大了。  
the children grew up as well.  
大的叫寿。  
The elder was Shou.  
小的叫朔。  
The younger was Shuo.  
急子把所有苦闷都压在心里。  
Jizi pressed all his bitterness down within.  
他仍旧爱宣姜。  
He still loved Xuan Jiang.  
可他也知道。  
But he also knew  
这一切再也不可能了。  
that none of it was possible anymore.  
于是他把对宣姜的爱。  
So he turned the love he bore Xuan Jiang  
转到了她两个儿子身上。  
toward her two sons.  
他喜欢寿，也喜欢朔。  
He liked Shou, and he liked Shuo as well.  
只要有机会。  
Whenever he had the chance,  
就带着他们玩。  
he took them out and played with them.  
可宣姜并不知道这些。  
But Xuan Jiang knew none of this.  
她只知道一件事。  
She knew only one thing.  
急子当初拒绝了她。  
Jizi had rejected her.  
于是她开始恨他。  
And so she began to hate him.  
她恨自己得不到他。  
She hated that she could not have him.  
她不再想和他私奔。  
She no longer dreamed of eloping with him.  
她开始想的是怎么报复。  
She began instead to think of revenge（报复）.  
世上最可怕的恨。  
The most terrible hatred in the world  
往往都是由爱变来的。  
is often the kind born out of love.  
而女人因爱生恨。  
And when a woman turns from love to hatred,  
更是叫人害怕。  
the result can be even more frightening.

### 报复，终于来到
### Revenge Finally Arrives

一晃十六年过去。  
In a flash, sixteen years went by.  
到了卫宣公十八年。  
It was now the eighteenth year of Duke Xuan of Wei.  
也就是前701年。  
That is, 701 BCE.  
急子三十三岁。  
Jizi was thirty-three.  
公子寿也十六岁了。  
Prince Shou was sixteen as well.  
而宣公因为荒淫过度。  
And Duke Xuan, worn down by excess（纵欲）,  
身体已经一天不如一天。  
was growing weaker day by day.  
这些年来。  
Over all those years,  
宣姜始终没找到报复急子的机会。  
Xuan Jiang had never found a chance to strike back at Jizi.  
其实她也明白。  
In truth, she understood why.  
急子品行太正。  
Jizi's conduct was too upright.  
做事又太谨慎。  
His actions were too careful.  
几乎抓不到任何破绽。  
He offered almost no opening（破绽） at all.  
而整个卫国上下。  
And throughout Wei,  
喜欢他的人又实在太多。  
too many people truly liked him.  
可宣姜也知道。  
But Xuan Jiang also knew  
自己剩下的时间已经不多了。  
that the time left to her was running short.  
一旦宣公死了。  
Once Duke Xuan died,  
急子继位。  
and Jizi succeeded him,  
她就再也没有机会。  
she would never have another chance.  
所以，她决定先下手。  
So she decided to move first.  
这天宣公又病倒在床上。  
That day Duke Xuan was once again sick in bed.  
宣姜和公子朔坐在床边。  
Xuan Jiang sat by the bed with Prince Shuo.  
还把宫女们都打发了出去。  
She sent all the maids away.  
然后，她便哭了起来。  
Then she began to cry.  
哭声把宣公从睡梦里惊醒。  
Her sobbing woke Duke Xuan from sleep.  
他一睁眼就问。  
The moment he opened his eyes, he asked,  
你为什么哭？  
"Why are you crying?  
难道是我要死了？  
Is it because I am about to die?"  
宣姜说。  
Xuan Jiang answered  
不是你要死。  
that it was not he who was going to die.  
是我们母子三人快活不成了。  
It was she and her two sons who could no longer go on living.  
宣公听得一头雾水。  
Duke Xuan was utterly confused（迷糊的） by this.  
你们为什么会死？  
"Why would the three of you die?"  
宣姜便开始造谣。  
That was when Xuan Jiang began to slander（诬陷）.  
她说，急子一直恨他们母子。  
She said Jizi had long hated her and her sons.  
天天发誓要杀了寿和朔。  
He swore every day to kill Shou and Shuo.  
然后再把她抢回去做妻子。  
And then seize her back as his wife.  
宣公一听，心里立刻发紧。  
The moment Duke Xuan heard this, his heart tightened.  
因为他最怕的。  
For what he feared most  
就是儿子把宣姜抢回去。  
was his son taking Xuan Jiang away from him.  
可单靠一张嘴。  
But mere words alone  
又让他有点迟疑。  
still made him hesitate.  
宣姜见他迟疑。  
Seeing that hesitation,  
干脆一把拉过公子朔。  
Xuan Jiang pulled Prince Shuo over at once.  
她说。  
She said,  
你要是不管。  
"If you will not act,  
反正迟早我也要做急子的老婆。  
then sooner or later I will become Jizi's wife anyway.  
朔，来，叫爷爷。  
Shuo, come, call him Grandfather."  
这一下正戳中宣公的软肋。  
That struck Duke Xuan exactly at his weakest point.  
这种笑话。  
Such a humiliation  
他是绝对受不了的。  
was something he could never bear.  
于是他终于问。  
So at last he asked,  
那你说怎么办？  
"Then what do you say should be done?"  
宣姜冷冷回道。  
Xuan Jiang answered coldly,  
他不死，我们就得死。  
"If he does not die, then we must.  
你自己看着办。  
You decide."  
宣公一咬牙。  
Duke Xuan clenched his teeth.  
终于决定杀掉自己的长子。  
At last he decided to kill his eldest son.  
可明着杀肯定不行。  
But he certainly could not kill him openly.  
那样名声就全完了。  
That would ruin his name completely.  
于是他想了个毒计。  
So he came up with a venomous（恶毒的） scheme.  
齐国正准备讨伐纪国。  
Qi was preparing to attack Ji.  
卫国也要派人去约定出兵日期。  
Wei too had to send an envoy to settle the date of troops.  
使者要执白旄而行。  
The envoy would travel bearing the white mao banner.  
路上会乘船。  
Part of the journey would be by boat.  
到了莘野就得下船。  
At Xinye he would disembark.  
只要在那里埋伏下人。  
If men were hidden there in ambush（埋伏）,  
见持白旄者就杀。  
and ordered to kill whoever carried the white mao,  
再把白旄和人头带回来领赏。  
then they could bring back the banner and the head for reward.  
这就是宣公的主意。  
That was Duke Xuan's plan.  
什么是白旄？  
What was the white mao?  
那是诸侯使者出行时的信标。  
It was the identifying standard（信标） carried by a feudal envoy.  
旗端系着白色牦牛尾。  
White yak-tail hair was tied to the top of it.  
也正因如此。  
And for that very reason,  
埋伏的人只认旗，不必认人。  
the killers in ambush needed only recognize the banner, not the man.  
宣公和宣姜自以为算尽了一切。  
Duke Xuan and Xuan Jiang thought they had calculated everything.  
却没想到。  
But they did not realize  
隔墙有耳。  
that walls can have ears.

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`条件状语从句`、`宾语从句`、`被动表达`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `If he does not die, then we must`。<br>
难点拆解：这里的 `If ... then ...` 是最直接的条件状语从句；`She said that Jizi had long hated ...` 是宾语从句；`ordered to kill whoever carried the white mao` 里还带被动和名词性结构，适合练“命令/安排”类表达。<br>
常用短语：
- `move first`：先下手
- `the moment ...`：一……就……
- `come up with`：想出
- `for that very reason`：也正因为如此

## 第十章 人鹤情未了  
## Chapter 10: His Love Affair with Cranes  

两个少年同船而去。  
Two young men drifted away in the same boat.  
船影悠悠，心里全是牵挂。  
The boat floated on, and the heart was full of longing.  
只盼对方平安无事。  
One could only hope the other would come to no harm.  
这几句诗。  
These lines  
出自《诗经·邶风·二子乘舟》。  
come from "Two Sons in a Boat" in the *Airs of Bei* section of the *Book of Songs*.  
它纪念的。  
What they commemorate  
正是急子和公子寿。  
is precisely Jizi and Prince Shou.  
这种兄弟争着替死的深情。  
This depth of brotherly devotion（手足深情）, with each trying to die in the other's place,  
也只有春秋时代才写得出来。  
feels like something only the Spring and Autumn age could produce.  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`定语从句`、`宾语从句`、`并列句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `This depth of brotherly devotion feels like something only the Spring and Autumn age could produce`。<br>
难点拆解：`with each trying to die in the other's place` 是补充说明的独立结构；`What they commemorate is ...` 里主语部分是主语从句；最后一句用 `feels like ... could ...` 表达“仿佛只有……才会……”。<br>
常用短语：
- `come to no harm`：平安无事
- `in the other's place`：替对方
- `feel like something only ... could produce`：像是只有……才会产生的东西

### 兄弟情深  
### Brotherly Devotion  

宣公和宣姜的阴谋。  
Duke Xuan and Xuan Jiang's plot（阴谋）  
偏偏被公子寿听见了。  
happened to be overheard by Prince Shou.  
同样是在后宫长大。  
People may grow up in the same inner palace,  
有人会变得又狠又贪。  
yet one turns ruthless（无情的） and greedy,  
有人却偏偏仁厚重情。  
while another becomes kind and loyal.  
卫宣公坏得透顶。  
Duke Xuan of Wei was rotten to the core.  
可他偏偏有两个好儿子。  
Yet he somehow had two excellent sons.  
这老天。  
Heaven itself,  
有时真说不清公不公平。  
at times, can be hard to judge as either fair or unfair.  
按常理说。  
By any ordinary calculation,  
公子寿最该盼着急子死。  
Prince Shou had the most to gain from Jizi's death.  
急子一死。  
If Jizi died,  
世子之位就是他的。  
the heirship（继承权） would be his.  
可他根本没往那儿想。  
But that thought never truly entered his mind.  
他只想着一件事。  
He thought of only one thing:  
哥哥不能死。  
his elder brother must not die.  
他得把人救出去。  
He had to save him.  
公子寿立刻去见急子。  
Prince Shou went to see Jizi at once.  
“哥哥，你快逃吧。”  
"Brother, you must run."  
他一口气说完全部阴谋。  
He poured out the entire conspiracy（密谋） in one breath.  
只要逃出卫国。  
As long as Jizi escaped Wei,  
凭他的贤名。  
his fine reputation  
到哪儿都会被奉为上宾。  
would make him an honored guest wherever he went.  
就算将来自己做了国君。  
Even if Prince Shou later became ruler himself,  
他也愿意把位子还给哥哥。  
he was willing to return the throne to his brother.  
可急子只说了一句。  
But Jizi answered with only one sentence.  
“违背父命而求活，不行。”  
"I cannot defy my father's order simply to save my own life."  
他说得很平静。  
He said it calmly.  
心里却比谁都明白。  
But inwardly he understood better than anyone  
真正要他死的。  
that the one who truly wanted him dead  
其实是宣姜。  
was Xuan Jiang.  
他知道她恨自己。  
He knew she hated him.  
可他心里。  
And yet, deep down,  
竟还爱着她。  
he still loved her.  
若是自己的死。  
If his death  
真能让她痛快一点。  
could bring her even a little satisfaction,  
那也算值得。  
then perhaps it would be worth it.  
公子寿反复苦劝。  
Prince Shou pleaded again and again.  
急子却一步也不肯退。  
Jizi would not yield a single step.  
眼看劝不动。  
Seeing he could not change his brother's mind,  
公子寿只能失望离开。  
Prince Shou had no choice but to leave in despair（绝望）.  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`条件状语从句`、`宾语从句`、`让步状语从句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `Prince Shou had the most to gain from Jizi's death, but that thought never truly entered his mind`。<br>
难点拆解：`If Jizi died, the heirship would be his` 是条件状语从句；`he understood ... that ... was Xuan Jiang` 用宾语从句补足“明白的内容”；`Even if ...` 则表示让步，突出公子寿并不贪恋君位。<br>
常用短语：
- `have the most to gain from`：最能从……中获利
- `pour out ... in one breath`：一口气说出……
- `yield a single step`：一步也不退

临行前一晚。  
On the night before Jizi's departure,  
公子寿来给哥哥送行。  
Prince Shou came to see his brother off.  
兄弟俩整夜喝酒。  
The two brothers drank through the whole night.  
他们都知道。  
Both of them knew  
这多半就是最后一面。  
that this was most likely their final meeting.  
急子是哥哥。  
Jizi was the elder brother,  
酒量却远不如寿。  
but he was no match for Shou in drink.  
一杯接一杯下去。  
Cup followed cup,  
急子终于醉倒了。  
and at last Jizi fell asleep in a stupor（昏睡）.  
天刚亮时。  
At first light,  
急子从梦里醒来。  
Jizi woke from sleep.  
“弟弟呢？”  
"Where is my brother?"  
“公子寿已经走了。”  
"Prince Shou has already left."  
急子本想立刻出发。  
Jizi meant to leave at once.  
可临出门时。  
But just as he was about to set out,  
他忽然发现少了白旄。  
he suddenly realized the white banner was missing.  
昨夜来过这里的人。  
Only one outsider had come there the night before.  
除了侍从。  
Aside from the attendants,  
只有公子寿。  
that person was Prince Shou.  
急子一下就明白了。  
In an instant, Jizi understood.  
“快去找他。”  
"Go find him, now."  
不久回报传来。  
The report came back soon enough.  
公子寿带着人。  
Prince Shou had taken attendants with him,  
拿着白旄乘船走了。  
and had sailed away carrying the white banner.  
急子大惊失色。  
Jizi went pale with alarm.  
他比谁都清楚。  
He knew better than anyone  
那面白旄意味着什么。  
what that banner meant.  
“立刻开船。”  
"Launch the boat at once."  
他一路催着船夫快划。  
All the way, he urged the rowers to move faster.  
一定要在莘野之前。  
He had to catch Prince Shou  
追上公子寿。  
before they reached Xinye.  
船已经很快了。  
The boat was already moving fast,  
急子还是嫌慢。  
but to Jizi it was still too slow.  
快到莘野时。  
As they neared Xinye,  
他终于看见前面有船。  
he finally spotted a boat ahead.  
他刚松一口气。  
He had just breathed easier  
随从就提醒他。  
when an attendant pointed something out.  
那不是追上了。  
They had not caught up.  
那船是迎面开来的。  
That boat was coming straight toward them.  
急子的心又提了起来。  
Jizi's heart lurched（猛地一紧） again.  
两船越靠越近。  
The two boats drew closer and closer.  
相距不过两丈。  
Only a few meters separated them.  
急子手握长剑。  
Sword in hand,  
纵身一跃。  
Jizi leapt across  
轻飘飘落到对面船上。  
and landed lightly on the other deck.  
自己的人全看呆了。  
His own men were stunned.  
可谁也跳不过去。  
But none of them could follow him.  
对面的船上。  
On the other boat,  
没有公子寿。  
Prince Shou was nowhere to be seen.  
只有十几个凶汉。  
There were only a dozen or so fierce-looking men,  
个个手里提刀。  
each holding a blade.  
急子扫了一眼。  
Jizi swept his eyes across the deck  
立刻看见角落里的白旄。  
and immediately saw the white banner lying in a corner.  
“你们把他杀了？”  
"You killed him?"  
那伙人点了点头。  
The men nodded.  
急子还不死心。  
Jizi still refused to believe it.  
“人呢？”  
"Where is he?"  
对方提来一颗人头。  
One of them lifted a severed head.  
正是公子寿。  
It was Prince Shou.  
急子当场痛哭。  
Jizi broke down in tears.  
“弟弟，你这是何苦。”  
"Brother, why did you do this?"  
“你死了，父亲也会难过。”  
"When you die, Father too will grieve."  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`时间状语从句`、`宾语从句`、`分词结构`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `He had to catch Prince Shou before they reached Xinye`。<br>
难点拆解：`before they reached Xinye` 是时间状语从句；`He knew ... what that banner meant` 里 `what ... meant` 是宾语从句；`Sword in hand` 是压缩后的分词式补充说明，让动作描写更紧凑。<br>
常用短语：
- `see someone off`：给某人送行
- `go pale with alarm`：惊得脸色发白
- `in an instant`：一瞬间
- `sword in hand`：手持长剑

船上的杀手都慌了。  
The killers on the boat were terrified（惊恐的）.  
他们刚杀了弟弟。  
They had just killed the younger brother.  
如今哥哥找上门来。  
Now the elder brother had come for them.  
看急子刚才那一下。  
Judging from that leap alone,  
谁都觉得他是绝顶高手。  
anyone would have taken him for a supreme master.  
若知道眼前这人。  
If they had known  
就是卫国第一剑客急子。  
that the man before them was Jizi, Wei's finest swordsman,  
他们根本不敢接这活。  
they would never have dared accept the job.  
急子抬起头。  
Jizi raised his head.  
所有人都以为。  
Every one of them thought  
他要动手报仇了。  
he was about to strike for revenge.  
可他没有出剑。  
But he did not draw his sword.  
他只是说。  
He only said,  
“你们杀错人了。”  
"You killed the wrong man."  
“该死的是我，不是他。”  
"The one meant to die was me, not him."  
他说得很诚恳。  
He said it with complete sincerity（真诚）.  
这下那伙人更怕了。  
That only frightened the men more.  
“来吧，杀了我。”  
"Come. Kill me."  
急子甚至上前一步。  
Jizi even stepped toward them.  
这是他们第一次见到。  
It was the first time in their lives  
有人主动求死。  
that someone had begged them for death.  
他们只觉得。  
All they could think was  
这一定是个圈套。  
that it had to be a trap.  
急子越求。  
The more Jizi begged,  
他们抖得越厉害。  
the harder they trembled.  
“求你们了，杀了我吧。”  
"Please, kill me."  
“不然你们拿不到赏金。”  
"Otherwise you will never collect the reward."  
他说得越真。  
The more earnest he sounded,  
那伙人越不敢下手。  
the less they dared move.  
急子急了。  
Jizi grew desperate.  
他大喝一声。  
With a shout,  
朝他们走过去。  
he strode toward them.  
为首那人彻底慌了。  
The leader panicked completely.  
他以为急子要出招。  
He thought Jizi was attacking.  
情急之下。  
In blind panic,  
他挥刀就砍。  
he slashed with his blade.  
刀光一闪。  
Steel flashed.  
血光也跟着迸开。  
Blood burst forth.  
急子倒下了。  
Jizi fell.  
这群人愣了好半天。  
The men stood frozen for a long while.  
他们这才明白。  
Only then did they realize  
世上真有人。  
that there really are people in this world  
会自己来送死。  
who walk willingly to their deaths.  
他们原是江上的亡命徒。  
They were river outlaws（亡命徒）, living by robbery and murder.  
拿钱替人办事。  
They had taken money to kill on commission（受雇行凶）.  
却完全不知道。  
What they had never known  
自己杀的是卫国两位公子。  
was that they had killed two princes of Wei.  
于是他们又带上急子的人头。  
So they took Jizi's head as well.  
多带一个不算多。  
One more head cost them little,  
万一急子说的是真话呢。  
and Jizi's words might, after all, have been true.  
作者随后开了个极黑的玩笑。  
The author then adds a very dark joke.  
一个人若主动求死。  
If someone begs too eagerly to be killed,  
反倒可能把恶徒吓住。  
he may frighten the criminal instead.  
急子和寿这对兄弟。  
As for Jizi and Shou,  
彼此相爱。  
they loved each other deeply.  
又争着替对方去死。  
And each tried to die in the other's place.  
卫国人太感动。  
The people of Wei were so moved  
才写下了《二子乘舟》。  
that they composed "Two Sons in a Boat."  
读到这里。  
By the time one reaches this point,  
也只能长叹一句。  
one can only let out a long sigh.  
人与人之间。  
The distance between one human heart and another  
境界竟会差到这种地步。  
can truly be this vast.  

### 乱伦  
### Incest  

两颗人头摆到宣公面前。  
The two severed heads were placed before Duke Xuan.  
他当场看傻了。  
He stared at them in shock.  
自己最出色的两个儿子。  
His two finest sons  
竟然被自己一手害死。  
had both been destroyed by his own doing.  
更讽刺的是。  
More bitterly still,  
他还得拿银子。  
he still had to pay silver  
赏给杀儿子的凶手。  
to reward the men who had killed his sons.  
宣姜也昏了过去。  
Xuan Jiang fainted as well.  
两个人先前一心要急子死。  
Both of them had been determined to kill Jizi.  
可真见到人头。  
Yet when they saw the severed head with their own eyes,  
还是后悔了。  
they regretted it.  
更让他们没想到的。  
What they had not expected most of all  
是公子寿也搭了进去。  
was that Prince Shou had died with him.  
和两个儿子相比。  
Set beside his two sons,  
宣公简直不值一提。  
Duke Xuan was scarcely worth mentioning.  
受了这一场惊吓。  
After that shock,  
不到半年他就死了。  
he died in less than half a year.  
害人害到最后。  
Those who ruin others  
往往也把自己赔进去。  
often ruin themselves as well.  
公子朔继位。  
Prince Shuo took the throne  
这就是卫惠公。  
and became Duke Hui of Wei.  
那年他才十五岁。  
He was only fifteen years old.  
可他在位才四年。  
But after only four years on the throne,  
左公子泄、右公子职。  
Left Prince Xie, Right Prince Zhi,  
再加上大夫宁跪。  
together with the grandee Ning Gui,  
就替急子鸣不平。  
rose in outrage on Jizi's behalf.  
他们联手赶走惠公。  
They joined forces and drove Duke Hui away.  
惠公逃去齐国。  
Duke Hui fled to Qi,  
投奔舅舅齐襄公。  
where he sought refuge（投奔） with his uncle Duke Xiang of Qi.  
急子的同母弟弟黔牟。  
Jizi's full younger brother Qian Mou  
则被立为国君。  
was installed as ruler instead.  
这时麻烦来了。  
At that point, a difficult question arose.  
宣姜该怎么办？  
What was to be done with Xuan Jiang?  
按常规。  
Under normal practice,  
她该被废为庶人。  
she should have been stripped of rank  
再赶回齐国。  
and sent back to Qi.  
可她偏偏是齐襄公的妹妹。  
But she happened to be Duke Xiang's sister.  
齐国又惹不起。  
And Qi was not a state Wei could afford to offend.  
所以卫国人商量来商量去。  
So the men of Wei debated the matter back and forth.  
最后想出个折中办法。  
In the end they came up with a compromise（折中办法）.  
请她搬出后宫。  
She would move out of the inner palace,  
另住豪宅。  
live in a separate mansion,  
照样有人护卫。  
keep her guards,  
太后待遇也不变。  
and retain all the privileges（待遇） of a dowager.  
卫国人觉得。  
The people of Wei thought  
这样已经很给面子了。  
that this was already generous enough.  
可齐襄公并不满意。  
Duke Xiang of Qi was not satisfied.  
他本来就想。  
He had long wanted  
替外甥把君位抢回来。  
to win the throne back for his nephew.  
只是当时还有顾虑。  
He simply had one concern.  
那时候他正想和周王室结亲。  
At the time, he was trying to forge a marriage tie with the Zhou royal house,  
而黔牟偏偏是周王的女婿。  
and Qian Mou happened to be the Zhou king's son-in-law.  
暂时不好翻脸。  
So it was awkward to break with him openly.  
可不翻脸。  
Yet not breaking with him  
不代表没有鬼主意。  
did not mean he had no schemes.  
黔牟有个同母弟弟。  
Qian Mou had a full younger brother,  
名叫昭伯。  
called Zhaobo,  
也叫公子舒。  
also known as Prince Shu.  
齐襄公灵机一动。  
Duke Xiang suddenly hit on a wild idea.  
既想保全妹妹。  
He wanted to protect his sister,  
又不想让她守寡。  
and he did not want her to remain a widow.  
那就把宣姜。  
So why not marry Xuan Jiang  
嫁给公子舒。  
to Prince Shu?  
宣姜自己倒愿意。  
Xuan Jiang herself was willing enough.  
可公子舒死活不肯。  
Prince Shu, however, refused outright.  
她是他的后妈。  
She was his stepmother.  
还给他父亲生过两个儿子。  
She had even borne two sons to his father.  
这不是乱伦是什么。  
If that was not incest（乱伦）, what was?  
可国家利益这面大旗。  
But once the banner of "state interest" was raised,  
什么荒唐事都能包装。  
almost any absurdity（荒唐事） could be dressed up as reasonable.  
在齐襄公和黔牟的双重压力下。  
Under pressure from both Duke Xiang and Qian Mou,  
公子舒还是把后妈娶回了家。  
Prince Shu finally married his stepmother.  
更荒唐的是。  
More absurd still,  
两个人后来还真挺恩爱。  
the two of them reportedly grew quite devoted to each other.  
他们一共生了五个孩子。  
Together they had five children.  
长子早夭。  
The eldest died young.  
老二公子申。  
The second son, Prince Shen,  
和老三公子毁。  
and the third, Prince Hui,  
后来都做了卫国国君。  
would later rule Wei.  
两个女儿里。  
As for the two daughters,  
一个嫁给宋桓公。  
one married Duke Huan of Song,  
一个嫁给许穆公。  
and the other married Duke Mu of Xu.  
后者就是许穆夫人。  
The latter became Lady Mu of Xu,  
也是《诗经》里有名的女诗人。  
a celebrated poetess（女诗人） in the *Book of Songs*.  
一个女人先后嫁给父子三人。  
One woman had successively married a father and his two sons.  
还生下三个诸侯。  
She also bore three rulers  
和两个诸侯夫人。  
and two noble wives.  
宣姜这一生。  
Xuan Jiang's life  
真是把荒唐二字写满了。  
was a complete monument to absurdity.  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`宾语从句`、`让步状语从句`、`被动表达`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `The people of Wei thought that this was already generous enough, but Duke Xiang of Qi was not satisfied`。<br>
难点拆解：`thought that ...` 是典型宾语从句；`Yet not breaking with him did not mean ...` 用否定表达转折；`was installed as ruler`、`was stripped of rank` 则是常见被动表达，适合历史叙述。<br>
常用短语：
- `seek refuge with`：投奔
- `come up with a compromise`：想出折中办法
- `forge a marriage tie with`：与……结亲
- `raise the banner of`：打出……的旗号

### 卫懿公养鹤  
### Duke Yi and His Cranes  

卫国的荒诞剧。  
Wei's theater of absurdity（荒诞）  
还远没有演完。  
was far from over.  
惠公被赶走八年后。  
Eight years after Duke Hui had been driven out,  
齐襄公终于出兵。  
Duke Xiang of Qi finally sent troops.  
卫国根本挡不住。  
Wei was no match at all.  
左右公子被杀。  
The Left and Right Princes were killed.  
黔牟因为是周王女婿。  
Because Qian Mou was the Zhou king's son-in-law,  
被送去周都。  
he was sent off to the Zhou capital  
继续投靠岳家。  
to rely on his royal in-laws.  
宁跪则逃去了秦国。  
Ning Gui fled to Qin.  
卫惠公就这样复位了。  
And so Duke Hui was restored to the throne.  
可他消停不了多久。  
He did not stay quiet for long.  
十二年后。  
Twelve years later,  
他因为记恨周朝收留黔牟。  
resenting Zhou for sheltering Qian Mou,  
竟然出兵攻打周王室。  
he actually marched against the Zhou court.  
周惠王被他赶跑。  
King Hui of Zhou was driven out by him,  
王子颓被他扶上王位。  
and Prince Tui was set up in his place.  
再过六年。  
Six years after that,  
卫惠公死了。  
Duke Hui died.  
他的儿子继位。  
His son succeeded him.  
这就是卫懿公。  
This was Duke Yi of Wei.  
时间是前六六九年。  
The year was 669 BCE.  
卫懿公完美继承了。  
Duke Yi perfectly inherited  
卫国祖传的荒唐。  
Wei's hereditary talent for absurd behavior.  
不过他和祖父不同。  
He did differ from his grandfather in one respect.  
祖父好色。  
His grandfather had been obsessed with women.  
他却不好女色。  
Duke Yi was not drawn to women at all.  
他只迷一样东西。  
He was obsessed（痴迷） with only one thing:  
鹤。  
cranes.  
他爱鹤爱到发狂。  
He loved cranes to the point of mania（狂热）.  
只要看上哪一只。  
If he took a fancy to one,  
花再多钱也要买回来。  
he would pay whatever it cost to buy it.  
消息一传开。  
Once word spread,  
各地的人都来献鹤。  
people from every direction came bearing cranes as tribute（进献）.  
后宫里。  
Inside the palace,  
宫女不算多。  
there were not that many maids,  
鹤却比宫女还多。  
but there were more cranes than maids.  
而宫女们的正经差事。  
And the maids' real duty  
也不再是伺候人。  
was no longer serving people.  
而是伺候鹤。  
It was serving cranes.  
若把他放到今天。  
If he were dropped into the modern world,  
去做动物保护协会会长。  
he might have made an excellent president of an animal-protection society.  
卫懿公一口气养了几百只鹤。  
Duke Yi kept hundreds of cranes.  
不出门时。  
When he stayed home,  
他就和鹤玩在一起。  
he spent his time playing among them.  
一旦出门。  
The moment he went out,  
也一定把鹤们带上。  
he had to bring the cranes along.  
它们排成两列。  
They marched in two orderly lines,  
倒也整整齐齐。  
perfectly neat and disciplined.  
于是卫懿公每次出行。  
So every outing by Duke Yi  
都成了天下奇观。  
became a spectacle（奇观） under heaven.  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`时间状语从句`、`原因状语从句`、`条件状语从句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `Duke Yi was obsessed with only one thing: cranes`。<br>
难点拆解：`Eight years after ...` 和 `Twelve years later` 都是时间框架；`resenting Zhou for sheltering Qian Mou` 压缩表达了原因；`If he took a fancy to one, he would pay ...` 则是很典型的条件句。<br>
常用短语：
- `be no match for`：不是……的对手
- `set up ... in his place`：扶持……取而代之
- `take a fancy to`：看上，喜欢上
- `word spread`：消息传开

可事情还没完。  
But it did not stop there.  
若只是养鹤。  
If he had merely kept cranes,  
那还不算最离谱。  
that still would not have been the most outrageous part.  
卫懿公偏偏想象力过盛。  
Duke Yi happened to possess an overactive imagination.  
他给鹤们封官加爵。  
He granted the cranes ranks and titles.  
什么鹤大夫。  
There were crane ministers,  
鹤将军。  
crane generals,  
鹤夫人。  
and crane ladies.  
《史记》还专门写过。  
The *Records of the Grand Historian* even records  
他说鹤有乘轩者。  
that some cranes rode in carriages.  
而且这些封号。  
And these titles  
并不是空头名义。  
were not empty honors.  
全都带着实打实的待遇。  
They came with real privileges（特权）.  
鹤官员出门。  
When crane officials went out,  
有车坐。  
they had carriages.  
有护卫。  
They had guards.  
有时甚至还有交通管制。  
At times there was even traffic control for them.  
鹤夫人另有宫室。  
The crane ladies had chambers of their own.  
卫懿公还会去“临幸”它们。  
Duke Yi even "visited" them as if they were human consorts,  
做出比禽兽还难看的事。  
doing things more disgraceful（可耻的） than beasts.  
照这个势头。  
At that rate,  
他简直巴不得。  
he nearly seemed to wish  
自己能生个鹤宝宝。  
for a crane child of his own.  
每次鹤下蛋。  
Whenever a crane laid eggs,  
他都盼着。  
he would secretly hope  
能孵出个半人半鹤的怪胎。  
that a half-human, half-crane creature might hatch.  
若有鹤因受宠而死。  
If a crane died from too much royal favor,  
他还会按夫人的规格厚葬。  
he would even bury it with the honors due a consort.  
他把心思全用在鹤身上。  
He poured all his energy into cranes.  
百姓饿肚子。  
The people went hungry,  
他却懒得过问。  
and he could not be bothered to care.  
百姓吃不上饭。  
While ordinary people lacked food,  
鹤却顿顿山珍海味。  
the cranes feasted on delicacies（珍馐）.  
将军立功得不到升迁。  
Human generals could win merit and still go unpromoted,  
鹤将军却动不动升半级。  
while crane generals were advanced at the slightest whim.  
上朝的时候。  
When court was in session,  
鹤站得比人还靠前。  
the cranes stood even closer to the throne than men did.  
更气人的是。  
More infuriating still,  
它们有时还会。  
they would sometimes  
骑在人头上拉屎。  
perch over someone's head and relieve themselves.  
全国怨声载道。  
Complaints filled the whole state.  
除了鹤。  
Other than the cranes,  
只要是人。  
every human being  
就没有不恨卫懿公的。  
hated Duke Yi.  
其实鹤也未必全满意。  
In truth, not even all the cranes were necessarily pleased.  
至少那些公鹤。  
At least the male cranes  
多半也醋意大发。  
were probably burning with jealousy（嫉妒）.  
于是卫国人给他起了个外号。  
So the people of Wei gave him a nickname.  
“卫鹤公。”  
"Duke Crane of Wei."  
看到这里。  
At this point,  
也只能替卫康叔叹口气。  
one can only sigh for Kang Shu of Wei.  
那么好的祖宗。  
Such a worthy founder,  
怎么就养出这么一群。  
and yet what followed him  
衣冠禽兽的后代。  
was a line of descendants dressed like men but no better than beasts.  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`条件状语从句`、`定语从句`、`并列句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `These titles were not empty honors`。<br>
难点拆解：`If he had merely kept cranes` 是与事实相反的条件表达；`that some cranes rode in carriages` 是宾语从句；后面一串短句并列推进，把“待遇越来越离谱”的节奏写得很顺。<br>
常用短语：
- `grant ... ranks and titles`：封官加爵
- `empty honors`：空头名义
- `at the slightest whim`：动不动，稍一兴起
- `burn with jealousy`：醋意大发

### 凉拌鹤肉  
### Chilled Crane Meat  

报应总会来的。  
Retribution（报应） always comes.  
只是有早有晚。  
Only the timing differs.  
卫懿公九年。  
In the ninth year of Duke Yi's reign,  
也就是前六六一年。  
that is, in 661 BCE,  
北方赤狄南下。  
the Red Di tribes marched south from the north.  
他们穿过邢国。  
They passed through Xing  
直扑卫国。  
and headed straight for Wei.  
消息一到。  
The moment the news arrived,  
卫国上下都慌了。  
the whole state of Wei was thrown into panic.  
养鹤养得忘乎所以的卫懿公。  
Duke Yi, who had lost himself in raising cranes,  
这才忽然想起。  
only then suddenly remembered  
世上还有许多事。  
that there were many things in this world  
比养鹤更要紧。  
more urgent than pampering cranes.  
他赶紧征兵。  
He hurried to conscript troops.  
也赶紧练兵。  
He also tried to drill them in haste.  
可惜一切都太晚了。  
Unfortunately, everything was already too late.  
老百姓跑的跑。  
The common people fled where they could.  
散的散。  
Those who stayed scattered.  
根本没人肯来当兵。  
Almost no one was willing to serve.  
你平日不把百姓当人。  
If you spend ordinary days treating the people as less than human,  
战时又凭什么。  
then on what grounds  
要他们为你卖命。  
can you ask them to die for you in war?  
将军们也纷纷托病。  
The generals all began pleading illness as well.  
既然打仗没功。  
If victory brought no reward,  
谁还愿意出头。  
who would want to risk his life?  
卫懿公想不通。  
Duke Yi could not make sense of it.  
平时这些人。  
These very people,  
怎么到了国难当头的时候。  
when disaster stood at the gate,  
忽然都不爱国了。  
had suddenly stopped being patriotic.  
“为什么？”  
"Why?"  
他怒问群臣。  
he demanded angrily of his ministers.  
群臣也忍不住了。  
By then the ministers could no longer hold back.  
他们回了他一句。  
They gave him a single answer.  
“君好鹤，鹤可令击翟。”  
"Since Your Grace loves cranes, let the cranes fight the Di."  
这话记在《史记》里。  
That line is preserved in the *Records of the Grand Historian*.  
意思再明白不过。  
Its meaning could not have been clearer.  
鹤将军平日。  
Those crane generals  
只要扭扭身子。  
needed only shake their feathers,  
抖抖羽毛。  
sway their bodies,  
地位就比真人还高。  
and they ranked above real men.  
既然如此。  
If that was how things worked,  
那就让它们上阵好了。  
then they could go to the battlefield.  
卫懿公这下老实了。  
That finally humbled Duke Yi.  
“寡人知错了。”  
"I know I was wrong."  
“那该怎么办？”  
"What should be done now?"  
石祁子接话最快。  
Shi Qizi answered at once.  
他是石碏的孙子。  
He was the grandson of Shi Que.  
“怎么办？凉拌。”  
"What should be done? Serve it cold."  
这话一出口。  
The sarcasm（讽刺） in that reply  
讽刺味十足。  
was sharp enough to cut.  
可卫懿公急昏了头。  
But Duke Yi was too panicked to hear the mockery.  
他还追问。  
He pressed on.  
“怎么个凉拌法？”  
"How exactly do we serve it cold?"  
石祁子这才挑明。  
Shi Qizi then laid it out plainly.  
百姓第一恨的。  
What the people hated most  
当然是你。  
was, of course, you.  
第二恨的。  
What they hated second most  
就是你的鹤。  
was your cranes.  
若想先保住你。  
If you want to save your own life first,  
就得把鹤全杀了。  
you must kill the cranes.  
剁碎、煮熟。  
Chop them up, boil them,  
再做成凉菜。  
then turn them into a cold dish  
分给大家吃。  
and hand them out to the people.  
好让大家出口恶气。  
That way they can vent their anger.  
卫懿公一听。  
The moment Duke Yi heard this,  
立刻拍板。  
he decided at once.  
宠物再重要。  
No pet, however cherished,  
也没自己的命重要。  
was more important than his own life.  
于是鹤将军也好。  
So crane generals,  
鹤大夫也好。  
crane ministers,  
鹤夫人也好。  
and crane ladies alike,  
凡是鹤。  
every crane there was,  
统统遭了殃。  
met disaster.  
这大概是鹤史上。  
It was, quite possibly,  
最惨的一场大屠杀。  
the bloodiest massacre（屠杀） in crane history.  
凉拌鹤肉端上街头。  
Chilled crane meat was served in the streets.  
卫国百姓争着去吃。  
The people of Wei rushed to eat it.  
那时候当然不用担心禽流感。  
In those days, of course, no one worried about bird flu.  
说到底。  
In the final analysis,  
鹤又有什么罪。  
what crime had the cranes really committed?  
可不管怎么说。  
Still, whatever the justice of it,  
老百姓总算出了一口气。  
the common people had at last vented their rage.  
也终于有人肯出来效力。  
And at last some men were willing to serve.  
这些鹤。  
The cranes, in the end,  
不过成了替罪羊。  
had simply become scapegoats（替罪羊）.  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`条件状语从句`、`宾语从句`、`时间状语从句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `If you want to save your own life first, you must kill the cranes`。<br>
难点拆解：这里的 `If ...` 是非常清楚的条件状语从句；`what the people hated most was ...` 是主语从句加表语结构；`The moment Duke Yi heard this` 则是时间状语从句，写出他立刻变脸的速度。<br>
常用短语：
- `throw ... into panic`：使……陷入恐慌
- `hold back`：忍住，克制
- `lay it out plainly`：把话挑明
- `vent one's anger`：出一口气

### 卫国沦陷  
### The Fall of Wei  

真到了开战时。  
When war finally arrived,  
卫国能拼凑出的兵力。  
all Wei could scrape together（东拼西凑）  
还不到两百乘战车。  
was fewer than two hundred chariots.  
原因并不复杂。  
The reason was not complicated.  
卫懿公即位后的九年里。  
In the nine years after Duke Yi took the throne,  
卫国几乎没正经练过兵。  
Wei had scarcely trained its army properly.  
也没添过什么新战车。  
Nor had it added many new chariots.  
旧战车倒坏了不少。  
The old chariots, meanwhile, had fallen into ruin.  
军费去了哪里？  
Where had the military funds gone?  
答案其实很简单。  
The answer was painfully simple.  
都砸在鹤身上了。  
They had all been poured into the cranes.  
买鹤要钱。  
Buying cranes cost money.  
养鹤要钱。  
Keeping cranes cost money.  
护理羽毛要钱。  
Maintaining feathers cost money.  
给鹤夫人准备孵蛋的地方。  
Preparing nesting rooms for the crane ladies cost money.  
修宫室、配专车。  
Building chambers and special carriages cost money.  
样样都要钱。  
Every part of it consumed money.  
将军们平时也不练兵。  
The generals did not train soldiers either.  
有门路的养鹤。  
Those with connections raised cranes.  
没门路的养鸡。  
Those without them raised chickens.  
反正谁也没闲着。  
In their own fashion, nobody stayed idle.  
这一百多乘战车。  
These hundred-odd chariots,  
再加上几千志愿兵。  
along with a few thousand volunteers,  
根本不够打。  
were nowhere near enough for a real war.  
直到这时候。  
Only at this point  
卫懿公才想起。  
did Duke Yi remember  
要向别国求援。  
to ask other states for help.  
可还是那句话。  
But the same answer remained:  
晚了。  
it was too late.  
这九年里。  
Over those nine years,  
卫国和诸侯几乎断了来往。  
Wei had almost ceased dealings with the other feudal states.  
老一代外交官早退休了。  
The old diplomats had long since retired.  
没退休的。  
Those who remained  
也没什么真正的经验。  
had little real experience.  
使者们出去一圈。  
The envoys made their rounds,  
各国反应却差不多。  
but the states answered much the same way.  
早干什么去了？  
Where had you been all this time?  
你们不是有鹤将军吗？  
Did you not have crane generals?  
这下连凉拌鹤肉。  
At that point, even chilled crane meat  
也救不了急。  
could no longer save the situation.  
只有齐国和宋国。  
Only Qi and Song  
还算给了点面子。  
gave something like a polite answer.  
一个说再看看。  
One said it would see what could be done.  
一个因为旧交情。  
The other, out of old friendship,  
也愿意考虑。  
was willing to consider it.  
可远水终究不解近渴。  
But distant water cannot quench immediate thirst.  
说到底。  
In the end,  
还得靠自己。  
Wei still had to rely on itself.  
于是卫懿公亲自北上。  
So Duke Yi personally led the army north.  
也许在他看来。  
Perhaps, in his eyes,  
鹤夫人都死光了。  
once the crane ladies were gone,  
自己活着也没什么意思。  
life itself had lost much of its meaning.  
所以这一仗。  
For that very reason,  
他反倒打得很勇。  
he actually fought with remarkable ferocity（勇猛）.  
可赤狄人太强。  
But the Red Di were simply too strong.  
人多。  
They were numerous,  
也凶悍。  
and they were fierce.  
乱刀之下。  
Under a storm of blades,  
卫懿公也成了凉拌菜。  
Duke Yi himself was chopped into a cold dish.  
只怕魂儿都去追鹤夫人了。  
One imagines his soul ran after the crane ladies.  
卫军被打垮以后。  
After the army of Wei was crushed,  
赤狄长驱直入。  
the Red Di advanced without resistance（毫无阻拦地）.  
他们攻入都城。  
They entered the capital,  
屠城放火。  
massacred the inhabitants and set the city ablaze.  
卫国公族仓皇南逃。  
The ruling clan of Wei fled south in panic.  
一路逃到黄河边。  
They ran all the way to the Yellow River.  
多亏宋国派船接应。  
Only because Song sent boats to receive them  
他们才渡过南岸。  
did they make it across to the southern bank.  
到了漕邑一清点。  
When they counted heads at Cao,  
只剩七百二十人。  
only seven hundred and twenty people were left.  
后来又从共邑、滕邑。  
Later, migrants from Gong and Teng  
陆续补了些人来。  
were brought in to add to the population.  
总算凑到四千多人。  
Only then did they scrape together more than four thousand people.  
这才勉强立起新君。  
Only then could they barely establish a new ruler.  
公子硕的儿子申。  
Shen, the son of Prince Shuo,  
被立为卫戴公。  
was enthroned as Duke Dai of Wei.  
可戴公没撑几天。  
But Duke Dai lasted only a few days.  
很快也死了。  
He soon died as well.  
于是又立他的弟弟毁。  
So his younger brother Hui was then enthroned,  
这就是卫文公。  
who became Duke Wen of Wei.  
自此之后。  
From that point on,  
卫国一路衰落。  
Wei declined steadily  
再也没真正缓过来。  
and never truly recovered its strength.  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`时间状语从句`、`倒装`、`原因状语从句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `Only at this point did Duke Yi remember to ask other states for help`。<br>
难点拆解：`Only at this point did ...` 是典型倒装，用来强调“直到这时候才”；`once the crane ladies were gone` 是时间兼条件色彩的从句；`Only because Song sent boats ... did they make it across` 也是强调原因时常见的倒装。<br>
常用短语：
- `scrape together`：东拼西凑
- `rely on itself`：靠自己
- `advance without resistance`：长驱直入
- `set ... ablaze`：放火焚烧

讲到这里。  
At this point,  
就该认真回头想想。  
it is worth seriously rethinking  
石碏当年的大义灭亲。  
Shi Que's old act of placing principle（大义） above family.  
从大道理上说。  
On the level of grand principle,  
石碏杀州吁。  
Shi Que's killing of Zhouxu,  
这个篡位者。  
that usurper（篡位者）,  
当然说得过去。  
can certainly be justified.  
可若把州吁。  
But if we place Zhouxu  
和后来的卫国国君们。  
beside the later rulers of Wei  
一一对照。  
and compare them one by one,  
问题就出来了。  
a troubling question appears.  
州吁之后。  
After Zhouxu,  
卫国主要经历了三位国君。  
Wei was chiefly ruled by three men.  
卫宣公。  
Duke Xuan,  
卫惠公。  
Duke Hui,  
还有卫懿公。  
and Duke Yi.  
卫宣公除了淫乱。  
Duke Xuan knew little beyond lust and incest.  
几乎一无是处。  
He was almost useless in every other regard.  
州吁却至少。  
Zhouxu, by contrast,  
还是个能打仗的有为青年。  
was at least a capable young man of action.  
再说卫惠公。  
Then consider Duke Hui.  
他居然敢率兵攻周。  
He actually dared to lead troops against the Zhou king.  
把周惠王赶走。  
He drove King Hui of Zhou away  
另立新王。  
and installed（拥立） another ruler in his place.  
这比单纯篡位。  
In terms of outrage,  
还更大逆不道。  
that was an even greater act of rebellion than mere usurpation.  
而他这么做。  
And why did he do it?  
不过是因为周王。  
Simply because the Zhou king  
收留了他的哥哥黔牟。  
had sheltered his elder brother Qian Mou.  
单论气量。  
In sheer breadth of spirit,  
卫惠公就差得远了。  
Duke Hui fell far short.  
当年卫宣公逃去小国邢国。  
When Duke Xuan fled to the small state of Xing,  
州吁可没发兵。  
Zhouxu did not send troops  
把人抓回来。  
to drag him back.  
至于卫懿公。  
As for Duke Yi,  
整天迷鹤误国。  
he ruined his state through his obsession with cranes,  
更没法和州吁相比。  
and cannot even be compared with Zhouxu.  
更有说服力的。  
Still more persuasive  
还有另一件事。  
is another fact.  
州吁的祖父卫武公。  
Zhouxu's grandfather, Duke Wu of Wei,  
是康叔以来。  
was Wei's finest ruler  
卫国最出色的国君。  
since Kang Shu.  
在他手里。  
Under his rule,  
卫国变强了。  
Wei grew stronger,  
爵位也从侯升到公。  
and its rank rose from marquis to duke.  
可卫武公怎么上位的？  
But how had Duke Wu himself taken the throne?  
也是杀兄夺位。  
He too had killed his elder brother and seized power.  
既然如此。  
If that is so,  
州吁若没被石碏除掉。  
then if Zhouxu had not been removed by Shi Que,  
未必不能。  
he might well have become  
做第二个卫武公。  
a second Duke Wu of Wei.  
所以别一听到篡位。  
So the moment you hear of usurpation（篡位）,  
就立刻认定。  
do not immediately decide  
那一定是坏事。  
that it must be evil.  
后面的故事里。  
In the stories that follow,  
还会有更多例子。  
there will be many more examples.  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`条件状语从句`、`宾语从句`、`倒装`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `If Zhouxu had not been removed by Shi Que, he might well have become a second Duke Wu of Wei`。<br>
难点拆解：这里用的是与过去事实相反的条件状语从句；`do not immediately decide that ...` 里有宾语从句；`Only at this point` 那类倒装在前一段已经出现，这一章末尾则用反事实条件句把作者的议论力度拉满。<br>
常用短语：
- `place principle above family`：大义灭亲
- `drive ... away`：赶走
- `fall far short`：远远不如
- `at the mere word of`：一听到……
